The first hill wiped off my landing gear and one wheel went up through the front spar on the lower left wing, breaking it off about two feet from the fuselage.

===Chinese===
行前我们答应带站上的一位女士飞一小圈。出发那天早晨，我先起飞作试验飞行，然后再带她游彭萨科拉。飞机刚离场、在海湾上空升到约两百英尺，发动机转速骤降到五百上下。我压坡度盘旋想回到机场，却差约十五英尺的高度够不着，被迫降落在距飞行场边缘不足一百英尺的沙丘上。第一座沙丘铲掉了我的起落架，一只轮子向上戳穿了左下翼的前梁，把前梁在离机身约两英尺处撞断。

===Original===
A quick survey of the plane showed that we would require a new landing gear and propeller in addition to the material required to splice the spar.

===Chinese===
粗粗一查，除了接梁所需的材料，我们还需要一套新起落架和一支新螺旋桨。

===Original===
The Navy hauled the plane into one of its large dirigible hangars and allowed us to make use of its equipment in repairing the damage. We purchased a spare landing gear and a propeller, then built a box frame around the broken spar and after gluing all the joints, screwed it in position and wound the splice with strong cord, which was then shrunk tight by several coats of dope. In this way the splice was made stronger than the original spar had ever been.

===Chinese===
海军把飞机拖进一座大型飞艇库，允许我们动用库里的设备修理损伤。我们买来一套备用起落架和一支螺旋桨，随后在断梁外围造了一个箱形框架，各接合处涂胶后用螺钉固定，接头部再缠上结实的细绳，最后刷上几道涂布油使绳收紧。这样修出来的接头，比原来的翼梁任何时候都结实。

===Original===
When we were not working on the ship we made several trips to the old Spanish forts which protected the city during the days when Florida still belonged to Spain. These are in an excellent state of preservation and contain a number of passageways, one of which is supposed to lead underground between the two fortifications, but although we searched carefully for the opening to this tunnel we never found it.

===Chinese===
不修飞机的时候，我们几度探访那些古老的西班牙要塞——在佛罗里达还属西班牙的年代，正是它们守卫着这座城市。要塞保存得极为完好，内有许多暗道，据说其中一条在地下连通两座堡垒，尽管我们仔细搜寻过这条地道的入口，却始终没有找到。

===Original===
In all we spent about a week repairing the plane and when it was ready to fly once more I tested it with an Irving parachute borrowed from one of the officers of the station. That was the first service type of chute I had ever worn and I experienced the unique feeling of not caring particularly whether the ship held together during the tests or not. I put that Canuck through maneuvers which I would never have dreamed of doing with it before, yet with the confidence of absolute safety.

===Chinese===
修这架飞机前后花了约一个星期。等它重新可以飞行时，我借了站上一名军官的一具欧文座式降落伞（Irving）作试验。那是我头一回穿上军用型降落伞，体验到一种奇特的心情：试飞中飞机散不散架，我并不特别在意。我让那架"加拿大佬"做遍了从前做梦也不敢对它做的机动，心里却怀着绝对的安全感。

===Original===
The advent of the service parachute was a tremendous step forward in the advance of aviation. It gave the test pilot a safe means of escape in most cases when all else had failed. It permitted formations to fly closer in comparative safety and in short allowed flyers to learn more about their planes than ever before. All this contributed to the ever-increasing knowledge of practical flying which makes possible the safety of present air commerce.

===Chinese===
军用降落伞的问世，是航空事业向前迈出的巨大一步。它在多数万般无奈的时刻，给了试飞员一条安全的逃生之路。它让编队得以在相对安全中飞得更紧凑；简言之，它使飞行员得以比以往任何时候都更透彻地了解自己的飞机。这一切都汇入了不断增长的实用飞行知识，正是这些知识，撑起了今日空中商务的安全。

===Original===
The airplane parachute has developed with the rapidity of the planes themselves. For years descents with chutes were made from balloons, but the first jump from a plane was by Capt. Berry at St. Louis, Missouri, in 1912. His parachute was a comparatively crude affair and of no use in an emergency. Ten years later, service type parachutes had been perfected which were strong enough to stand any strain the weight of a man's body falling through the air could place on them, no matter how many thousand feet he fell before releasing the parachute from its pack; and today, fifteen years after Capt. Berry made the first jump from an airplane, every army and air mail pilot is required to wear a parachute.

===Chinese===
飞机降落伞与飞机本身同步飞速发展。多年来，人们只从气球上伞降；而从飞机上跳伞的第一人是贝里上尉（Capt. Berry），1912 年在密苏里州圣路易斯。他的降落伞还是件相当简陋的装置，紧急情况下派不上用场。十年之后，军用型降落伞已臻完善，无论人体在空中坠落多少千英尺后才把伞从伞包中释放，它都结实得足以承受任何冲击；而今天，距贝里上尉首次从飞机上跳伞十五年之后，每一名陆军飞行员和航邮飞行员都必须佩戴降落伞。

===Original===
The test flight over, we lashed a five gallon can of gasoline on each wing and followed along the Gulf of Mexico to Pascagoula, Mississippi. There was a small crack halfway down the back of the Canuck's gasoline tank and when the gas no longer oozed out through the crack we knew that the tank was half empty. By carrying the two gas cans we obtained an extra hour's cruising range, and when the main tank became low I would pour their contents into it through a short length of steam hose. In this way we expected to make longer flights between landing fields and partially make up for the time lost at Pensacola.

===Chinese===
试验飞行结束后，我们在每侧机翼上各绑一只五加仑汽油桶，沿墨西哥湾飞往密西西比州帕斯卡古拉（Pascagoula）。"加拿大佬"油箱背面半腰有一道小裂缝，等汽油不再从裂缝里渗出来，我们就知道油箱用空一半了。带上这两只油桶，我们多出一小时的巡航里程；主油箱一少，我就用一截短蒸汽软管把桶里的油灌进去。这样我们指望在降落场之间飞更远的距离，多少补回在彭萨科拉损失的时间。

===Original===
From Pascagoula we went to New Orleans, landing in the race track north of the city. Then to Lake Charles and from there to Rice Field at Houston, Texas. At Rice Field we installed three fuel tanks under the top wing and center section, which gave us twenty-seven gallons extra capacity, or, in addition to the five gallon cans lashed on the wings, a cruising range of about four hundred miles.

===Chinese===
离开帕斯卡古拉，我们到了新奥尔良（New Orleans），降落在城北的赛马场里。然后到莱克查尔斯（Lake Charles），再从那里到得克萨斯州休斯敦的赖斯机场（Rice Field）。在赖斯机场，我们在上翼和中央翼段下方加装了三个油箱，多出二十七加仑容量——再加上绑在翼上的五加仑油桶，巡航里程约有四百英里。

===Original===
The field was covered with water and as our next stop was to be Brooks Field, which is just a few miles south of San Antonio, we only filled the wing and main tanks, leaving the five gallon cans empty.

===Chinese===
机场积满了水，而我们的下一站是布鲁克斯机场——就在圣安东尼奥以南几英里——所以我们只加满了翼上油箱和主油箱，五加仑的桶都空着。

===Original===
At Brooks I obtained definite instructions to report not later than March fifteenth.

===Chinese===
在布鲁克斯，我拿到明确的指令：报到不得迟于三月十五日。

===Original===
It was then the end of February but we decided to push on as far west as the intervening time would allow. Then I would return by rail and Klink would continue alone.

===Chinese===
当时已是二月底，但我们决定趁这段时间能往西推进多远就推进多远。然后我乘火车返回，克林克独自继续飞。

===Original===
We filled all of our tanks and after running along the ground for half a mile, stalled into the air; but after three circles of Brooks Field were completed and the plane was less than fifty feet high we landed and left one of the cans. Klink held the other in his lap in the rear cockpit.

===Chinese===
我们加满了全部油箱，地面滑跑半英里后，失速挣扎着离了地；可是绕布鲁克斯机场转完三圈，飞机还不到五十英尺高，我们只得降落，卸下一只油桶。另一只由克林克抱在膝上，坐在后座舱里。

===Original===
We had no more trouble in attaining several hundred feet of altitude with the lessened load and greatly lessened resistance, which counted for much more than the weight of the gasoline, but an hour later, when the elevation of the ground below us increased as the mountains were approached, we were again just skimming the mesquite and cactus. At last it was necessary for Klink to heave his gas can over the side and for me to turn the ship down a ravine to keep from striking the ground. It was disappointing enough to leave the first can at Brooks Field but I do not believe Klink will ever forget the sight of the second as it burst on the ground below us.

===Chinese===
载重和阻力都大减之后——阻力比汽油的重量重要得多——爬升到几百英尺高度已不在话下；可是一小时后，随着群山逼近、下方地势越走越高，我们又贴着牧豆树和仙人掌的尖顶擦飞了。最后，克林克只好把他的油桶从舱边扔了下去，我也只得把飞机压进一道山沟，才没有撞上地面。把第一只桶留在布鲁克斯机场已经够让人泄气的了，但我想克林克永远忘不了第二只桶在我们脚下地面上摔开的那一幕。

===Original===
Sometime later we came to the West Nueces River and, mistaking it for the Rio Grande, turned north. We had been cutting across country but had hardly flown long enough to have reached the Rio Grande. The Rio Grande was the only river, according to my map, with a railroad running along the northeast bank. We followed the West Nueces to Camp Wood where the rails ended. By that time I knew that the map was in error and we were on the wrong course, but as there was insufficient fuel remaining to warrant our cutting across the mountains to the west, we landed in a small sheep pasture near Barksdale. This pasture was not large enough for us both to take off together so I flew the ship over to Camp Wood alone and landed in the town square. With the wind blowing from the right direction, and by taking off under two telephone lines and over one road, the square afforded a long enough runway, provided the wind was blowing from the proper direction.

===Chinese===
过了一阵，我们来到西纽埃西斯河（West Nueces River），错把它当成格兰德河（Rio Grande），便转向北飞。我们一路斜穿旷野，其实并没飞到足以抵达格兰德河的时间。按我的地图，格兰德河是唯一一条东北岸沿河铺着铁路的河。我们顺着西纽埃西斯河飞到坎普伍德（Camp Wood），铁轨到那里就到了头。那时我已明白地图有误、我们飞错了方向，但剩余的燃料不足以让我们冒险向西横穿群山，便降落在巴克斯代尔（Barksdale）附近一块小牧羊场上。这块牧场不够我们俩一起满载起飞，我便独自把飞机飞到坎普伍德，降落在镇广场上。只要风从对的方向吹，从两条电话线下方、一条道路上掠过起飞，这广场倒也提供了一条够长的跑道——当然，前提还是风得对向。

===Original===
The next day conditions were ideal but Klink wanted to go to a dance that evening, and the day after, the wind was blowing from the opposite direction. Our remaining time was passing rapidly and we were both anxious to get to California before my return to Brooks Field. If we could get the plane to a larger field six miles south of Camp Wood we would have room to take off with a full load of gasoline.

===Chinese===
第二天条件理想，可克林克当晚要去参加一场舞会；再下一天，风又吹起了相反的方向。剩下的时间过得飞快，我们都盼着在我回布鲁克斯机场之前赶到加利福尼亚。要是能把飞机弄到坎普伍德以南六英里的一块大场地，就有满载汽油起飞的余地了。

===Original===
One of the town streets was wide enough to take off from, provided I could get a forty-four foot wing between two telephone poles forty-six feet apart and brush through a few branches on each side of the road later on. We pushed the ship over to the middle of the street and I attempted to take off. The poles were about fifty feet ahead and just before passing between them there was a rough spot in the street. One of the wheels got in a rut and I missed by three inches of the right wing tip. The pole swung the plane around and the nose crashed through the wall of a hardware store, knocking pots, pans and pitchforks all over the interior.

===Chinese===
镇上有一条街宽度够起飞，条件是我得把四十四英尺长的机翼从相距四十六英尺的两根电话线杆之间穿过去，并且随后还得扫过路两侧的几根树枝。我们把飞机推到街心，我尝试起飞。线杆就在前方约五十英尺处，快到两杆之间时，街面上有处坑洼。一只轮子陷进车辙，我的右翼尖以三英寸之差没能躲过。线杆把飞机甩了个转，机头一头撞穿五金店的墙，锅碗瓢盆和干草叉撒了一屋子。

===Original===
The merchant and his son thought that an earthquake was in progress and came running out into the street. He was highly pleased to find an airplane halfway into his place of business and not only refused to accept anything for damages, but would not even allow us to have the wall repaired. He said the advertising value was much more than the destruction.

===Chinese===
店主和他儿子以为地震了，跑到街上。发现一架飞机半个身子扎在自己的铺子里，他喜出望外，非但拒绝收取任何赔偿，甚至不许我们修那面墙。他说，这广告的价值，远远大过那点损失。

===Original===
The greatest damage to the plane was a broken propeller, although from that time on it always carried left rudder. We wired for a new propeller and a can of dope from Houston and in a few days were hedgehopping the mountain tops in true Canuck fashion on our way west.

===Chinese===
飞机最大的损失是一支断掉的螺旋桨——虽然从那以后，它总是带着向左偏的方向舵。我们发电报到休斯敦要来新螺旋桨和一罐涂布油，几天之后，便以纯正的"加拿大佬"做派掠着山顶树梢，一路向西飞去。

===Original===
A Canuck, or JN-4C is nothing more or less than a Canadian Jenny and while it is lighter and performs a little better than a Jenny, it is subject to the same characteristic of being able to just miss most everything it passes over.

===Chinese===
所谓"加拿大佬"，即JN-4C，说白了就是加拿大版"珍妮"。它比"珍妮"轻、性能略好，却同样有一样本事：从什么东西上空飞过，都总能差之毫厘地擦不着。

===Original===
We passed over the Rio Grande and cut through a corner of Mexico, then landed on one of the Army emergency fields at Pumpville and induced the section boss to sell us enough gasoline to continue our flight.

===Chinese===
我们飞越格兰德河，斜穿墨西哥一角，随后降落在普普维尔（Pumpville）的一处陆军应急场上，说动了铁路工段长卖给我们足够的汽油，继续飞行。

===Original===
Dusk overtook us near Maxon, Texas, and we landed between the cactus and Spanish dagger west of the town, which consisted of a section house and three old box cars of the type used throughout the Southwest for housing the Mexican section hands.

===Chinese===
暮色在得克萨斯州马克森（Maxon）附近追上了我们，我们降落在镇西的仙人掌与"西班牙匕首"刺丛之间。那镇子统共只有一栋工段房和三节旧棚车——就是西南各地用来安置墨西哥养路工的那种棚车。

===Original===
The section boss was living alone. He was soon to be relieved and stationed in some more populated locality. We spent the night with him and in the morning cleared a runway for the ship. Maxon was quite a distance above sea level and as the air was less dense, an airplane required a longer distance to take off in. There was a small mountain on the east end of the field and the land sloped upwards towards the west. We worked until midday cutting sagebrush and cactus. There was a light breeze from the west and the air was hot and rough. After using three-quarters of the runway the Canuck rose about four feet above the ground but stopped there, and when the end of the runway was passed the wings and landing gear scraped along on the sagebrush. As soon as we picked up a little extra flying speed, another clump of sagebrush would slow the ship down again until, after we had gone about two hundred yards, a large Spanish dagger plant passed through the front spar of the lower left wing. After being cut off by the internal brace wires, it remained firmly planted in the center of the outer bay. We landed immediately and found the plane to be undamaged except for a fourteen inch gap in the spar and a number of rips in the wing fabric.

===Chinese===
工段长独自一人住着，不久就要换防，调往人烟稠密些的地方。我们在他那儿过了一夜，早晨为飞机清出一条跑道。马克森海拔相当高，空气稀薄，飞机起飞需要更长的滑跑。场地东头有座小山，地势向西抬升。我们砍山艾和仙人掌一直干到正午。西边吹着微风，空气又热又颠。跑道用掉四分之三时，"加拿大佬"离地约四英尺，却停在了那儿；跑完跑道尽头，机翼和起落架只好擦着山艾丛往前蹭。刚多攒起一点速度，又是一丛山艾把飞机拖慢；这样跑了约两百码后，一大棵"西班牙匕首"穿透了左下翼前梁。它的上半截被翼内张线削断，剩下的牢牢插在外侧翼段的正中。我们立刻降落查看，发现飞机除了翼梁上一道十四英寸的豁口和几处蒙皮撕裂外，并无大碍。

===Original===
The engineer on a passing freight train had seen us go down and stopped long enough for Klink to climb on board. It was agreed that Klink would go to the nearest place where he could get the material to make repairs, while I remained with the plane. We were thirty-two miles from the nearest store and as the section boss was leaving that day for his new location, I walked a mile and a half to a ranch house, where I arranged for accommodations until we were ready to fly again.

===Chinese===
一列过路货车的司机看见我们落下，停了车，正好够克林克爬上去。我们商定：克林克去最近能弄到修理材料的地方，我留下看飞机。我们离最近的商店有三十二英里，而工段长当天就要去新岗位，我便步行一英里半到一户牧场人家，在那里安排了食宿，直到我们能够重新起飞。

===Original===
Klink went all the way to El Paso before he could get any dope and wing fabric. Meanwhile I spent the day with the plane, and a large part of the night following the ranchers' hounds in their search for wildcats and panthers. They had treed a large cat the night before while we were staying in the section house, but were unable to duplicate their performance for my benefit. About all I succeeded in accomplishing after following them for hours, was to pull one dog out of a wire fence which had caught his foot as he jumped over.

===Chinese===
克林克一路跑到埃尔帕索（El Paso），才买到涂布油和翼蒙皮。与此同时，我白天守着飞机，大半夜跟着牧场主的猎犬们搜山找野猫和豹子。我们住在工段房的头天夜里，他们曾把一只大猫赶上过树，可为了我却没法再显一次身手。跟了他们几个小时下来，我唯一的收获，是把一条跳栅栏时被铁丝网挂住脚的猎犬解救出来。

===Original===
Klink returned with a can of pigmented dope, two lengths of crating board, some nails and screws, a can of glue, several balls of chalkline, and enough fabric to replace the torn wing covering. We borrowed a butcher knife, a needle and thread, and an axe from the rancher, and set in to make the Canuck airworthy once more. We hewed the crating down roughly to size, cut it into proper lengths with an old hacksaw blade from our toolkit, and finished it off with the butcher knife. In a short time we had constructed a second box splice similar to the one at Pensacola, but a few feet farther out on the spar.

===Chinese===
克林克带回来一罐带颜料的涂布油、两块板条箱板材、一些钉子和螺钉、一罐胶水、几团粉线，还有足够更换破损翼面的一块蒙皮。我们向牧场主借了屠刀、针线和一把斧头，着手让"加拿大佬"重返蓝天。我们用斧头把板材大致劈成毛坯，用工具箱里那截旧钢锯条锯出合适的长度，再用屠刀削光。不多时，第二个箱形接梁便告成型，同彭萨科拉那个如出一辙，只是位置在翼梁上更靠外几英尺。

===Original===
We had just enough dope to cover the splice, so the fabric in the outer bay was left undoped; and after we had sewed up the longer rents caused by the sagebrush, we were once more ready to take the air.

===Chinese===
涂布油刚好够涂接头那一段，外侧翼段的蒙皮便只好裸着；把山艾丛撕开的几道长口子缝补停当，我们又一次做好了升空的准备。

===Original===
It was too near the fifteenth of March to continue west, so we decided to take the Canuck back to San Antonio where we would finish off the repairs and Klink would continue on to California alone.

===Chinese===
离三月十五日太近了，来不及再往西飞，我们便决定把"加拿大佬"飞回圣安东尼奥，在那里收完修理的尾工，然后克林克独自继续飞往加利福尼亚。

## VII I Join the Air Mail / 七 我加入航空邮政

===Original===
I went by rail to St. Louis and took an OX-5 Standard out for a barnstorming trip in Illinois, Missouri and Iowa. The Post Office Department had just advertised a number of contract air mail routes for bid, one of which was between St. Louis and Chicago by way of Springfield, IL. I decided to barnstorm around the country until it was determined which bidder would be assigned the contract. The Robertson Aircraft Corp. had placed a bid and offered me the position of chief pilot if they were successful in getting the contract.

===Chinese===
我乘火车去了圣路易斯，然后驾驶一架OX-5"标准"式（OX-5 Standard）出门，到伊利诺伊、密苏里和艾奥瓦一带作特技巡飞（barnstorming）。邮政部当时刚公告了一批对外招标的承包航邮航线，其中一条是圣路易斯经伊利诺伊州斯普林菲尔德至芝加哥。我决定先在全国各地巡飞卖艺，等合同归属确定再说。罗伯逊飞机公司（Robertson Aircraft Corp.）投了标，并提出若能中标，便聘我出任首席飞行员。

===Original===
After returning from Iowa I flew on several flying circus dates and made a few short cross country flights to nearby cities.

===Chinese===
从艾奥瓦回来后，我又飞了几场"飞行马戏团"（flying circus）的场子，并到邻近城市作了数次短途越野飞行。

===Original===
On June second, while testing a commercial plane built at Lambert Field, I was forced to make a second emergency jump. I had flown the ship for a few minutes the previous week and on this occasion was testing it for various maneuvers. I had completed everything except tailspins, but when I attempted a right spin the plane refused even to start, so after a second attempt with the same result I gave that up and tried one to the left. The ship fell in easily and, when I reversed the controls after a half turn, came out at once. I then put it into a second left spin and held the controls in a spinning position during two complete turns. When I reversed them they had no apparent effect and using the engine was of no assistance. After trying for fifteen hundred feet to bring the ship out of the spin, I rolled over the right side of the cockpit and, since I had jumped only about three hundred and fifty feet above the ground, I pulled the rip cord as soon as the stabilizer had passed. The chute opened quickly but while it was functioning, I had fallen faster than the spinning ship. On its next revolution the plane was headed directly towards the chute. How close it passed will never be known, for the risers leading up from my harness were twisted and swung me around as the ship passed. However, less than twenty-five feet intervened between the wing and my parachute.

===Chinese===
六月二日，我在试飞一架兰伯特机场制造的商用飞机时，被迫进行了第二次应急跳伞。前一星期我已驾驶这架飞机飞过几分钟，这一次是对它做各种机动的测试。除螺旋俯冲外，各项都已试完；但当我尝试右螺旋时，飞机连进入都不肯，第二次尝试仍是同样结果，我便放弃右螺旋，改试向左。飞机一下子就进入了左螺旋，半圈之后我逆转操纵，它立即改出。接着我让它第二次进入左螺旋，并保持操纵于螺旋位置整整两圈。当我逆转操纵时，它们显然毫无作用，动用发动机也于事无补。在设法使飞机改出螺旋的一千五百英尺高度耗尽之后，我从座舱右侧翻滚出去；由于跳离时离地面只剩约三百五十英尺，水平尾翼一掠过，我便立即拉开了开伞索。降落伞很快张开，但在伞完全生效之前，我坠落得比那架旋转着的飞机还快。飞机转到下一圈时，机头正对着降落伞直冲过来。它擦得有多近永远无从知晓了，因为从我背带上引出的吊伞绳被拧转，飞机掠过时把我整个人旋了半圈。总之，机翼与我的降落伞之间的距离不足二十五英尺。

===Original===
I watched the plane crash in a grain field and turned my attention to landing. A strong wind was drifting me towards a row of high tension poles and it was necessary to partially collapse the chute in order to hasten the descent and land before striking the wires. I landed rather solidly in a potato patch and was dragged several feet and over a road before several men arrived and collapsed the chute. In addition to the strong wind and rough air, collapsing or "cutting" the chute so close to the ground had caused a very rapid descent, and my shoulder had been dislocated in landing.

===Chinese===
我看着飞机坠毁在一片谷物田里，随即将注意力转向自己的着陆。大风正把我朝一排高压电线杆吹去，为了加快下降、在撞上电线之前落地，我不得不把伞局部收拢。我相当结实地砸在一片马铃薯地里，被拖出去好几英尺、越过一道马路，直到几个人赶来进行收伞。除了大风与颠簸气流之外，在如此低的高度收拢——"切"——降落伞造成了极快的下降，落地时我的肩膀脱了臼。

===Original===
In July I went on two weeks active duty at Richards Field, Missouri, where I instructed on Jennies and DH-4's. In August I flew a Curtiss Oriole to Nevada, Missouri, to carry passengers during the Missouri National Guard encampment.

===Chinese===
七月，我到密苏里州理查兹机场（Richards Field）服了两周现役，在那里执教"珍妮"与DH-4。八月，我驾驶一架柯蒂斯"黄鹂"式（Curtiss Oriole）飞往密苏里州内华达城，在密苏里国民警卫队集训期间运送乘客。

===Original===
While at Nevada I received a proposition to fly in a circus in Colorado and, as there was no immediate prospect of starting work on the mail route, I accepted and when the encampment ended I flew the Oriole back to St. Louis and took a train west.

===Chinese===
在内华达城期间，我接到一个去科罗拉多参加飞行马戏团表演的邀约；由于航邮航线一时还开不了工，我便应承下来。集训一结束，我就把"黄鹂"式飞回圣路易斯，然后乘火车西行。

===Original===
On arriving at the field a few miles east of Denver, I discovered the plane I was to fly to be the same Lincoln Standard that Lynch and I had flown to Montana three years before. We did a little barnstorming along the eastern slope of the Rockies preliminary to the start of our flying circus. We had contracted to exhibit before a number of fairs in Colorado and there was nothing barred in the exhibitions. We put on everything the committee was willing to pay for. At the smaller places we used only one plane, but at the more important exhibitions two were required.

===Chinese===
到达丹佛以东几英里的飞行场后，我发现要飞的那架飞机，正是三年前林奇和我飞去蒙大拿的那架"林肯·标准"式（Lincoln Standard）。在飞行马戏团开演之前，我们先沿落基山东坡做了些特技巡飞。我们承揽了科罗拉多多处集市博览会的表演，节目没有任何禁条——委员会肯出钱的项目我们全都上演。小地方只用一架飞机，较重要的演出则需要两架。

===Original===
We flew to the town where a fair was taking place about one day before we were to exhibit. In that way everything was in readiness for the circus and the next morning there was no delay in our performance.

===Chinese===
我们大约提前一天飞到举行集市的城市。这样一来，马戏团的一切都已就绪，次日早晨表演毫不耽搁。

===Original===
We started with wing-walking. The performer would climb out of the cockpit and walk along the entering edge of the wing to the outer bay strut, where he climbed up onto the top wing, and stood on his head as we passed the grandstand. After finishing his stunts on the wing he would go to the landing gear and from there to the center section, where he sat while the plane looped and did Jenny Immelmans. From the center section he went to the tail and then, unless it was an unusual occasion, the wing-walking exhibition was over.

===Chinese===
开场是机翼行走（wing-walking）。表演者从座舱爬出，沿机翼前缘走到外侧翼段支柱处，再攀上上翼，在我们经过看台时倒立其上。翼上动作做完后，他就下到起落架，再到中央翼段，坐在那里随飞机翻筋斗、做"珍妮"式殷麦曼。从中央翼段他再移步机尾，此后——除非是特别场合——机翼行走表演便告结束。

===Original===
After wing-walking came the breakaway. This was accomplished by fastening a cable to the landing gear. The performer went out to the wing tip, fastened his harness to the loose end of the cable and to all appearances fell off the wing. No one on the ground could see the cable and a breakaway always produced quite a sensation. Iron loops were clamped along the cable for use in climbing back up.

===Chinese===
机翼行走之后是断脱坠落（breakaway）。做法是把一根钢缆固定在起落架上。表演者走到翼尖，把背带扣在钢缆的活动端，然后看上去就像从机翼上跌了下去。地面上谁也看不见那根钢缆，断脱坠落总能引起不小的轰动。钢缆上夹有一道道铁环，供他攀回机身上来。

===Original===
One of our feature attractions was the plane change. A rope ladder was attached to the wing of a plane and as one ship flew past the grandstand with the performer standing near the tip of the top wing, a second plane with the ladder attached, passed over the first, so that the ladder was in easy reach of the performer. We usually made two fake attempts to effect the change and actually counted on the third for success. In this way the feat looked more difficult.

===Chinese===
我们的招牌节目之一是换机表演。一架飞机的机翼上系一条绳梯；当一架飞机载着站在上翼翼尖附近的表演者飞过看台时，系着绳梯的第二架飞机从其上方掠过，使绳梯恰好够得着表演者。我们通常先假装尝试两次，实际指望第三次成功。这样，这套绝技看上去就更难了。

===Original===
A parachute was attached to the opposite wing from the rope ladder. After the plane change was completed, the performer jumped off with the chute and the show was over.

===Chinese===
绳梯对侧的机翼上另挂一具降落伞。换机表演完成后，表演者带着降落伞跳下去，演出便全部结束。

===Original===
In the evening we made a night fireworks flight. A series of candles, which when lighted emitted a trail of fire for several hundred feet behind the ship, was attached to each wing. After these candles had burned out, two magnesium flares started burning, lighting up the country below well enough to read a book very clearly. The display was set off by an electric battery in the cockpit.

===Chinese===
入夜，我们进行夜间焰火飞行。两翼各挂一排焰烛，点燃后在机后拖出数百英尺长的火尾。焰烛燃尽之后，两枚镁光焰弹开始燃烧，把下方原野照得亮到可以清清楚楚地读书。整套焰火由座舱里的一组电池点燃。

===Original===
When the plane reached an altitude of two or three thousand feet, a number of bombs were dropped to attract attention; then the switch was thrown in to start the trails and colored lights, and the ship looped and shunted around the comet-like trail of fire.

===Chinese===
飞机升到两三千英尺高度后，先投下几枚爆响弹以吸引注意；随后合上电闸，火尾与彩光一齐点燃，飞机便翻着筋斗、左右穿梭，绕着那条彗星似的火尾飞舞。

===Original===
Our greatest difficulty in night flying lay in lighting the landing fields from which to operate. Sometimes a number of cars were on the field and I landed and took off across the beams of their headlights. Under such conditions the ground was well illuminated and landing very simple. On other occasions there would not be more than one car available and in one instance, on a dark night, I took off and landed by the light of a pocket flashlight which one of the men flashed constantly while I was in the air, to enable me to keep track of the landing field.

===Chinese===
夜航最大的难处在于给作业用的降落场地照明。有时场地上停着好几辆汽车，我就横穿过它们的车头灯光束降落和起飞。在这种条件下，地面照得够亮，降落非常简单。另一些时候却只有一辆车可用；有一次在一个漆黑的夜里，我就靠一支手电筒的光起降——我在空中时，一个伙计不停地朝它挥手示意，好让我盯住那块降落场。

===Original===
At one town in Colorado, we were booked for a fireworks exhibition to be given between darkness and midnight. We had been barnstorming during the day and on our way to this town we ran short of lubricating oil. By the time we had replenished our supply it was too late to get in before dark, and I had never landed at that town before. The owner of the plane, however, was sure that he could easily locate the landing field, even in darkness. He had been there many times and he knew that the field was "right next to the golf links."

===Chinese===
在科罗拉多的一个镇子，我们约定在天黑至午夜之间演一场焰火。我们白天一直在巡飞卖艺，赶往该镇的途中润滑油又不够了。等添足润滑油，天黑前已赶不到，而我也从未在那个镇子降落过。不过机主满有把握，说哪怕摸黑也能轻松找到降落场。他去过许多次，知道那块场地"就在高尔夫球场旁边"。

===Original===
We arrived over the town and after circling a few times, I throttled the motor and shouted "Where's the field?"

===Chinese===
我们飞临该镇上空，盘旋几圈后，我收小油门，喊道："场地在哪儿？"

===Original===
The reply was immediate and full of confidence, "Right next the golf links."

===Chinese===
回答脱口而出，信心十足："就在高尔夫球场旁边。"

===Original===
"Well, where are the golf links?"

===Chinese===
"那高尔夫球场在哪儿？"

===Original===
"I don't know!"

===Chinese===
"我不知道！"

===Original===
I was up against another of the very amusing but equally serious incidents in barnstorming life. We were over strange territory on a dark night and with a rapidly diminishing fuel supply. It was imperative to land within a very few minutes, yet it was not possible to tell one field from another, and even the line fences were not visible.

===Chinese===
我又一次撞上了特技巡飞生涯中那种十分滑稽却同样严重的事件。我们深夜悬在陌生地域上空，燃料正迅速耗尽。必须几分钟内落地，却又分不清哪块是哪块地，连田界的栅栏都看不见。

===Original===
I flew around until the outline of a strawstack appeared in the field below us. This field was outlined on one side by the lighter color of the pasture adjoining it and a number of trees were discernible along the end. There was no way of telling whether it contained posts or ditches, but we had no alternative, and I landed beside the strawstack in the center.

===Chinese===
我绕飞着，直到下方田里显出一座麦草垛的轮廓。这块田一侧以毗邻牧场较浅的颜色为界，一端隐约可见几棵树。无从判断地里有没有木桩或沟渠，但我们别无选择，我便落在田中央那座草垛旁。

===Original===
A hasty examination of the field showed it to be suitable for night flying and we hailed the first car passing for a ride into the town. We had difficulty in locating our fireworks and, as the stores were all closed, still more time was lost before we obtained the bailwire, nails and boards used in building the framework for the flares and candles and attaching it to the plane.

===Chinese===
匆匆查看场地，确认它适于夜间飞行，我们便拦下第一辆过路车搭进镇里。我们的焰火一时找不到，商店又全关了门，等到弄齐搭焰弹焰烛支架并把它固定上飞机所用的绑扎铁丝、钉子和木板，又耗掉了更多时间。

===Original===
It was nearly midnight when the ship was at last ready for the display. Only one car remained on the field. We ran this machine out beside the strawstack and placed it in a position to show up on one side of the stack, in addition to throwing most of its light on the field. I was about to take off when the headlights on the car became so dim that they were entirely useless. One of the men had a pocket flashlight and I took off while he threw its beam on the strawstack.

===Chinese===
飞机终于备好演出时已近午夜。场上只剩下一辆汽车。我们把它开到草垛旁，摆好位置，让它既照亮场地、又在草垛一侧映出轮廓。我正要起飞，车头灯却暗得完全不管用了。一个伙计有支手电筒，他把光束照在草垛上，我就此起飞。

===Original===
It was eleven-forty when I left the ground and eleven-fifty-seven when the last flare had burned out. Our contract had been fulfilled with three minutes to spare.

===Chinese===
我离地时是十一点四十分，最后一枚焰弹燃尽时是十一点五十七分。合同规定的演出如约完成，还富余三分钟。

===Original===
I located the field by the flashing of the spotlight and levelled off and landed by its beam.

===Chinese===
我靠那道闪烁的手电光找到场地，拉平后顺着光束降落。

===Original===
If the position of a light is known and the field is fairly level, it is not necessary to see the ground, but a plane can be stalled in and landed on the darkest night. Pilots often bring their ships down when only the outline of a field is visible. For this reason it is imperative that no obstructions such as farm machinery, or live stock, be allowed to remain on a landing field at night.

===Chinese===
只要知道灯的位置、场地又相当平整，就无须看清地面，哪怕在最黑的夜里也能让飞机失速进场、徐徐落地。飞行员常常只凭一块场地的轮廓就把飞机降下来。因此，夜间绝不可把农具、牲畜之类障碍物留在降落场上，这一点至关重要。

===Original===
Locating a strange flying field by its position in relation to an equally strange golf course, is just one of the many instances in a pilot's life where comedy goes hand in hand with the most serious situations.

===Chinese===
靠一个同样陌生的高尔夫球场的位置来找一块陌生的飞行场，只是飞行员生涯中喜剧与最严峻局面并肩而行的诸多例子之一。

===Original===
In one instance, the story is told of a young pilot who had just learned to fly. He was taking the owner of his plane for a short flight and was demonstrating the various maneuvers he had learned. Finally he put the machine into a spin, but after several turns, discovered that he was not able to come out, and after trying vainly in every way he could remember hearing of from his instructor, he leaned forward in the cockpit and tensely informed his passenger that they were about to crash. Not realizing the seriousness of the situation, the owner replied, "What the —﻿—﻿— do you care, it's not your ship!"

===Chinese===
有这样一个故事：讲的是一个刚学会飞行的年轻飞行员。他带着机主作短途飞行，向他演示自己学过的各种机动。最后他让飞机进入了螺旋，转了几圈后发现改不出来，又把从教官那里听来的各种办法徒劳地试了个遍，于是他俯身向前，紧张地通知乘客：他们就要坠机了。机主没有意识到情况的严重性，答道："你——的——关你什么事，这又不是你的飞机！"

===Original===
It was usually the case that a person inexperienced in the art of flying became quite disturbed over some trivial thing that was of little importance, yet was perfectly at home and enjoying life tremendously at a time when the pilot was straining every effort to avoid disaster.

===Chinese===
惯常的情形是：不谙飞行之道的人会为某件无关痛痒的小事惊慌失措，而在飞行员竭尽全力避免灾难的关头，他却安然自得、乐不可支。

===Original===
People would argue indefinitely, trying to persuade one of us to overload the plane past its danger point by carrying more than two passengers at a time from a small field, and it was of no consequence to them whether the plane cleared the nearest trees by a safe margin, or stalled over the uppermost branches by inches. Explanations on our part were next to useless.

===Chinese===
人们会无休止地争辩，劝我们中的某个人超载飞行——从小场地一次搭载两名以上乘客、把飞机压过危险限度；至于飞机是从最近的树梢上以安全裕度掠过，还是在树顶枝头之上几英寸处失速坠落，他们毫不在乎。我们这边的解释近乎徒劳。

===Original===
If we refused to overload the ship someone cited an example where a plane had carried several passengers at one time and it made no difference what kind of a machine it was, or how large an airport it was operating from. The fact that it carried more passengers than we did indicated that it was operated by a better pilot, and that our plane was not as safe to ride in.

===Chinese===
如果我们拒绝超载，就有人举出某架飞机一次搭载过好几名乘客的例子；那是什么样的机型、从多大的机场起飞，全都无关紧要。它载的乘客比我们多，这一事实本身就表明驾驶它的是更高明的飞行员，而我们的飞机坐起来不够安全。

===Original===
We could struggle along close to the ground trying to get a little altitude and our passengers would have the time of their lives, waving at the people below, but let the motor start to miss, although the plane might be several thousand feet high, with several large fields in sight, and they would glance nervously back at the pilot wishing that they had never considered taking a ride in an airplane.

===Chinese===
我们可以贴着地面挣扎着往上爬、设法多挣一点高度，乘客们却快活到了极点，朝下面的人群挥手；可只要发动机一开始断火——哪怕飞机已在几千英尺高空、视野里就有好几块大场地——他们就会紧张地回头瞟飞行员，恨不得自己从未动过坐飞机的念头。

===Original===
The International Air Races were to be held, that year, at New York during the first part of October and, since our fair contracts were over by the last of September, we decided to enter in the "On to New York" competition, which was for civilian planes only, and was decided by points given for distance, speed, number of passengers carried, and the size of the engine used.

===Chinese===
那一年的国际航空竞赛会（International Air Races）定于十月上旬在纽约举行；由于我们的集市演出合同到九月底全部结束，我们决定报名参加"飞向纽约"（On to New York）竞赛——该竞赛只限民用飞机，按飞行距离、速度、载客人数和所用发动机的大小计分定名次。

===Original===
We had our motor overhauled at Denver and expected to fly from there to San Francisco for the start. Some of the repair parts for the engine were delayed and we were several days late in leaving Denver. Even then it was only through night work and leaving a number of things undone that we got away. A fifty-gallon center section gasoline tank had been installed which, in addition to the regular fuselage tank, gave us a capacity of one hundred and seven gallons.

===Chinese===
我们在丹佛把发动机大修了一遍，打算从那里飞往旧金山参加比赛。发动机的一些修理件延误了，我们离开丹佛时已晚了好几天。即便如此，也是靠连夜赶工、撂下一堆活没干才得以动身。机上装了一只五十加仑的中翼段油箱，加上常规的机身油箱，总容量达到一百零七加仑。

===Original===
We installed the engine one night and idled it for several hours before daybreak in order to work in the bearings; then we took off for San Francisco.

===Chinese===
一天夜里我们装上发动机，赶在破晓前让它低速运转了几个小时，以便磨合轴瓦；随后便起飞前往旧金山。

===Original===
Our first stop was at Rawlins, Wyoming, where the highest field on the transcontinental air mail route is located. We refilled at Rawlins and made Evanston that night.

===Chinese===
第一站是怀俄明州罗林斯（Rawlins），横贯大陆航邮航线上最高的飞行场就在那里。我们在罗林斯加满油，当晚抵达埃文斯顿（Evanston）。

===Original===
At Evanston we were starting the engine preparatory to taxiing over and tying down for the night, when our carburetor caught fire. In the haste to get started we had neither put a fire screen on the intake, nor a drain pipe down from the bowl. The engine was covered with oil and the gasoline overflowing from the bowl carried the flames down around it. Soon the entire nose of the ship was ablaze and although we shovelled earth over the motor, it appeared that the wings would soon catch fire. If the fabric began to burn, the ship was gone. I had just finished removing all loose equipment from the cockpit when a small hand extinguisher arrived and with its aid the fire was soon put out.

===Chinese===
在埃文斯顿，我们正要启动发动机，准备滑行过去过夜系留，化油器突然起了火。匆忙赶路之中，我们既没在进气口装防火网，也没从浮子室接出泄流管。发动机上满是滑油，浮子室溢出的汽油把火焰引着淌向四周。飞机整个机头很快烧成一片，尽管我们往发动机上铲土覆压，机翼看来也快着了。蒙皮一旦烧起来，这架飞机就完了。我刚把座舱里所有松散的设备搬完，一支小手提灭火器送到了，靠它火很快扑灭。

===Original===
All of the ignition wire insulation was burned off but otherwise very little damage had been done.

===Chinese===
点火线的绝缘层全部烧光，除此之外损失不大。

===Original===
We were delayed twenty-four hours rewiring the engine and cleaning out the dirt shovelled on in the attempt to put out the fire.

===Chinese===
重新给发动机接线、清掉灭火时铲上去的泥土，又耽误了我们二十四小时。

===Original===
After Rawlins we stopped at Salt Lake City, and from there we flew over the Great Salt Lake Desert to Battle Mountain, Nevada, where we spent the night.

===Chinese===
过了罗林斯，我们停盐湖城，再从那里飞越大盐湖沙漠（Great Salt Lake Desert），抵达内华达州巴特尔芒廷（Battle Mountain）过夜。

===Original===
We took off from Battle Mountain with full gas tanks and after following the passes until part of the fuel was consumed, and the load correspondingly lightened, we passed over the Sierra Nevada Range at eight thousand five hundred feet, and landed at Oakland, California. The same evening, without refilling, we flew over San Francisco Bay to Crissey Field.

===Chinese===
我们从巴特尔芒廷满油起飞，沿山间隘路飞行，直到烧掉部分燃料、载重相应减轻之后，才以八千五百英尺的高度飞越内华达山脉（Sierra Nevada Range），在加利福尼亚州奥克兰（Oakland）降落。当晚没有加油，我们又飞越旧金山湾（San Francisco Bay），抵达克里西机场（Crissey Field）。

===Original===
The following day we took off from Crissey Field on the start of our race to New York. One of the rules of the contest was that each plane should carry a log with the starting point and number of passengers carried attested to by two witnesses. By the time we had made out the log and serviced our plane, it was afternoon and darkness overtook us at Lovelocks, Nevada.

===Chinese===
次日，我们从克里西机场起飞，开始飞往纽约的竞赛。比赛规则之一是每架飞机须携带一份航行日志，上面写明起点和载客人数，并由两名证人签字证明。等我们填好日志、把飞机整备完毕，已是下午，夜幕在内华达州洛夫洛克（Lovelocks）追上了我们。

===Original===
The next night was spent in Rawlins, Wyoming, after a stop at Salt Lake City for fuel.

===Chinese===
在盐湖城加油停留之后，第二夜宿于怀俄明州罗林斯。

===Original===
We arrived in Rawlins with a valve blowing badly and were delayed a day in pulling the bank and grinding in another valve.

===Chinese===
抵达罗林斯时，一只气门漏气漏得厉害，我们拆下一列缸体、研磨换装另一只气门，又耽搁了一天。

===Original===
We were far behind our schedule due to the late start from Denver; the delay at Evanston, and again at Rawlins; but without further trouble we would still be able to reach New York on time. Another valve began blowing, however, soon after leaving Rawlins, and when we took off from our next stop at Sidney, Nebraska, the motor had lost a number of revolutions.

===Chinese===
由于从丹佛动身已晚，加上在埃文斯顿和罗林斯的两次耽搁，我们远远落后于计划；但只要不再出岔子，仍能按时抵达纽约。然而离开罗林斯不久，另一只气门又开始漏气，等从下一站内布拉斯加州悉尼（Sidney）起飞时，发动机已掉了不少转速。

===Original===
We flew to Lincoln from Sidney and after taking the short remaining time into consideration, we decided to abandon the race and start barnstorming.

===Chinese===
我们从悉尼飞到林肯市，盘算了一下所剩无几的时间，决定放弃比赛，重操特技巡飞旧业。

===Original===
We overhauled the engine at Lincoln and worked over towards St. Louis, where we arrived about the end of October.

===Chinese===
我们在林肯市把发动机大修一遍，然后一路演出着朝圣路易斯方向推进，十月底前后抵达。

===Original===
At St. Louis we decided to tie up for the winter and I began instructing students for the Robertson Aircraft Corporation on OX-5 Standards. The Corporation had been awarded the air mail contract but actual operation was not to start until the next spring, so during the winter months I spent my time instructing and test flying in their commercial service.

===Chinese===
在圣路易斯，我们决定歇业过冬，我开始为罗伯逊飞机公司执教学员，教他们飞OX-5"标准"式。公司已经拿下航邮合同，但实际运营要到次年春天才开始，于是整个冬季，我的时间都花在教学和为他们从事的商用飞行服务做试飞上。

===Original===
For the first time in my flying career I was to be in one plane longer than a few months, so in November, 1925, I enlisted in the 110th Observation Squadron of the 35th Division Missouri National Guard, and was commissioned a First Lieutenant soon afterward.

===Chinese===
在我的飞行生涯中，这是我头一回要长驻一处、超过几个月，于是 1925 年 11 月，我应征加入密苏里国民警卫队第 35 师第 110 侦察中队（110th Observation Squadron），不久之后被任命为中尉。

===Original===
The squadron was stationed on Lambert Field. Every Sunday was spent in flying. We had a number of JN training planes and one TW-3 which was the commanding officer's personal ship.

===Chinese===
中队驻扎在兰伯特机场。每个星期日都用于飞行。我们有好几架JN教练机和一架TW-3，后者是指挥官的专机。

===Original===
The organization was composed mainly of pilots who had flown during the war, but after the Armistice had gone back to civilian life. Their only method of keeping in training was by flying National Guard planes in their spare moments and attending camp two weeks each year.

===Chinese===
这支部队主要由战时飞过、停战后重返平民生活的飞行员组成。他们保持训练的唯一办法，就是闲暇时飞国民警卫队的飞机，每年再参加两周集训。

===Original===
Two nights and one day each week were devoted to military service by these officers and the enlisted men under them. Their pay was small and most of them lost more from neglect of their business than they received for their military services. The remuneration was hardly considered. However they joined the Guard for two reasons: first, because of the opportunity it offered to keep in flying training, and second, because they considered it a patriotic duty to keep fit for immediate service in case of National emergency.

===Chinese===
这些军官及其麾下的士兵每周投入两个晚上和一天服兵役。薪饷微薄，大多数人因疏于照管本业所受的损失，比兵役所得还要多。报酬几乎不在考虑之列。不过他们加入警卫队的原因有二：其一，警卫队提供了保持飞行训练的机会；其二，他们认为保持战备、以便国家危急时刻立即应召服役，是一种爱国义务。

===Original===
Appropriations were not large and often insufficient but, although at times it required part of the squadron's pay checks, the ships were kept in the air.

===Chinese===
拨款不多，常常不足，但飞机始终维持着飞行状态——尽管有时得动用中队官兵的部分薪饷。

===Original===
The National Guard squadrons offer an excellent opportunity for young men to get a start in aviation. Instruction is given each week, covering practically every branch of military aeronautics, and practical flying experience is obtained both in the air and on the ground under actual operating conditions. Each year a few members of the squadron are sent to the army schools at San Antonio for flying training, and upon returning these men take their places in the commissioned personnel of the organization.

===Chinese===
国民警卫队各中队为年轻人进入航空界提供了极好的起点。每周都有授课，几乎涵盖军事航空的各个分支；实际的飞行经验，则是在真实的运行条件下于空中和地面上取得的。每年中队都有几名成员被送往圣安东尼奥（San Antonio）的陆军学校受飞行训练，归来后这些人便进入该部队的军官行列。

===Original===
The inauguration of our Air Mail service was to take place on April fifteenth, and as spring drew near we were kept busy making preliminary preparations. The De Havilands were to be completed and tested; a ground organization built up; the terminal airports decided upon and facilities for taking on and discharging the mail arranged for; in addition to the untold detail arrangements which go to make up the organization of a successful airline.

===Chinese===
我们的航邮开航定于四月十五日。随着春天临近，我们忙于各项先期筹备：德·哈维兰机要完工并试飞；地面机构要组建起来；终端机场要敲定，收发邮件的设施要安排妥当——此外还有数不清的细目安排，它们共同构成一条成功航线的组织。

===Original===
Contract air mail routes are located by the Post Office Department and are so arranged that the mail service can be improved by use of air transportation over other means of communication.

===Chinese===
承包航邮航线由邮政部划定，其布设原则是：借助航空运输，使邮政服务优于其他通信手段。

===Original===
The route is opened for bid and the contract awarded to the lowest bidder who is responsible and in a position successfully to carry on operations.

===Chinese===
航线公开招标，合同授予报价最低、又有能力切实运营的可靠投标人。

===Original===
The contractor can bid any amount up to three dollars per pound of mail and is paid by the pound for the actual amount carried over his route.

===Chinese===
承包商可按每磅邮件最高三美元的额度内任意报价，并按其实际承运量以磅计酬。

===Original===
Our route, between St. Louis and Chicago was operated on a schedule which saved one business day over train service to New York. A letter mailed in St. Louis before three-thirty p.m. was rushed to Lambert Field by a fast mail truck, transferred to the plane which was waiting with engine turning over, landed on the Air Mail field at Maywood, Illinois at seven-fifteen, transferred to one of the Chicago–New York overnight planes, retransferred at Cleveland, Ohio, and was in the Post Office in New York in time for the first morning delivery.

===Chinese===
我们这条圣路易斯—芝加哥航线按这样的时刻表运行：寄往纽约可比火车省出一个营业日。下午三点半以前在圣路易斯投寄的一封信，由快速邮车火速送往兰伯特机场，转装上早已开着发动机等候的飞机，七点一刻降落在伊利诺伊州梅伍德（Maywood）的航邮机场，再转上芝加哥—纽约夜航班机之一，在俄亥俄州克利夫兰再度转机，赶得上进纽约邮政局的第一班晨间投递。

===Original===
An answer could be mailed at New York in the evening and be delivered in St. Louis before noon on the following day. If sent through the ordinary mail it would not arrive until one day later.

===Chinese===
回信当晚在纽约投邮，次日上午便可送达圣路易斯。若走普通邮件，则要晚一天才能到。

===Original===
The advantages of air transportation are most apparent over long distances. The air mail flies from New York to San Francisco in thirty-six hours, whereas a train requires nearly four days to make the same trip.

===Chinese===
航空运输的长处在长距离上最为明显。航邮从纽约到旧金山飞三十六个小时，而火车走完同样的旅程几乎要四天。

===Original===
The United States, through the efforts of the Post Office and the Department of Commerce, is being covered with a network of air mail routes, and it is only a matter of the public using this service before nearly every city in the country will be served by airlines.

===Chinese===
在邮政局和商务部的努力下，一张航邮航线网正覆盖整个美国；只待公众使用这项服务，全国几乎每座城市就将由航线连通。

12. Curtiss Field, L. I.—Police guarding the *Spirit of St. Louis* on its way to the runway for the takeoff
13. Curtiss Field, L. I.—Captain René Fonck wishes Lindbergh the best of luck
14. Curtiss Field, L. I.—Getting ready for the takeoff. "The weather was pretty sick"
15. Curtiss Field, L. I.—Just before starting on the big adventure
16. "I didn't use my periscope all the time, but could look out my windows"
17. Paris, France—A salutation from M. Bleriot
18. Paris, France—Paul Painlevé, French Minister of War, extends his welcome. On the right is Ambassador Myron T. Herrick

20. Paris, France—Crowds at the City Hall at the official reception
21. Paris, France—Guests at the luncheon of M. Bleriot

25. London, England—With H.R.H. the Prince of Wales and Lord Lonsdale (left) in the royal box at the Derby Ball
26. London, England—Crowds pressing around the *Spirit of St. Louis* as the plane landed. Some of the souvenir-hunters managed to tear away bits of the wing
27. London, England—"At Croyden Field I escaped to the top of the observation tower overlooking the crowd"
28. The U.S.S. *Memphis*, flagship on which the author returned to America
29. Washington DC—Coming down the gangplank of the U.S.S. *Memphis*, followed by his mother
30. Washington DC—Charles Evans Hughes confers the Cross of Honor from the United States Flag Association
31. Washington DC—At the tomb of "The Unknown Soldier" at Arlington Cemetery
32. Washington DC—Speeches at Washington Monument. The President at Colonel Lindbergh's right
33. From the Top of Washington Monument
34. Washington DC—At Arlington Cemetery
35. Copy of the $25,000 check presented by Raymond Orteig

37. Welcome in New York Harbor
38. New York City—Riding up Broadway
39. New York City's welcome
40. New York City—A June snowstorm
41. New York City—The parade passing through Central Park where over 400,000 people were gathered. A solid bank of humanity flanked our passage
42. New York City—Parade in Central Park as seen from a nearby skyscraper
43. New York City—Speaking at the ceremonies in Central Park. Governor Smith of New York behind the "mike"
44. Brooklyn, NY—Speaking at the ceremonies in Prospect Park
45. The *Spirit of St. Louis* after her return
46. Mitchel Field, L. I.—After the flight to Washington
47. St. Louis' welcome—Looking down Washington Avenue
48. Mother and son

===Chinese===
1. 《我们》（"We"）
2. 父与子
3. 威斯康星州麦迪逊——中西部一所军校的教官与同学们
4. "圣路易斯精神号"（Spirit of St. Louis）启运
5. 制造这架飞机的人们
6. 密苏里州圣路易斯——纽约至巴黎不着陆飞行的出资人
7. 飞机的机身骨架
8. 研究飞行用导航图
9. 飞机的仪表板
10. 《我们》进行一次试飞
11. 长岛柯蒂斯机场（Curtiss Field）——"圣路易斯精神号"的吉祥物帕齐（Patsy）
12. 长岛柯蒂斯机场——警察护送"圣路易斯精神号"前往跑道准备起飞
13. 长岛柯蒂斯机场——勒内·丰克上尉（Captain René Fonck）祝林德伯格好运
14. 长岛柯蒂斯机场——起飞前的准备。"天气糟透了"
15. 长岛柯蒂斯机场——踏上伟大冒险的前一刻
16. "我并不一直使用潜望镜，而是可以从舷窗向外看"
17. 法国巴黎——布莱里奥先生（M. Bleriot）致意
18. 法国巴黎——法国陆军部长保罗·潘勒韦（Paul Painlevé）致欢迎辞。右侧是美国大使迈伦·T·赫里克
19. 法国巴黎——与杜梅尔格先生（M. Doumerque）及赫里克大使在一起
20. 法国巴黎——官方欢迎仪式上市政厅前的人群
21. 法国巴黎——布莱里奥先生午宴上的宾客
22. 法国巴黎——抵达巴黎后不久，与赫里克大使在大使馆台阶上
23. 英国伦敦——克罗伊登机场（Croyden Field）的欢迎场面，近五十万熙攘的人群聚集于此
24. 比利时布鲁塞尔——官方欢迎仪式上与利奥波德王储（Crown Prince Leopold）在一起
25. 英国伦敦——与威尔士亲王殿下及朗斯代尔勋爵（Lord Lonsdale，左）在德比舞会（Derby Ball）的皇家包厢中
26. 英国伦敦——飞机降落时人群簇拥着"圣路易斯精神号"。一些搜集纪念品的人硬是从机翼上撕下了碎块
27. 英国伦敦——"在克罗伊登机场，我躲到了俯瞰人群的瞭望塔顶上"
28. 美国"孟菲斯"号（U.S.S. Memphis），作者乘之返美的旗舰
29. 华盛顿哥伦比亚特区——走下"孟菲斯"号的舷梯，母亲随行在后
30. 华盛顿哥伦比亚特区——查尔斯·埃文斯·休斯（Charles Evans Hughes）授予美国国旗协会荣誉十字勋章
31. 华盛顿哥伦比亚特区——在阿灵顿国家公墓（Arlington Cemetery）的"无名烈士墓"前
32. 华盛顿哥伦比亚特区——在华盛顿纪念碑（Washington Monument）前发表演说。总统站在林德伯格上校右侧
33. 自华盛顿纪念碑顶俯瞰
34. 华盛顿哥伦比亚特区——在阿灵顿国家公墓
35. 雷蒙德·奥泰格所赠25000美元支票的样张
36. 纽约市——接受奥泰格奖奖章。奥泰格先生站在斯科特上校（Colonel Scott）与我本人之间的中央
37. 纽约港的欢迎
38. 纽约市——沿百老汇而行
39. 纽约市的欢迎
40. 纽约市——六月的暴雪（纸屑）
41. 纽约市——游行队伍穿过中央公园（Central Park），逾400,000人聚集于此。人墙密不透风地夹道而立
42. 纽约市——自附近摩天楼俯瞰中央公园中的游行
43. 纽约市——在中央公园的仪式上致辞。纽约州州长史密斯立于"麦克风"后
44. 纽约布鲁克林——在展望公园（Prospect Park）的仪式上致辞
45. 归来的"圣路易斯精神号"
46. 长岛米切尔机场（Mitchel Field）——飞往华盛顿之后
47. 圣路易斯的欢迎——俯瞰华盛顿大道（Washington Avenue）
48. 母与子

## III London / 三 伦敦

===Original===
The flight from Brussels was comparatively simple and there was little or no strain on the plane. The pilot steered straight across the Channel, reaching England on the southeast corner.

===Chinese===
从布鲁塞尔起的这一程相当简单，飞机几乎没有吃什么力。飞行员径直飞越海峡，从东南角进入英格兰。

===Original===
The weather was nearly perfect; in fact Lindbergh was never privileged during his stay in England to see a real London fog.

===Chinese===
天气近乎完美；事实上，林德伯格在英格兰逗留期间，始终无缘得见一场真正的伦敦雾。

===Original===
It did not seem long before he found himself throttling his motor above the great field at Croyden on the outskirts of London. A tremendous crowd had gathered—a crowd almost as large as that which had watched him land that memorable night at Le Bourget. And again no sooner had his wheels touched the ground than this crowd, too, made a rush for his plane. Fortunately, officials of the Royal Air Club dashed up in a motor car and got the pilot aboard just in time to rescue him from the uncontrollable enthusiasm of the throng.

===Chinese===
没过多久，他便发现自己已在伦敦郊外克罗伊登（Croyden）大机场上空收小油门了。一大群人早已聚集在那里——人数几乎赶得上勒布尔热那个难忘之夜围观他降落的人群。而且机轮刚一触地，这里的人群也朝他的飞机猛冲过来。所幸皇家航空俱乐部（Royal Air Club）的官员们乘汽车疾驰而至，及时把飞行员接上车，从人群难以自抑的热情中把他救了出来。

===Original===
As in Paris, all the carefully laid plans of the reception committee were swept aside. Even Secretary for Air, Sir Samuel Hoare, and Ambassador Houghton were swallowed up by the multitude. Later another crowd, almost as large, was found waiting at the American Embassy for a glimpse of the American air traveller.

===Chinese===
和巴黎一样，接待委员会精心拟定的全部计划都被冲得七零八落。连航空大臣萨缪尔·霍尔爵士（Sir Samuel Hoare）和霍顿大使本人，也被人海吞没了。后来发现，又有一群规模几乎相当的人聚在美国大使馆外，等着看这位美国飞行家一眼。

===Original===
Then came welcomed rest. Lindbergh dined with some friends of the Ambassador and went to bed early. Next morning he went direct to Croyden and found that, despite his fears, very little harm had been done to his ship save for one little hole in the wing and a landing strut that had lost two bolts.

===Chinese===
接着是受欢迎的休息。林德伯格与大使的几位朋友共进晚餐，早早上了床。第二天早晨他直奔克罗伊登，发现飞机尽管让人担心，受损却微乎其微——只是机翼上有一个小洞，一根起落架支柱掉了两颗螺栓。

===Original===
Monday was a comparatively quiet day. There was a luncheon at the Embassy attended by many persons prominent in the government and otherwise. One war hero was perhaps especially interesting to an air man. This was Lieut.-Col. W. A. Bishop, the Canadian ace, who had brought down 72 German planes.

===Chinese===
星期一是相对安静的一天。大使馆有一场午宴，宾客中有许多政界及其他各界的显要。有一位战争英雄对飞行员来说或许尤其有趣——那就是加拿大王牌飞行员W·A·毕晓普中校（Lieut.-Col. W. A. Bishop），他曾击落 72 架德国飞机。

===Original===
In the afternoon a Memorial Day service was held at St. Margaret's Church, Westminster. This honoring in England of our Civil War dead was a strange experience for the visitor. One of the veterans present, Jabez Jrayell, aged 86, had known President McKinley as a comrade in arms. After the service and sermon a procession formed, which, headed by the Stars and Stripes, moved slowly from the church to Westminster Abbey. There, Ambassador Houghton, with Lindbergh at his side, walked to a tomb and laid a wreath on which was inscribed:

===Chinese===
下午，阵亡将士纪念日的仪式在威斯敏斯特圣玛格丽特教堂（St. Margaret's Church, Westminster）举行。在英格兰悼念我们内战的阵亡者，对这位来客是一种奇特的经历。在场的退伍老兵中，有一位 86 岁的贾贝兹·杰雷尔（Jabez Jrayell），当年曾与麦金莱总统（President McKinley）并肩作战。礼拜与布道之后，一支队伍组成了，星条旗为前导，从教堂缓缓行进至威斯敏斯特教堂（Westminster Abbey）。在那里，霍顿大使携林德伯格走到一座墓前，敬献花圈，上面写着：

===Original===
"In memory of England's unknown warrior from the American people."

===Chinese===
"谨代表美国人民，纪念英格兰的无名战士。"

===Original===
In the evening British newspaper men gave Lindbergh a dinner in the Abraham Lincoln room of the Savoy. On the speaker's table before the guest of honor were five sandwiches and a half gallon jar of water. The Chairman gravely announced: "Captain Lindbergh will now partake of his customary meal." After a round of laughing applause the real dinner began.

===Chinese===
晚上，英国新闻界人士在萨伏伊饭店（Savoy）的亚伯拉罕·林肯厅设宴款待林德伯格。贵宾面前的主讲台上，摆着五块三明治和一罐半加仑装的水。主席一本正经地宣布："林德伯格机长现在将用他的惯例餐食。"在一阵会心的大笑鼓掌之后，正式的晚宴才开始。

===Original===
Next morning was the 31st of May. On this day Lindbergh was received by the King of England. King George talked to him alone for some time about his flight, and by his conversation showed he understood a great deal about flying. Setting aside all precedent, he personally presented Lindbergh with the Royal Air Force Cross. The only other Americans who ever received this cross were the crew of the NC-4, the United States Navy plane, which crossed the Atlantic by way of the Azores.

===Chinese===
次日上午是 5 月 31 日。这一天，林德伯格受到英王的接见。乔治国王（King George）单独与他谈了许久他的飞行，言谈间显出他对飞行相当在行。他抛开一切先例，亲自向林德伯格颁授了皇家空军十字勋章（Royal Air Force Cross）。此前获颁此勋章的美国人，只有 NC-4 的机组——那架取道亚速尔群岛（Azores）飞越大西洋的美国海军飞机。


===Original===
After his conversation with the King, Lindbergh was presented to Queen Mary. From Buckingham Palace he went to York House to be received by the Prince of Wales. The Prince wanted to know what he was going to do in the future, to which Lindbergh promptly replied: "I am going to keep on flying."

===Chinese===
与国王交谈后，林德伯格被引见给玛丽王后（Queen Mary）。离开白金汉宫（Buckingham Palace），他又前往约克府（York House）受威尔士亲王（Prince of Wales）接见。亲王想知道他今后打算做什么，林德伯格应声答道："我要继续飞下去。"

===Original===
He visited Prime Minister Baldwin at Number 10 Downing Street, the little house from which so big a slice of this world is being run. Mr. Baldwin took him out on the balcony to watch the colorful ceremony known as the Trooping of the Colors, which epitomizes the dignity and power of the British empire.

===Chinese===
他到唐宁街 10 号拜会了鲍德温首相（Prime Minister Baldwin）——从这幢小房子里，掌管着这个世界好大一块。鲍德温先生带他走上阳台，观看名为"军旗敬礼分列式"（Trooping of the Colors）的绚丽典礼，那是大英帝国尊严与权势的缩影。

===Original===
At a luncheon given by the Air Council, Lindbergh was presented with the *Daily Mail*'s gold aviation cup, which was instituted many years ago by the late Lord Northcliffe. Here Sir Samuel Hoare was the principal speaker. After complimenting Lindbergh on his flight he continued:

===Chinese===
在航空委员会（Air Council）举行的午宴上，林德伯格获赠《每日邮报》（Daily Mail）金质航空杯——此奖杯是已故的诺思克利夫勋爵（Lord Northcliffe）多年前设立的。席间萨缪尔·霍尔爵士是主宾演讲人。他对林德伯格的飞行称许一番之后，接着说道：

===Original===
"There are some foolish people—I am glad to think there are very few of them—who are asking you the question: 'Of what use to the world are these efforts and sacrifices? Of what use to the world is a flight like Captain Lindbergh's?' If I had time I should prove to them that from a technical point of view these long distant flights are of great value. They stimulate progress; they test reliability.

===Chinese===
"有些愚蠢的人——我想令人欣慰的是这种人很少——会向你提出这样的问题：'这些努力和牺牲对世界有什么用？林德伯格机长这样的飞行对世界有什么用？'倘若时间允许，我当向他们证明：从技术角度看，这些远程飞行价值重大。它们刺激进步，检验可靠性。

===Original===
"Is it not of value to the technical progress of aviation that a single air-cooled engine of 220 hp, consuming only 10 gallons of petrol an hour, should have travelled over 3,600 miles and been fit for another lap at the end of this?

===Chinese===
"一台 220 马力的气冷发动机，每小时只烧 10 加仑汽油，飞了 3,600 英里还绰绰有余、到终点还能再飞一程——难道这对航空的技术进步没有价值吗？

===Original===
"Is not a long distance flight of this kind of great value as a test of aerial navigation? Flying through fogs and storms, Captain Lindbergh never seems to have deflected from his course. Surely this experience is not only a testimony of his great skill as a navigator but also a lesson in the study of navigation. But I set aside these technical justifications, for upon a flight of this kind the world at large rightly reaches its verdict upon broader grounds. The peoples of many countries are today applauding Captain Lindbergh's achievement not so much because some material gain will be obtained in this or that way, but because it is a fine example of nerve and endurance, of skill, courage, enterprise and adventure.

===Chinese===
"这样的远程飞行，作为对空中导航的检验，难道不也是极有价值的吗？穿越浓雾与风暴，林德伯格机长似乎从未偏离过航线。这段经历当然不仅证明了他作为领航员的高超技艺，也是导航研究上生动的一课。不过这些技术上的道理且放一边，因为对这样一次飞行，世人自会站在更宽广的立场上作出公允的评判。今天，许多国家的人民为林德伯格机长的成就喝彩，与其说是因为可以由此获得这样那样的物质利益，不如说是因为它是胆魄与耐力、技艺、勇气、进取与冒险精神的一个杰出典范。

===Original===
"The more drab the world becomes the more gladly we welcome such fine achievements as his.

===Chinese===
"世界越是变得单调灰暗，我们越是乐于迎来像他这样美好的成就。

===Original===
"Today therefore I ask you to drink to the health of Captain Lindbergh as the pilot who has broken the world's record, and as a worthy representative of our close friends and war allies, the pilots of the United States of America. Still more, however, do I ask you to drink to his health as a young man who embodies the spirit of adventure and lights up the world with a flash of courage and daring, and, I am glad to say, of success."

===Chinese===
"因此，今天我请大家为林德伯格机长的健康干杯——他是打破世界纪录的飞行员，是我们的密友与战争盟友、美利坚合众国飞行员们的当之无愧的代表。而更重要的是，我请诸位为这样一个青年人的健康干杯——他体现着冒险的精神，以一道勇气与胆识、并且我很高兴地说、还有成功的光芒，照亮了这个世界。"

===Original===
In the late afternoon, at the invitation of Lord and Lady Astor, Lindbergh had tea at the House of Commons. That evening the Royal Air Club gave him a dinner at the Savoy. From there he went to a Swedish festival and at midnight attended the famous Derby Eve Ball at Albert Hall. He arrived with the Prince of Wales and as he entered the band struck up "Yankee Doodle." There he made his shortest speech of many short ones. It was simply: "I thank you for my reception tonight. It has been one of the greatest of my life."

===Chinese===
傍晚时分，应阿斯特勋爵夫妇（Lord and Lady Astor）之邀，林德伯格在下议院（House of Commons）用了茶。当晚，皇家航空俱乐部在萨伏伊饭店为他设宴。宴后他又赶赴一个瑞典节的庆典，午夜时分出席了阿尔伯特大厅（Albert Hall）著名的德比前夜舞会。他与威尔士亲王同来，一进门，乐队便奏起了《扬基歌》（Yankee Doodle）。在那里，他作了许多简短讲话中最短的一篇，只有一句："感谢你们今晚对我的接待。这是我一生中最盛大的时刻之一。"

===Original===
On June 1st Lindbergh saw his first Derby. Three hundred thousand people had travelled to Epsom Downs to see this great traditional spectacle of horse racing. As the visiting flier was guest of Lord Lonsdale he sat in a box surrounded by royalty.

===Chinese===
6 月 1 日，林德伯格观看了他生平第一场德比马赛。三十万人赶到埃普索姆赛马场（Epsom Downs），观看这项历史悠久的赛马盛事。这位远来的飞行家是朗斯代尔勋爵（Lord Lonsdale）的客人，坐在被王亲国戚环绕的包厢里。

===Original===
That evening was his last in England's capital. The combined American societies of London entertained him at a banquet. After many speeches, Sir Samuel Hoare rose once more to the occasion and expressed this parting thought:

===Chinese===
当晚是他在英国首都的最后一夜。伦敦各家美国社团联合设宴款待他。在诸多演讲之后，萨缪尔·霍尔爵士再度起身，说了这样一段临别赠言：

===Original===
"Perhaps before long, instead of a single flight, we can induce you to make a regular habit of it. The sooner air communications are established between the two English speaking nations, the better our relations will be. You came to us as a great aviator, but I know you leave as a real friend of England. I am not sure that the latter part of your trip has not been as important as your first.

===Chinese===
"也许不久之后，我们就能劝得您把单次的飞行变成一项常规——两个英语民族之间的空中交通建立得越早，我们的关系就会越好。您以一位伟大飞行家的身份来到我们中间，但我知道，您离开时已是英格兰真正的朋友。我甚至觉得，您此行的后半段与前半段同样重要。

===Original===
"It was a triumph of man over machinery, of man over the brute forces of nature. The flight was a tribute to the young men of the world—of the new generation which has sprung up since the war, determined to subdue the forces of nature—determined in the near future to make the air a great highway for intercourse between your people and ours."

===Chinese===
"这是人对机器的胜利，是人对自然蛮力的胜利。这次飞行是献给全世界青年人的赞歌——献给大战之后成长起来的新一代，他们决心征服自然的力量，决心在不久的将来，使天空成为你们人民与我们人民之间交往的通衢大道。"

===Original===
The day set for departure was misty. On arrival at Kinnerly Airdrome Lindbergh found conditions too difficult to fly to Paris. So there he remained that night as the guest of the Royal Air Force. But he had little sleep; for at 3:30 a messenger awakened him with word the weather was clearing.

===Chinese===
预定的启程之日雾气蒙蒙。一到金纳利机场（Kinnerly Airdrome），林德伯格便发现天气条件太差，无法飞往巴黎。于是当夜他以英国皇家空军客人的身份留宿那里。但他没睡多少；凌晨 3 点 30 分，一名信使唤醒了他，说天气正在转晴。

===Original===
He hopped off at 6:20 a.m. but thirty-eight minutes later, due to low visibility, he came down at Lympnel, England. At eight o'clock a big Handley Page mail and passenger plane flew over. Whereupon Lindbergh quickly went aloft and used the big ship as a guide all the way to Le Bourget.

===Chinese===
他于上午 6 点 20 分起飞，但三十八分钟后，因能见度太低，降落在英格兰的林普内尔（Lympnel）。八点钟，一架大型汉德利·佩奇式（Handley Page）邮运客机从头顶飞过。林德伯格随即迅速升空，一路以这架大飞机为向导，直抵勒布尔热。

===Original===
In the afternoon he attended a ceremony at the Swedish Church in Paris and the next morning—Saturday June 5th—he took off for Cherbourg at 9:22 accompanied by twenty planes.

===Chinese===
下午，他出席了巴黎瑞典教堂的一场仪式。次日早晨——6 月 5 日，星期六——他于 9 点 22 分起飞前往瑟堡，有二十架飞机伴飞。

===Original===
Just as he was ready to go, Costes and Rignot, the two French aviators who were leaving on their eastward trip in an effort to beat the nonstop record he had established, came over to say goodbye and he wished them Godspeed.

===Chinese===
正要动身时，两位法国飞行员科斯特与里尼奥（Costes and Rignot）过来道别——他们即将启程东飞，力争打破他创下不着陆飞行纪录。他祝他们一路顺风。

===Original===
On the way to Cherbourg Lindbergh ran into wind, rain, hail and fog. He landed there at 11:35 amid what seemed to be the entire population of the port. He was cordially welcomed by the full staff of city officials. After lunch at the Mayor's château he was motored into the city proper, and at the Gare Maritime a plaque was unveiled commemorating the spot where he had first flown over France on his way to Le Bourget.

===Chinese===
前往瑟堡途中，林德伯格遭遇了风、雨、冰雹和浓雾。他于 11 点 35 分降落，迎接他的几乎是全港的居民。市政府全体官员热忱相迎。在市长府邸用过午餐后，他乘车进入市区，在海事车站（Gare Maritime），一块纪念牌揭幕，纪念他飞往勒布尔热途中首次飞临法国上空的地方。

===Original===
To avoid pressure of the crowd he jumped upon a Cunard tender at the dock and reached the fast launch of Admiral Burrage which carried him to the U.S.S. *Memphis*, ordered by President Coolidge to bring the flier home.

===Chinese===
为避开人群的挤压，他跳上码头一艘冠达公司（Cunard）的交通艇，登上伯拉奇海军上将的快艇，被送往美国军舰"孟菲斯"号——柯立芝总统已下令由它载这位飞行家返美。

## II My First Plane / 二 我的第一架飞机

===Original===
Since I had first started flying at Lincoln, the year before, I had held an ambition to own an airplane of my own. So when I took my last flight with Lynch in Montana, and started down the Yellowstone, I had decided that the next spring I would be flying my own ship.

===Chinese===
自前一年在林肯市初次开始飞行起，我就一直怀有拥有一架属于自己的飞机的抱负。因此，当我在蒙大拿与林奇做了最后一次飞行、顺黄石河而下时，我已下定决心：来年春天，我要驾驶自己的飞机。

===Original===
Consequently when April arrived, I left Miami and went to Americus, Georgia, where the Government had auctioned off a large number of "Jennies," as we called certain wartime training planes. I bought one of these ships with a new Curtiss OX-5 motor and full equipment for five hundred dollars. They had cost the Government nearly twice as many thousands, but at the close of the war the surplus planes were sold for what they would bring and the training fields were abandoned. Americus, Georgia, was a typical example of this. The planes had been auctioned for as little as fifty dollars apiece the year before. A few days after I arrived, the last officer left the post and it took its place among the phantom airports of the war.

===Chinese===
于是，四月一到，我便离开迈阿密，前往佐治亚州的阿梅里库斯（Americus）——政府曾在那里拍卖大批"珍妮"（Jenny），我们对某些战时教练机就是这样称呼的。我以五百美元买了一架这样的飞机，带着崭新的柯蒂斯 OX-5（Curtiss OX-5）发动机和全套装备。当年政府购置它们时每架花了近万美元，但战争一结束，剩余飞机便贱价抛售，训练机场也被废弃。佐治亚的阿梅里库斯正是这种情形的典型例证：一年前，这些飞机曾以每架区区五十美元的价钱拍卖出去。我到达几天后，最后一名军官撤离了驻地，这座机场也就此跻身于大战留下的幽灵机场之列。

===Original===
I lived alone on the post during the two weeks my plane was being assembled, sometimes sleeping in one of the twelve remaining hangars and sometimes in one of the barracks buildings. One afternoon a visiting plane arrived and Reese stepped out of the cockpit. I had not heard from him since we had traded planes in Montana, and he stayed with me on the post that night while we exchanged experiences of the previous year.

===Chinese===
飞机装配的那两个星期里，我独自一人住在驻地，有时睡在仅存的十二座机库之一里，有时睡在营房里。一天下午，一架过路飞机降落，里斯从座舱里钻了出来。自从我们在蒙大拿换机之后，我一直没有他的音讯。当晚他与我一起留在驻地，我们畅谈了各自过去一年的经历。

===Original===
One of the interesting facts bearing on the life of aviators is that they rarely lose track of one another permanently. Distance means little to the pilot, and there is always someone dropping in from somewhere who knows all the various flyers in his section of the country, and who is willing to sit down and do a little "ground flying" with the local pilots. In this way intimate contact is continually established throughout the clan. ("Ground flying" is the term used to designate the exchange of flying experiences among airmen.)

===Chinese===
关于飞行员的生活，有一个有趣的事实：他们彼此之间很少永久失去联系。对飞行员来说，距离算不了什么，总有人从某个地方顺道来访，他认识那一带所有的飞行员，也愿意坐下来与当地飞行员来一番"地面飞行"（ground flying，指飞行员之间交流飞行经历的行话）。整个飞行家族就这样始终保持着密切的联系。

===Original===
I had not soloed up to the time I bought my Jenny at Americus, although at that time the fact was strictly confidential.

===Chinese===
直到在阿梅里库斯买下这架"珍妮"时，我还没有单飞过，不过这个事实在当时是严格保密的。

===Original===
After my training at Lincoln I had not been able to furnish the required bond and, although I had done a little flying on cross country trips with Bahl and Lynch, I had never been up in a plane alone. Therefore when my Jenny was completely assembled and ready to fly I was undecided as to the best method of procedure. No one on the field knew that I had never soloed. I had not been in a plane for six months; but I did not have sufficient money to pay for more instruction, so one day I taxied to one end of the field, opened the throttle and started to take off. When the plane was about four feet off the ground, the right wing began to drop, so I decided that it was time to make a landing. I accomplished this on one wheel and one wing skid but without doing any damage to the ship. I noticed that the wind was blowing hard and suddenly decided that I would wait for calmer weather before making any more flights and taxied back to the hangar.

===Chinese===
在林肯受训后，我一直交不起那笔必需的保证金；虽然跟巴尔和林奇在长途转场中飞过一些，我却从未独自驾机升过空。因此，当我的"珍妮"全部装配完毕、可以飞行时，我一时拿不准该怎样着手才最稳妥。机场上没有人知道我没单飞过。我已有六个月没碰过飞机，但又拿不出足够的钱付更多的训练费，于是有一天，我把飞机滑到机场一端，推开油门，开始起飞。飞机离地约四英尺时，右翼开始下坠，我便断定该降落了。我用一个机轮和一根翼橇完成了这次降落，飞机毫发无损。我注意到风刮得正紧，便突然决定等天气平静些再飞，于是滑回了机库。

===Original===
A pilot who was waiting for delivery on one of the Jennies offered to give me a little dual instruction, and I flew around with him for thirty minutes and made several landings. At the end of this time he taxied up to the line and told me that I would have no trouble and was only a little rusty from not flying recently. He advised me to wait until evening when the air was smooth and then to make a few solo flights.

===Chinese===
一位正等待提货"珍妮"的飞行员主动提出给我做一点双座带飞，我跟他飞了三十分钟，做了几次降落。结束后他把飞机滑回停机线，告诉我说我不会有问题，只是近来没飞、手生了些。他建议我等到傍晚气流平稳时再飞几个单飞起落。

===Original===
When evening came I taxied out from the line, took one last look at the instruments and took off on my first solo.

===Chinese===
傍晚来临，我把飞机滑出停机线，最后看了一眼仪表，开始了我的第一次单飞。

===Original===
The first solo flight is one of the events in a pilot's life which forever remains impressed on his memory. It is the culmination of difficult hours of instruction, hard weeks of training and often years of anticipation. To be absolutely alone for the first time in the cockpit of a plane hundreds of feet above the ground is an experience never to be forgotten.

===Chinese===
首次单飞是飞行员一生中永远铭刻在记忆里的大事之一。它是多少小时的艰难受训、多少星期的刻苦操练、往往还有多少年的热切期盼的顶点。平生第一次独自坐在几百英尺高空的座舱里，那种体验终生难忘。

===Original===
After a week of practice flights around Southern Field I rolled my equipment and a few spare parts up in a blanket, lashed them in the front cockpit and took off for Minnesota.

===Chinese===
在南方机场（Southern Field）周边练习飞行一周之后，我把装备和几件备件卷进一条毯子，捆在前座舱里，起飞前往明尼苏达。

===Original===
This was my first cross country flight alone, less than a week after my solo hop. Altogether I had less than five hours of solo time to my credit. I had, however, obtained invaluable experience the year before while flying around in the western states with Biffle, Bahl, and Lynch.

===Chinese===
这是我的第一次单独长途转场飞行，距我单飞尚不足一周。我的单飞时间累计还不到五小时。不过，头一年与比弗尔、巴尔和林奇在西部各州飞来飞去，我已获得了无比宝贵的经验。

===Original===
While learning to fly in Nebraska the previous spring, I discovered that nearly every pilot in existence had flown in Texas at one time or another during his flying career. Accordingly I decided that, at the first opportunity I would fly to Texas myself and although I travelled a rather roundabout way from Georgia to Minnesota, my course passed through Texarkana en route.

===Chinese===
前一年春天在内布拉斯加学飞时，我就发现几乎每一个现役飞行员在其飞行生涯中都曾在得克萨斯飞过。于是我打定主意，一有机会便亲自飞一趟得克萨斯。虽然我从佐治亚回明尼苏达绕了个不小的弯子，航线却途经特克萨卡纳（Texarkana）。

===Original===
The first hop was from Americus to Montgomery, Alabama, and passed over some fairly rough territory of which both Georgia and Alabama have their share.

===Chinese===
第一段航程从阿梅里库斯到亚拉巴马州的蒙哥马利（Montgomery），途经相当崎岖的地带——这类地形佐治亚和亚拉巴马都各有一份。

===Original===
I had been warned before leaving the field, that the airline course to Texas was over some of the "worst flying country in the south" and had been advised to take either a northern course directly to Minnesota or to follow the Gulf of Mexico. This advice served to create a desire to find out what the "worst flying country in the south" looked like. I had a great deal of confidence in my Jenny with its powerful OX-5 engine, and it seemed absurd to me at that time to detour by airplane. Consequently I laid my route in the most direct line possible to conform with my limited cruising range with forty gallons of fuel.

===Chinese===
起飞离场前就有人告诫过我，通往得克萨斯的航线要飞越"南方最恶劣的飞行乡野"，并劝我或者取道北路直飞明尼苏达，或者沿着墨西哥湾绕行。这番劝告反倒勾起了我的好奇心，想看看"南方最恶劣的飞行乡野"究竟是什么模样。我对我的"珍妮"和它那台有力的 OX-5 发动机信心十足，况且在我看来，坐飞机还要绕道，未免可笑。于是我按最直接的直线规划航线，只要不超出四十加仑燃料所限的续航半径就行。

===Original===
The flight to Montgomery was uneventful. I landed at the army field there before noon, filled the fuel tanks and took off again for Meridian, Mississippi.

===Chinese===
飞往蒙哥马利的一路平安无事。我在中午前降落在那里的陆军机场，加满油箱，再度起飞，前往密西西比州的梅里迪安（Meridian）。

===Original===
I arrived over Meridian in late afternoon and for the first time was faced with the problem of finding a suitable field and landing in it.

===Chinese===
傍晚时分我抵达梅里迪安上空，平生第一次面临这样的难题：要自己找一块合适的场地，并降落在那里。

===Original===
An experienced pilot can see at a glance nearly everything necessary to know about a landing field. He can tell its size, the condition of the ground, height of grass or weeds, whether there are any rocks, holes, posts or ditches in the way, if the land is rough and rolling or flat and smooth; in short whether the field is suitable to land in or if it would be advisable to look for another and better one. In fact, the success of a barnstorming pilot of the old days was measured to a large extent by his artfulness in the choice of fields from which to operate. Often, in case of motor failure, the safety of his passengers, himself, and his ship depended upon his alertness in choosing the best available landing place and his ability in maneuvering the plane into it. If his motor failure was only partial or at high altitude, time was not so essential, as a plane can glide a great distance, either with a motor which only "revs" down a couple of hundred rpm or without any assistance from the engine at all. The average wartime machine could glide at least five times its height, which meant that if it was five thousand feet above the ground the pilot could pick a field to land in five miles away with safety; but if the failure was soon after the takeoff, then instant decision and immediate action were necessary.

===Chinese===
有经验的飞行员对一块降落场，几乎一眼就能看出所有需要了解的情况：场地大小、地面状况、草或杂草的高低、有无岩石、坑洼、木桩或沟渠挡道，地形是崎岖起伏还是平坦规整；总之，这块场地是否适于降落，还是不如另觅一处更好的。事实上，昔日特技巡飞飞行员成就的大小，很大程度上就取决于他挑选作业场地的眼光。一旦发动机失灵，乘客、他本人和飞机的安全，往往就系于他能否当机立断选中现有条件最好的降落地点，并能否把飞机灵巧地操纵进去。如果发动机只是部分失效，或者失灵时飞机尚在高空，时间就不那么紧要，因为一架飞机——无论发动机只是掉了每分钟几百转，还是完全帮不上忙——都能滑翔很远。战时飞机平均至少能滑翔五倍于自身高度的距离，也就是说，如果它处在五千英尺高空，飞行员尽可以放心选定五英里外的一块场地降落；可如果失灵恰恰发生在起飞后不久，那就必须当机立断、立即行动。

===Original===
An amateur, on the other hand, has not overcome the strangeness of altitude, and the ground below looks entirely different than it does from the air, although there is not the sensation, in an airplane, of looking down as from a high building. Hills appear as flat country, boulders and ditches are invisible, sizes are deceptive and marshes appear as solid grassland. The student has not the background of experience so essential to the successful pilot, yet his only method of learning lies in his own initiative in meeting and overcoming service conditions.

===Chinese===
而生手则不然：他还没有消除对高度的陌生感，俯瞰之下的地面与空中看去的样子截然不同——尽管在飞机上并没有像站在高楼顶上往下望的那种眩晕感。山丘看似平地，巨石和沟渠无从辨认，尺寸大小令人错判，沼泽看上去倒像坚实的草地。学员还不具备一名成功飞行员所必需的经验底子，而他唯一的学习途径，便是靠自己的主动去应对并克服实际飞行中的种种状况。

===Original===
There was no regular airport in Meridian in 1923, and a few fields available for a reasonably safe landing. After a half hour's search I decided on the largest pasture I could see, made the best kind of a short field landing I knew how by coming down just over the treetops, with the engine wide open, to the edge of the field, then cutting the gun and allowing the ship to slow down to its landing speed. This method brings the plane in with tremendous velocity and requires a much larger landing field than is necessary, but until the pilot has flown long enough to have the "feel" of his ship it is far safer to come in fast than too slow.

===Chinese===
1923年的梅里迪安没有正规机场，只有几块可供较为安全降落的场地。搜寻半小时后，我选定了目力所及最大的一块牧场，用我所会的最稳妥的小场地降落法：开着全油门、贴着树梢下到场边，然后收油门，让飞机减速到降落速度。这种方法使飞机进场速度极大，需要的场地也比实际必要的大得多，但在飞行员尚未飞到足够时长、尚未摸透座机"脾性"之前，进场宁可偏快，也远比偏慢安全。

===Original===
It had been raining at Meridian and the field was a little soft, so that when my "Jenny" finally did settle to the ground it had a very short roll and there was still some clear ground in front.

===Chinese===
梅里迪安此前一直在下雨，场地有些松软，因此我的"珍妮"最终触地后滑跑距离很短，前方还留着一截空地。

===Original===
I taxied up to a fence corner alongside of a small house and proceeded to tie down for the night. I had gained considerable respect for the wind in Kansas and Nebraska, so after turning off the gasoline and letting the motor stop by running the carburetor dry, (a safety expedient to keep the ever-present person who stands directly under the propeller while he wiggles it up and down, from becoming an aeronautical fatality) I pushed the nose of the plane up to a fence and after blocking the wheels securely, tied each wing tip to a fence post and covered the motor and cockpit with a canvas in case of rain.

===Chinese===
我把飞机滑到一座小屋旁的栅栏角上，准备系留过夜。在堪萨斯和内布拉斯加，我已对风心生足够的敬畏，于是先关掉油路，让化油器里的油烧干、发动机自行停转（这是一条保命的应急措施，免得那种无处不在的人物——总爱站在螺旋桨正下方把它摇上摇下的人——成为航空事故的殉难者），再把机头抵到栅栏边，塞牢机轮，把两侧翼尖各系在一根栅栏柱上，又用帆布把发动机和座舱盖好，以防下雨。

===Original===
By this time the usual barnstorming crowd had gathered and I spent the remaining daylight explaining that the hole in the radiator was for the propeller shaft to go through; that the wings were not made of catgut, tin, or cast iron, but of wood framework covered with cotton or linen shrunk to drum tightness by acetate and nitrate dope; that the only way to find out how it feels to fly was to try it for five dollars; that it was not as serious for the engine to stop as for a wing to fall off; and the thousand other questions which can only be conceived in such a gathering.

===Chinese===
这时，特技巡飞惯常引来的人群已经聚拢，我把天黑前剩下的时间都用来答疑：散热器上那个洞是让螺旋桨轴穿过去的；机翼不是肠线、白铁或铸铁做的，而是木制骨架外蒙棉布或亚麻布、再涂上醋酸与硝酸涂布漆绷得像鼓面一样紧；想知道飞上天是什么滋味，唯一的办法就是花五美元试一试；发动机停车可不如掉一个机翼那样要紧；还有千百个只有这种场合才想得出来的问题。

===Original===
As night came on and the visibility decreased the crowd departed, leaving me alone with a handful of small boys who always remain to the last and can only be induced to depart by being allowed to follow the aviator from the field.

===Chinese===
夜幕降临，能见度渐低，人群散去，只留下我和一小帮男孩——他们总要捱到最后，只有获准跟着飞行员走出场地，才肯离去。

===Original===
I accepted an invitation to spend the night in the small house beside the field.

===Chinese===
我接受了邀请，在场地旁那座小屋里过夜。

===Original===
The next morning I telephoned for a gas truck to come out to the field and spent the time before the truck arrived in the task of cleaning the distributor head, draining the carburetor jet wells and oiling the rocker arms on the engine.

===Chinese===
第二天早晨，我打电话叫一辆加油车开到场地来，等车的时间则用来清扫分电盘盖、放空化油器量孔油井，并给发动机的气门摇臂上油。

===Original===
While I was working, one of the local inhabitants came up and volunteered the information that he had been a pilot during the war but had not flown since and "wouldn't mind takin' a ride again." I assured him that much as I would enjoy taking him up, flying was very expensive and that I did not have a large fund available to buy gasoline. I added that if he would pay operating costs, which would be five dollars for a short ride, I would be glad to accommodate him. He produced a five dollar bill and after warming up the motor I put him in the cockpit and taxied through the mud to the farthest corner of the field. This was to be my first passenger.

===Chinese===
我干活时，一个当地人走上前来，主动告诉我他大战期间当过飞行员，此后再没飞过，"不介意再兜一趟风"。我向他保证，尽管我很乐意带他上天，可飞行开销很大，我手头并没有多少买汽油的余钱。我又说，如果他肯付飞行成本——短途兜一圈五美元——我很乐意效劳。他掏出一张五美元钞票，我暖机之后便把他让进座舱，驾着飞机碾过泥泞，滑到场地最远的角落。这将是我的第一位乘客。

===Original===
The field was soft and the man was heavy; we stalled over the fence by three feet and the nearest tree by five. I found myself heading up a thickly wooded slope, which was sloping upward at least as fast as I was climbing in that direction and for three minutes my Jenny and the slope fought it out over the fifteen feet of air between them. Eventually, however, in the true Jenny style we skimmed over the hilltop and obtained a little reserve altitude. I had passed through one of those almost-but-not-quite accidents for which Jennies are so famous and which so greatly retarded the growth of commercial flying during the postwar period.

===Chinese===
场地松软，这位先生又富态；我们越过栅栏时余量只有三英尺，越过最近的树只有五英尺。我发现自己正朝一道林木茂密的坡地冲去，而那道坡向上抬升的速度至少不亚于我爬升的速度，足足三分钟里，我的"珍妮"和那道山坡就在它们之间那十五英尺的空气中殊死较量。最终，凭着"珍妮"一贯的本色，我们擦着坡顶掠了过去，还挣得了一点备用高度。我闯过了那种"差点儿就出事"的事故——"珍妮"正因此闻名，而这种事故也大大拖累了战后商业飞行的发展。

===Original===
I decided that my passenger was entitled to a good ride after that takeoff and kept him up chasing a buzzard for twenty minutes. After we landed he commented on the wonderful takeoff and how much he enjoyed flying low over the treetops; again assured me that he had flown a great deal in the war; and rushed off to tell his friends all about his first airplane ride.

===Chinese===
我觉得经历了那样一次起飞之后，我的乘客理应好好兜一圈，便带着他在天上追了一只秃鹫二十分钟。降落后，他对那记精彩的起飞赞叹不已，说贴着树梢低飞多么过瘾；再次向我保证他大战时飞过很多次；随后便飞奔而去，把他的首次空中之旅讲给朋友们听。

===Original===
The gasoline truck had arrived and after servicing the ship I took off again and headed west. I had no place in mind for the next stop and intended to be governed by my fuel supply in picking the next field.

===Chinese===
加油车到了，给飞机加完油，我再度起飞，朝西飞去。下一站停哪里我心里没数，打算全凭燃料余量来决定下一块场地。

===Original===
The sky was overcast with numerous local storms. I had brought along a compass, but had failed to install it on the instrument board, and it was of little use in a suitcase out of reach. The boundary lines in the south do not run north and south, east and west as they do in the Northern states but curve and bend in every conceivable direction, being located by natural landmarks rather than meridians and parallels. I was flying by a map of the entire United States, with each state relatively small.

===Chinese===
天空阴云密布，局部雷雨频繁。我随身带了罗盘，却没装到仪表板上，搁在手够不着的提箱里，几乎派不上用场。南方的地界不像北方各州那样横平竖直、正南正北，而是朝着各个想得到的方向曲折弯绕，以天然地标而非经纬线来划定。我导航用的是一张全美国地图，每个州都显得很小。

===Original===
I left Meridian and started in the direction of Texas, cutting across country with no regard for roads or railways. For a time during the first hour I was not sure of my location on the map, but soon passed over a railway intersection which appeared to be in the proper place and satisfied me about my position. Then the territory became wilder and again I saw no checkpoints. The storm areas were more numerous and the possible landing fields farther apart, until near the end of the second hour I decided to land in the first available field to locate my position and take on more fuel. It required nearly thirty more minutes to find a place in which a plane could land and take off with any degree of safety, and after circling the field several times to make sure it was hard and contained no obstacles, I landed in one corner, rolled down a hillside, taxied over a short level stretch, and came to rest halfway up the slope on the far side of the field.

===Chinese===
