我离开梅里迪安，朝得克萨斯方向飞去，抄田野直线穿行，全然不管公路铁路。头一个小时里，有段时间我在地图上吃不准自己的位置，但不久便飞越一处铁路道口，它似乎正在应在的位置上，让我放了心。随后地势愈发荒僻，又见不到检查点。雷雨区越来越多，可降场地相隔越来越远，到第二个小时临近结束时，我决定见着第一块可用的场地就落下去，弄清方位、补充燃料。又花了将近三十分钟，我才找到一处能让飞机多少安全起降的地方。我绕着场地盘旋数圈，确认地面坚实、没有障碍之后，降在场角，顺着坡面滚下去，滑过一小段平地，停在了场地远侧半坡上。

===Original===
A storm was approaching rapidly and I taxied back towards the fence corner at rather high speed. Suddenly I saw a ditch directly in front of me and an instant later heard the crash of splintering wood as the landing gear dropped down and the propeller came in contact with the ground. The tail of the plane rose up in the air, turned almost completely over, then settled back to about a forty-five degree angle. My first "crack-up"!

===Chinese===
一场雷雨正迅速逼近，我以相当高的速度朝栅栏角滑回去。突然，我看到正前方横着一条沟，转瞬间便听到木片崩裂的巨响——起落架陷了下去，螺旋桨打到了地面。机尾猛地翘向空中，机身几乎完全立起，随后回落到约四十五度角。我的第一次"摔机"！

===Original===
I climbed out of the cockpit and surveyed the machine. Actually the only damage done was to the propeller, and although the wings and fuselage were covered with mud, no other part of the plane showed any marked signs of strain. I had taxied back about thirty feet east of the landing tracks and had struck the end of a grass-covered ditch. Had I been ten feet farther over, the accident would never have happened. The usual crowd was assembling, as the impact of the "prop" with the ground had been heard in all of the neighboring fields and an airplane was a rare sight in those parts.

===Chinese===
我爬出座舱，检视飞机。实际上唯一损毁的只是螺旋桨，机翼和机身虽然糊满了泥，其余部件却没有明显的受力痕迹。我滑回时偏在降落轨迹以东约三十英尺，撞上了一条被荒草掩盖的沟的尽头。倘若再偏过十英尺，这场事故根本就不会发生。惯常的人群又聚拢过来，因为"螺旋桨"撞击地面的声音四邻八舍的田里都听得见，况且在那一带，飞机可是稀罕物。

===Original===
They informed me that I was halfway between Maben and Mathiston, Mississippi, and that I had flown one hundred and twenty-five miles north instead of west.

===Chinese===
他们告诉我，我现在处于密西西比州马本（Maben）与马西斯顿（Mathiston）之间的中点，而且我飞的方向不是西，而是往北偏了一百二十五英里。

===Original===
When enough men had assembled we lifted the plane out of the ditch, pushed it over to a group of pine trees and tied it down to two of the trees. After removing all loose equipment I rode into Maben with one of the storekeepers who had locked up his business when he heard about the landing of the plane.

===Chinese===
等凑齐了人手，我们把飞机抬出沟，推到一片松树旁，把它系在两棵树上。卸下所有活动装备后，我搭上一个杂货店老板的车进了马本镇——他听说有飞机降落，索性锁了铺子赶来。

===Original===
I wired Wyche at Americus to ship me one of the two propellers I had purchased before leaving, then engaged a room at the old Southern Hotel.

===Chinese===
我给阿梅里库斯的怀切（Wyche）拍了电报，让他把我临行前买下的两具螺旋桨之一运来，随后在老旧的南方旅馆（Southern Hotel）订了个房间。

===Original===
While waiting for the propeller I had extracted promises from half a dozen citizens to ride at five dollars each. This would about cover the cost of the "prop," as well as my expenses while waiting for it to arrive. When it did come I put it on the shaft between showers, with the assistance of most of Maben and Mathiston. I gave the plane a test flight and announced that I was ready to carry passengers when it was not raining too hard.

===Chinese===
等螺旋桨的日子里，我说动了六位镇民，答应每人花五美元上天兜一圈。这大致够付"桨"钱，也够抵我候件期间的食宿开支。螺旋桨到货后，我趁着阵雨间歇，在马本和马西斯顿两镇大半人力协助下把它装上了轴。我试飞了一次，宣布只要雨不太大，随时可以载客。

===Original===
The Mississippians who were so anxious to fly when the propeller was broken immediately started a contest in etiquette. Each and every one was quite willing to let someone else be first and it required psychology, diplomacy, and ridicule before the first passenger climbed into the cockpit. I taxied over to the far corner of the field, instructed my passenger how to hold the throttle back to keep the ship from taking off, and lifted the tail around in order to gain every available foot of space for the takeoff.

===Chinese===
螺旋桨还断着的时候急不可耐要飞的密西西比人，此刻立刻展开了一场礼让竞赛。人人都很乐意让别人先上，直到第一位乘客坐进座舱，先后动用了心理学、外交手腕和冷嘲热讽。我把飞机滑到场地最远的角落，教乘客如何按住油门不让飞机起飞，又抬起机尾调转方向，好为起飞争取每一寸可用空间。

===Original===
The first man was so audibly pleased with his ride that the others forgot their manners of a few minutes before and began arguing about who was to be next.

===Chinese===
第一位乘客飞完之后那份高兴全写在了嗓门上，其余的人顿时把几分钟前的谦让忘得一干二净，争起谁下一个上。

===Original===
That afternoon a group of whites chipped in fifty cents apiece to give one of the negroes a hop, provided, as they put it, I would do a few "flip flops" with him. The negro decided upon was perfectly willing and confident up to the time when he was instructed to get in; even then he gamely climbed into the cockpit, assuring all of his clan that he would wave his red bandana handkerchief over the side of the cockpit during the entire flight in order to show them that he was still unafraid.

===Chinese===
那天下午，一群白人每人凑了五十美分，要送其中一个黑人上天兜一圈，条件是——按他们的说法——我得带他做几个"翻跟头"（flip flops）。被选中的那位黑人起先满口乐意、信心十足，直到叫他登机时也没露怯；他还硬着头皮爬进座舱，向全族老小保证，整个飞行途中他都会在座舱边挥舞他那块红印花大手帕，好让大家看见他依然毫不畏惧。

===Original===
After reaching the corner of the field I instructed him, as I had the previous passengers, to hold the throttle back while I was lifting the tail around. When I climbed back in my cockpit I told him to let go and opened the throttle to take off. We had gone about fifty yards when it suddenly occurred to him that the ship was moving and that the handle he was to hold on to was not where it should be. He had apparently forgotten everything but that throttle, and with a death grip he hauled it back to the closed position. We had not gone far enough to prevent stopping before reaching the other end of the field and the only loss was the time required to taxi back over the rough ground to our starting point. Before taking off the next time, however, I gave very implicit instructions regarding that throttle.

===Chinese===
到了场地角落，我像嘱咐前几位乘客那样，叫他在我抬尾转向时按住油门。等我回到自己的座舱，便叫他松手，推开油门起飞。我们前进了约五十码，他突然意识到飞机在动，而他要抓的那个把手不在该在的地方。显然他什么都忘了，只记得那根油门，于是使出攸关性命的死劲，把它拉回到了关闭位置。好在我们走得还不算远，来得及在抵达场地另一头之前停下，唯一损失的只是沿崎岖地面滑回起点的那点时间。不过，下一次起飞之前，我把关于那根油门的嘱咐说得再明白不过了。

===Original===
I had promised to give this negro a stunt ride yet I had never had any instruction in aerobatics. I had, however, been in a plane with Bahl during two loops and one tailspin. I had also been carefully instructed in the art of looping by Reese who, forgetting that I was not flying a Hisso standard with twice the power of my Jenny, advised me that it was not necessary to dive excessively before a loop but rather to fly along with the motor full on until the plane gathered speed, then to start the loop from a level flying position.

===Chinese===
我答应过给这位黑人做一次特技飞行，可我从未受过任何特技训练。不过，我随巴尔坐飞机经历过两个筋斗和一次螺旋俯冲，还得到过里斯的悉心指点，学过筋斗的门道——他忘了我开的不是功率比"珍妮"大一倍的"希索"标准式（Hisso standard），告诫我做筋斗前不必俯冲过猛，只需开着全油门平飞，待飞机攒足速度，再从平飞姿态进入筋斗。

===Original===
I climbed up to three thousand feet and started in to fulfill my agreement by doing a few airsplashes, steep spirals and dives. With the first deviation from straight flight my passenger had his head down on the floor of the cockpit but continued to wave the red handkerchief with one hand while he was holding on to everything available with the other, although he was held in securely with the safety belt.

===Chinese===
我爬升到三千英尺，开始履行约定：来几下空中掠飞（airsplash）、陡螺旋和俯冲。飞机刚一偏离直线飞行，我的乘客便把头埋到了座舱底板上，却仍用一只手挥着那块红手帕，另一只手抓住一切抓得住的东西——尽管安全带已把他系得牢牢的。

===Original===
Finally, remembering my ground instructions, I leveled the plane off and with wide open motor waited a few moments to pick up maximum speed, then, slowly pulling back on the stick I began to loop. When I had gotten one-fourth of the way around, the ship was trembling in a nearly stalled position; still, the Curtiss motor was doing its best and it was not until the nose was pointing directly skyward at a ninety degree angle that the final inertia was lost and for an instant we hung motionless in the perfect position for a whipstall. I kicked full right rudder immediately to throw the plane over on its side but it was too late, the controls had no effect.

===Chinese===
最后，想起地面上的那番教诲，我把飞机改平，开着全油门稍候片刻，让它攒足最大速度，然后缓缓带杆，开始做筋斗。转到四分之一处，飞机已在近乎失速的状态下颤动；柯蒂斯发动机仍在竭尽全力，直到机头直指苍穹、呈九十度角时，最后的惯性终于耗尽，有一瞬间我们一动不动地悬在那里，正处于鞭式失速（whipstall）的完美姿态。我立刻蹬满右舵，想把飞机甩向侧方，但为时已晚，操纵面毫无反应。

===Original===
The Negro meanwhile decided that the "flip flops" were over and poked his head over the side of the cockpit looking for mother earth. At that instant we whipped. The ship gathered speed as it slid backwards towards the ground, the air caught the tail surfaces, jerked them around past the heavier nose and we were in a vertical dive; again in full control, but with no red handkerchief waving over the cockpit. I tried another loop in the same manner but just before reaching the stalling point in the next one I kicked the ship over on one wing and evaded a whipstall. After the second failure I decided that there must be something wrong with my method of looping and gave up any further attempt for that afternoon. But it was not until we were almost touching the ground that the bandana again appeared above the cowling.

===Chinese===
那名黑人此时断定"翻跟头"已经结束，把脑袋探出座舱舷边寻找大地母亲。就在那一瞬，我们甩坠了。飞机倒退着向地面滑落、越滑越快，气流兜住尾翼面，把它们猛地甩过较重的机头，我们随即进入了垂直俯冲；操纵又完全有效了，只是座舱上再也不见那块挥舞的红手帕。我以同样的方式又试了一个筋斗，但快到失速点时，我一脚把飞机蹬向一侧机翼，躲开了鞭式失速。两度失败之后，我认定自己做筋斗的方法必有差池，那天下午便放弃了进一步尝试。可是直到我们几乎要擦到地面，那块印花大手帕才重新出现在整流罩上方。

===Original===
I remained in Maben for two weeks carrying over sixty passengers in all or about three hundred dollars worth. People flocked in from all over the surrounding country, some travelling for fifteen miles in oxcarts just to see the plane fly.

===Chinese===
我在马本待了两个星期，总共载了六十多位乘客，约值三百美元。人们从四乡八野涌来，有人赶着牛车走了十五英里，就为看一眼飞机飞行。

===Original===
One old negro woman came up and asked—

===Chinese===
一位黑人老太太走上前来问道——

===Original===
"Boss! How much you all charge foah take me up to Heaben and leave me dah?"

===Chinese===
"老板！你们要收多少钱，才肯把我带上天堂、把我撂在那儿呀？"

===Original===
I could have carried many more passengers but it rained nearly every day and each flight rutted the field badly. When I landed it was necessary to pass over a soft spot between two hillsides, and before taking off I had to taxi back over this soft place on the way to the far corner of the field. During the last few days several men were required on each wing to push the plane through the mud to the hillside beyond. Another difficulty was that the old black wartime rubber shock absorber cord had deteriorated to such an extent that I replaced it with hemp rope and taxiing over the harder parts of the field was a very rough procedure, especially since the ground had been plowed in years gone by and allowed to grow sod without being harrowed.

===Chinese===
我本可以载更多乘客，但几乎天天下雨，每次飞行都把场地碾出深深的辙印。降落时必须经过两道山坡之间的一块软地，起飞前又得滑过这片软地前往场地尽头的角落。最后几天，每侧机翼都得好几个人推着飞机蹚过泥泞，送到那边的坡上。另一个难处是，那根战时的黑色旧橡胶减震绳已经老化得不成样子，我只好换上麻绳，结果在场地较硬的地段滑行时颠得厉害——尤其那地早年犁过又撂荒长草，从未耙过。

===Original===
I made several attempts to find another suitable field nearby but there was none from which I could safely operate.

===Chinese===
我几次三番想在附近另找一块合适的场地，但找不出一块能让我安全作业的。

===Original===
Landing fields are of primary importance to safety in aviation. It is not a question of how small a field a plane can operate from, but rather of how large a field is necessary to make that operation safe.

===Chinese===
降落场对航空安全至关重要。问题不在于飞机能从多小的场地起降，而在于需要多大的场地才能使起降安全。

===Original===
Large and well equipped airports situated close to cities will go far towards developing commercial airlines and keeping the United States at the top in aeronautical activity.

===Chinese===
紧邻城市的大型现代化机场，将大大推动商业航线的开拓，并使美国稳居航空活动之首。

===Original===
The cities who foresee the future of air transportation and provide suitable airports will find themselves the center of airlines radiating in every direction.

===Chinese===
那些预见到空运未来并提供合适机场的城市，将发现自己成为四通八达航线的中心。

===Original===
When an airline is organized, one of the primary considerations is the condition and location of the various landing fields where terminals are contemplated. If the airport is small and in poor condition, or if a passenger must of necessity spend nearly as much time in traveling from the business district out to the field as it will require for him to fly from the field to his destination, then it is very probable that some other city will be selected for the stopping point.

===Chinese===
筹建一条航线时，首要考虑的事项之一，便是拟设航站各降落场的状况与位置。如果机场狭小破旧，或者乘客从商业区赶往机场所花的时间几乎同他从机场飞抵目的地的时间一样长，那么经停点十有八九会落在别的城市。

===Original===
The condition of the field together with the fact that after a heavy rain it was often necessary to carry gasoline in five gallon cans a mile and a half over the railroad tracks by hand forced me to leave Maben and a large number of would-be passengers behind, and early one morning I took off for the last time and again headed for Texas.

===Chinese===
场地状况已然如此，加上每逢大雨之后，常要提着五加仑的油桶、沿铁路路基步行一英里半去人工运汽油，这终于迫使我把马本和一大批跃跃欲试的乘客抛在身后。一天清晨，我最后一次起飞，再度朝得克萨斯飞去。

## X New York to Paris / 十 从纽约到巴黎

===Original===
At New York we checked over the plane, engine and instruments, which required several short flights over the field.

===Chinese===
在纽约，我们对飞机、发动机和仪表做了检查，为此在机场上空试飞了几个短程。

===Original===
When the plane was completely inspected and ready for the transatlantic flight, there were dense fogs reported along the coast and over Nova Scotia and Newfoundland, in addition to a storm area over the North Atlantic.

===Chinese===
飞机全部查验完毕、可以投入跨大西洋飞行时，沿岸以及新斯科舍和纽芬兰上空却报有浓雾，北大西洋上还有一个风暴区。

===Original===
On the morning of May 19th, a light rain was falling and the sky was overcast. Weather reports from land stations and ships along the great circle course were unfavorable and there was apparently no prospect of taking off for Paris for several days at least. In the morning I visited the Wright plant at Paterson, New Jersey, and had planned to attend a theatre performance in New York that evening. But at about six o'clock I received a special report from the New York Weather Bureau. A high pressure area was over the entire North Atlantic and the low pressure over Nova Scotia and Newfoundland was receding. It was apparent that the prospects of the fog clearing up were as good as I might expect for some time to come. The North Atlantic should be clear with only local storms on the coast of Europe. The moon had just passed full and the percentage of days with fog over Newfoundland and the Grand Banks was increasing so that there seemed to be no advantage in waiting longer.

===Chinese===
5 月 19 日晨，天上下着小雨，天空阴沉。大圆航线沿线陆上台站和船舶发来的天气报告都不利，至少几天之内显然没有飞往巴黎的希望。上午我参观了新泽西州帕特森（Paterson）的莱特工厂，本打算当晚去纽约看一场戏。可大约六点钟，我收到纽约气象局的一份特别报告：整个北大西洋为高压所控制，新斯科舍和纽芬兰上空的低压正在退去。显然，雾消云散的前景已是相当长一段时间里所能指望的最好情形了。北大西洋应当是晴朗的，只有欧洲沿岸有局部风暴。月亮刚过望，纽芬兰和大浅滩（Grand Banks）出现雾天的日数比例正在上升，再等下去似乎没有任何好处。

===Original===
We went to Curtiss Field as quickly as possible and made arrangements for the barograph to be sealed and installed, and for the plane to be serviced and checked.

===Chinese===
我们尽快赶到柯蒂斯机场，安排人封好并装上自记气压计，同时给飞机整备、检查。

===Original===
We decided partially to fill the fuel tanks in the hangar before towing the ship on a truck to Roosevelt Field, which adjoins Curtiss on the east, where the servicing would be completed.

===Chinese===
我们决定先在机库里给油箱加一部分油，再用卡车把飞机拖到罗斯福机场——它在柯蒂斯机场以东、与之紧邻——在那里完成全部整备。

===Original===
I left the responsibility for conditioning the plane in the hands of the men on the field while I went into the hotel for about two and one-half hours of rest; but at the hotel there were several more details which had to be completed and I was unable to get any sleep that night.

===Chinese===
我把整备飞机的责任交给场上的人员，自己回旅馆去休息大约两个半小时；可到了旅馆还有几件细节必须办完，那一夜我一点儿也没能合眼。

===Original===
I returned to the field before daybreak on the morning of the twentieth. A light rain was falling which continued until almost dawn; consequently we did not move the ship to Roosevelt Field until much later than we had planned, and the takeoff was delayed from daybreak until nearly eight o'clock.

===Chinese===
二十日拂晓前，我回到机场。小雨一直下到将近天明；因此我们把飞机挪到罗斯福机场的时间比计划晚了许多，起飞也从黎明推迟到将近八点。

===Original===
At dawn the shower had passed, although the sky was overcast, and occasionally there would be some slight precipitation. The tail of the plane was lashed to a truck and escorted by a number of motorcycle police. The slow trip from Curtiss to Roosevelt was begun.

===Chinese===
黎明时分阵雨已经过去，虽然天仍阴着，偶尔还飘点零星小雨。飞机尾部拴在一辆卡车上，由一批摩托警察护送，从柯蒂斯到罗斯福的缓慢转运开始了。

===Original===
The ship was placed at the extreme west end of the field heading along the east and west runway, and the final fueling commenced.

===Chinese===
飞机被安放在机场最西端，机头对着东西向跑道，最后的加油开始了。

===Original===
About 7:40 a.m. the motor was started and at 7:52 I took off on the flight for Paris.

===Chinese===
上午 7 点 40 分左右，发动机启动；7 点 52 分，我起飞，踏上了飞往巴黎的航程。

===Original===
The field was a little soft due to the rain during the night and the heavily loaded plane gathered speed very slowly. After passing the halfway mark, however, it was apparent that I would be able to clear the obstructions at the end. I passed over a tractor by about fifteen feet and a telephone line by about twenty, with a fair reserve of flying speed. I believe that the ship would have taken off from a hard field with at least five hundred pounds more weight.

===Chinese===
夜里下过雨，场地有些松软，重载的飞机加速非常缓慢。不过一过半程标记，我就看得出能够越过跑道尽头的障碍物了。我以相当充裕的速度余量，从一台拖拉机上方约十五英尺、一条电话线上方约二十英尺处掠过。我相信，倘若是从坚硬场地上起飞，这架飞机还能再多载至少五百磅。

===Original===
I turned slightly to the right to avoid some high trees on a hill directly ahead, but by the time I had gone a few hundred yards I had sufficient altitude to clear all obstructions and throttled the engine down to 1,750 rpm. I took up a compass course at once and soon reached Long Island Sound where the Curtiss Oriole with its photographer, which had been escorting me, turned back.

===Chinese===
我稍稍右转，避开正前方一座山丘上的几棵大树；不过飞出几百码后，高度已足以越过一切障碍，我把发动机转速收到 1,750 转/分。我随即转入罗盘航向，很快到达长岛海峡（Long Island Sound），一直为我护航的那架载着摄影师的柯蒂斯"黄鹂"式在那里折返了。

===Original===
The haze soon cleared and from Cape Cod through the southern half of Nova Scotia the weather and visibility were excellent. I was flying very low, sometimes as close as ten feet from the trees and water.

===Chinese===
轻雾很快散去，从科德角到新斯科舍南半部，天气和能见度都极好。我飞得很低，有时离树木和水面只有十英尺。

===Original===
On the three hundred mile stretch of water between Cape Cod and Nova Scotia I passed within view of numerous fishing vessels.

===Chinese===
在科德角与新斯科舍之间三百英里的水面上，无数渔船进入我的视野。

===Original===
The northern part of Nova Scotia contained a number of storm areas and several times I flew through cloudbursts.

===Chinese===
新斯科舍北部有好几片风暴区，我数次穿越倾盆骤雨。

===Original===
As I neared the northern coast, snow appeared in patches on the ground and far to the eastward the coastline was covered with fog.

===Chinese===
临近北部海岸时，地面出现斑斑残雪，而远远的东方，海岸线被雾笼罩着。

===Original===
For many miles between Nova Scotia and Newfoundland the ocean was covered with caked ice but as I approached the coast the ice disappeared entirely and I saw several ships in this area.

===Chinese===
新斯科舍与纽芬兰之间的海面上，许多英里都覆着板结的浮冰；但一接近海岸，冰完全消失了，我在这片海域看见了几艘船。

===Original===
I had taken up a course for St. Johns, which is south of the great Circle from New York to Paris, so that there would be no question of the fact that I had passed Newfoundland in case I was forced down in the north Atlantic.

===Chinese===
我取的是飞往圣约翰斯（St. Johns）的航向，它在纽约—巴黎大圆航线以南——这样，万一我被迫降落在北大西洋，"已飞过纽芬兰"这一事实便无可置疑。

===Original===
I passed over numerous icebergs after leaving St. Johns, but saw no ships except near the coast.

===Chinese===
离开圣约翰斯后，我飞越了无数冰山，但除近岸处外没有看见一艘船。

===Original===
Darkness set in about 8:15 and a thin, low fog formed over the sea through which the white bergs showed up with surprising clearness. This fog became thicker and increased in height until within two hours I was just skimming the top of storm clouds at about ten thousand feet. Even at this altitude there was a thick haze through which only the stars directly overhead could be seen.

===Chinese===
大约 8 点 15 分夜幕降临，海面上腾起一层薄薄的低雾，白色的冰山在雾中反而显出惊人的清晰。雾越来越浓、越来越高，两小时之内，我已在一万英尺左右的高度贴着风暴云的顶飞行。即便在这个高度，仍有浓重的霾，只有头顶正上方的星星看得见。

===Original===
There was no moon and it was very dark. The tops of some of the storm clouds were several thousand feet above me and at one time, when I attempted to fly through one of the larger clouds, sleet started to collect on the plane and I was forced to turn around and get back into clear air immediately and then fly around any clouds which I could not get over.

===Chinese===
没有月亮，天非常黑。有些风暴云的云顶高出我几千英尺；有一次我试图从一朵较大的云中穿过去，飞机上开始积起冻雨，我被迫立即掉头回到净空之中，此后凡飞不过去的云就绕着飞。

===Original===
The moon appeared on the horizon after about two hours of darkness; then the flying was much less complicated.

===Chinese===
黑暗持续了大约两个小时后，月亮升上了地平线，飞行随之简单多了。

===Original===
Dawn came at about 1 a.m. New York time and the temperature had risen until there was practically no remaining danger of sleet.

===Chinese===
纽约时间约凌晨 1 点，天亮了。气温已经回升，结冻雨的危险实际上已不存在。

===Original===
Shortly after sunrise the clouds became more broken although some of them were far above me and it was often necessary to fly through them, navigating by instruments only.

===Chinese===
日出后不久，云层变得较为破碎，不过有些云仍远在我上方，常常必须穿云而过，全凭仪表导航。

===Original===
As the sun became higher, holes appeared in the fog. Through one the open water was visible, and I dropped down until less than a hundred feet above the waves. There was a strong wind blowing from the northwest and the ocean was covered with white caps.

===Chinese===
太阳升高后，雾中现出一个个空洞。从一个洞里看到了开阔的水面，我随即下降到离浪顶不足一百英尺的高度。西北风刮得很猛，海面上白浪滔滔。

===Original===
After a few miles of fairly clear weather the ceiling lowered to zero and for nearly two hours I flew entirely blind through the fog at an altitude of about 1,500 feet. Then the fog raised and the water was visible again.

===Chinese===
飞了几英里相当晴好的天气之后，云幕高度降到零，在约 1,500 英尺的高度上我全然盲飞，穿雾近两个小时。随后雾升高了，水面重新显露出来。

===Original===
On several more occasions it was necessary to fly by instrument for short periods; then the fog broke up into patches. These patches took on forms of every description. Numerous shorelines appeared, with trees perfectly outlined against the horizon. In fact, the mirages were so natural that, had I not been in mid-Atlantic and known that no land existed along my route, I would have taken them to be actual islands.

===Chinese===
又有几次不得不短暂改飞仪表；随后雾碎成一片一片。这些雾片形态千奇百怪，无数条海岸线浮现出来，树木衬着地平线轮廓分明。说实话，这些幻景逼真至极，倘若我不是身处大西洋中部、明知航线上没有任何陆地，准会把它们当成真正的岛屿。

===Original===
As the fog cleared I dropped down closer to the water, sometimes flying within ten feet of the waves and seldom higher than two hundred.

===Chinese===
雾一散，我就下降得更贴近水面，有时在离浪十英尺以内飞行，很少高于二百英尺。

===Original===
There is a cushion of air close to the ground or water through which a plane flies with less effort than when at a higher altitude, and for hours at a time I took advantage of this factor.

===Chinese===
贴近地面或水面有一层气垫，飞机在其中飞行比在高空省力，我一连几个小时都在利用这个有利条件。

===Original===
Also, it was less difficult to determine the wind drift near the water. During the entire flight the wind was strong enough to produce white caps on the waves. When one of these formed, the foam would be blown off, showing the wind's direction and approximate velocity. This foam remained on the water long enough for me to obtain a general idea of my drift.

===Chinese===
另外，贴近水面时也较容易判断风致偏流。整个飞行途中，风力之强足以在浪尖掀起白沫。白帽浪一形成，泡沫便被吹离浪头，显出风向和大概的风速。这些泡沫在水面上留存的时间，足够我对自己的偏流形成一个大致的概念。

===Original===
During the day I saw a number of porpoises and a few birds but no ships, although I understand that two different boats reported me passing over.

===Chinese===
白天我看见了许多鼠海豚和几只鸟，却没有看见船——虽然我听说有两艘不同的船报告我从它们上空飞过。

===Original===
The first indication of my approach to the European Coast was a small fishing boat which I first noticed a few miles ahead and slightly to the south of my course. There were several of these fishing boats grouped within a few miles of each other.

===Chinese===
我逼近欧洲海岸的第一个迹象，是一条小小的渔船，最初出现在前方几英里、航线路偏南的地方。这样的渔船有好几条，彼此相距不过几英里，聚在一处。

===Original===
I flew over the first boat without seeing any signs of life. As I circled over the second, however, a man's face appeared, looking out of the cabin window.

===Chinese===
我飞过第一条船，没有见到任何人的迹象。可当我绕着第二条船盘旋时，一张人脸出现了，正从舱窗里向外张望。

===Original===
I have carried on short conversations with people on the ground by flying low with throttled engine, shouting a question, and receiving the answer by some signal. When I saw this fisherman I decided to try to get him to point towards land. I had no sooner made the decision than the futility of the effort became apparent. In all likelihood he could not speak English, and even if he could he would undoubtedly be far too astounded to answer. However, I circled again and closing the throttle as the plane passed within a few feet of the boat I shouted, "Which way is Ireland?" Of course the attempt was useless, and I continued on my course.

===Chinese===
我以前曾用这样的办法与地上的人作过简短的交谈：收小油门低飞，喊出问题，再靠某种信号收到回答。见到这个渔夫时，我决定试试让他指一指陆地的方向。可这个念头刚起，其徒劳便显而易见了。十有八九他不会讲英语；即便会，也必定惊得目瞪口呆，无从答起。不过我还是又绕了一圈，当飞机在离船几英尺处掠过时，我收了油门，喊道："爱尔兰在哪个方向？"这番尝试当然毫无用处，我只好继续赶路。

===Original===
Less than an hour later a rugged and semi-mountainous coastline appeared to the northeast. I was flying less than two hundred feet from the water when I sighted it. The shore was fairly distinct and not over ten or fifteen miles away. A light haze coupled with numerous local storm areas had prevented my seeing it from a long distance.

===Chinese===
不到一小时之后，一条嶙峋而半多山的海岸线出现在东北方。望见它时，我正飞在离水面不足二百英尺的高度。海岸相当清晰，距离不过十或十五英里。一层薄霾加上多处局部风暴区，使我无法从远处望见它。

===Original===
The coastline came down from the north, curved over towards the east. I had very little doubt that it was the southwestern end of Ireland but in order to make sure I changed my course towards the nearest point of land.

===Chinese===
海岸线从北方延伸下来，向东弯去。我几乎不怀疑它就是爱尔兰的西南端，但为了确证，我朝最近的陆地点改了航向。

===Original===
I located Cape Valentia and Dingle Bay, then resumed my compass course towards Paris.

===Chinese===
我辨认出瓦伦西亚角（Cape Valentia）和丁格尔湾（Dingle Bay），随后恢复飞往巴黎的罗盘航向。

===Original===
After leaving Ireland I passed a number of steamers and was seldom out of sight of a ship.

===Chinese===
离开爱尔兰后，我飞越过许多艘轮船，很少有看不见船的时候。

===Original===
In a little over two hours the coast of England appeared. My course passed over Southern England and a little south of Plymouth; then across the English Channel, striking France over Cherbourg.

===Chinese===
两个多小时后，英格兰的海岸出现了。我的航线飞越英格兰南部，在普利茅斯（Plymouth）略偏南处过海；随后横穿英吉利海峡，在瑟堡（Cherbourg）一带进入法国。

===Original===
The English farms were very impressive from the air in contrast to ours in America. They appeared extremely small and unusually neat and tidy with their stone and hedge fences.

===Chinese===
与我们在美国的农场相比，英国的农场从空中看去令人印象深刻。它们显得极小，却又异常整洁，石砌的围篱和树篱齐齐整整。

===Original===
I was flying at about a fifteen hundred foot altitude over England and as I crossed the Channel and passed over Cherbourg, France, I had probably seen more of that part of Europe than many native Europeans. The visibility was good and the country could be seen for miles around.

===Chinese===
我以约一千五百英尺的高度飞越英格兰；等我渡过海峡、飞过法国瑟堡上空时，我对欧洲这一带的见识，恐怕已超过许多土生土长的欧洲人。能见度很好，四野之景尽收眼底，绵延数英里。

===Original===
People who have taken their first flight often remark that no one knows what the locality he lives in is like until he has seen it from above. Countries take on different characteristics from the air.

===Chinese===
初次飞行的人常常感叹：不从空中看一眼，谁也不知道自己住的地方究竟是什么模样。国家也从空中显出别样的面貌。

===Original===
The sun went down shortly after passing Cherbourg and soon the beacons along the Paris-London airway became visible.

===Chinese===
飞过瑟堡不久，太阳便落了，很快，巴黎—伦敦航线沿线的信标灯亮了起来，清晰可见。

===Original===
I first saw the lights of Paris a little before ten p.m., or five p.m. New York time, and a few minutes later I was circling the Eiffel Tower at an altitude of about four thousand feet.

===Chinese===
晚上将近十点、即纽约时间下午五点，我第一次望见巴黎的灯火；几分钟后，我在约四千英尺的高度绕着埃菲尔铁塔盘旋。

===Original===
The lights of Le Bourget were plainly visible, but appeared to be very close to Paris. I had understood that the field was farther from the city, so continued out to the northeast into the country for four or five miles to make sure that there was not another field farther out which might be Le Bourget. Then I returned and spiralled down closer to the lights. Presently I could make out long lines of hangars, and the roads appeared to be jammed with cars.

===Chinese===
勒布尔热机场的灯火清晰可见，但看去离巴黎非常近。我原以为机场离城更远，于是继续朝东北飞出城郊四五英里，确认更远处没有另一个可能是勒布尔热的机场。然后我折回来，朝灯光盘旋下降。不一会儿，我便认出一排排长长的机库，公路上看去堵满了汽车。

===Original===
I flew low over the field once, then circled around into the wind and landed.

===Chinese===
我在机场上空低掠了一遭，然后绕到迎风方向，降落到地面。

===Original===
After the plane stopped rolling I turned it around and started to taxi back to the lights. The entire field ahead, however, was covered with thousands of people all running towards my ship. When the first few arrived, I attempted to get them to hold the rest of the crowd back, away from the plane, but apparently no one could understand, or would have been able to conform to my request if he had.

===Chinese===
飞机停稳后，我把它转过头，开始朝灯光方向滑行。然而前方整个机场都布满了人，成千上万，全都朝我的飞机跑来。最先赶到的那几个人一到，我就试图让他们拦住后面的人群，让他们离飞机远些，但显然谁也听不懂；即便听得懂，也没人办得到。

===Original===
I cut the switch to keep the propeller from killing someone, and attempted to organize an impromptu guard for the plane. The impossibility of any immediate organization became apparent, and when parts of the ship began to crack from the pressure of the multitude I decided to climb out of the cockpit in order to draw the crowd away.

===Chinese===
我切断了电门，免得螺旋桨伤人性命，又试图临时组织一支飞机护卫队。当场组织之不可能旋即显露：人群挤压之下，机身部件开始噼啪开裂，我决定爬出座舱，把人群引开。

===Original===
Speaking was impossible; no words could be heard in the uproar and nobody apparently cared to hear any. I started to climb out of the cockpit, but as soon as one foot appeared through the door I was dragged the rest of the way without assistance on my part.

===Chinese===
说话是不可能的；喧嚣之中什么话也听不见，而且显然也没人想听。我开始往座舱外爬，可我的脚刚从舱门口露出来，就身不由己地被拽了出去。

===Original===
For nearly half an hour I was unable to touch the ground, during which time I was ardently carried around in what seemed to be a very small area, and in every position it is possible to be in. Everyone had the best of intentions but no one seemed to know just what they were.

===Chinese===
将近半个小时，我的脚挨不着地面。这段时间里，我被人热切地举着，在一片看来很小的地域内转来转去，凡是可以想象的姿势都被摆了个遍。人人都怀着最大的善意，只是谁也说不清那善意究竟是什么。

===Original===
The French military flyers very resourcefully took the situation in hand. A number of them mingled with the crowd; then, at a given signal, they placed my helmet on an American correspondent and cried: "Here is Lindbergh." That helmet on an American was sufficient evidence. The correspondent immediately became the center of attraction, and while he was being taken protestingly to the Reception Committee via a rather devious route, I managed to get inside one of the hangars.

===Chinese===
法国军方飞行员们机敏果断地控制了局面。他们中的几个人混入人群，然后按约定的信号，把我的飞行帽扣在一名美国记者头上，高喊："林德伯格在这里！"飞行帽扣在美国人头上，便是铁证。那位记者立刻成了万众瞩目的人物，正当他一路抗议着、被绕着弯儿送往接待委员会时，我设法钻进了一座机库。

===Original===
Meanwhile a second group of soldiers and police had surrounded the plane and soon placed it out of danger in another hangar.

===Chinese===
与此同时，另一队士兵和警察围住了飞机，很快把它送进另一座机库，脱离了险境。

===Original===
The French ability to handle an unusual situation with speed and capability was remarkably demonstrated that night at Le Bourget.

===Chinese===
法国人处置非常事变的迅速与干练，那一夜在勒布尔热得到了非凡的展现。

===Original===
Ambassador Herrick extended me an invitation to remain at his Embassy while I was in Paris, which I gladly accepted. But grateful as I was at the time, it did not take me long to realize that a kind Providence had placed me in Ambassador Herrick's hands. The ensuing days found me in situations that I had certainly never expected to be in and in which I relied on Ambassador Herrick's sympathetic aid.

===Chinese===
赫里克大使邀我在巴黎期间下榻大使馆，我欣然接受。尽管当时我感激不已，但没过多久我便明白：是仁慈的天意把我交到了赫里克大使的手中。随后数日，我陷入了自己绝未曾料到的种种境地，全靠赫里克大使体恤相助。

===Original===
These situations were brought about by the wholehearted welcome to me—an American—that touched me beyond any point that any words can express. I left France with a debt of gratitude which, though I cannot repay it, I shall always remember. If the French people had been acclaiming their own gallant airmen, Nungesser and Coli, who were lost only after fearlessly departing in the face of conditions insurmountably greater than those that confronted me, their enthusiastic welcome and graciousness could not have been greater.

===Chinese===
这一切都源于法国人对一个美国人发自肺腑的欢迎——那份情意之深，任何言语都无法表达于万一。我离开法国时欠下了一笔感激的债，虽无力偿还，却将铭记终身。倘若法国人欢呼的是他们自己英勇的飞行员——农热塞尔与科利，两人面对比我艰难得不可逾越的境况，义无反顾地起飞，随后捐躯大洋——他们热情的欢迎与深挚的礼遇，也不可能更隆重了。

===Original===
In Belgium as well, I was received with a warmth which reflected more than simply a passing curiosity in a transatlantic flight, but which was rather a demonstration by the people of their interest in a new means of transportation which eventually would bring still closer together the new world and the old. Their welcome, too, will be a cherished memory for all time.

===Chinese===
在比利时，我同样受到热忱的接待。那份热忱所反映的，不只是对一次跨大西洋飞行的猎奇，更是人民对一种新型交通方式的关切——它终将把新大陆与旧大陆拉得更近。他们的欢迎，也将是我永生珍视的记忆。

===Original===
In England, I experienced one final unforgettable demonstration of friendship for an American. That spontaneous wonderful reception during my brief visit seemed typical of what I had always heard of the good sportsmanship of the English.

===Chinese===
在英格兰，我经历了对一个美国人的最后一场难忘的友谊盛典。短短访问期间那自发而美妙的接待，恰如我一向听闻的英国人的堂堂君子之风。

===Original===
My words to all those friends in Europe are inadequate, but my feelings of appreciation are boundless.

===Chinese===
对欧洲所有这些朋友，我的言辞是贫乏的，但我的感激之情无边无际。

===Original===
Conclusion

===Chinese===
结论

===Original===
When I was contemplating the flight to Paris I looked forward to making a short tour of Europe with especial regard to the various airports and aeronautical activities.

===Chinese===
还在筹划巴黎之行时，我便期待着在欧洲作一次短暂游历，特别想看看各地的机场和航空事业。

===Original===
After I arrived, however, the necessity for returning to America in the near future became apparent and, after a consultation with Ambassador Houghton, who informed me that President Coolidge was sending the cruiser *Memphis* to Cherbourg for my return journey to America, I flew the *Spirit of St. Louis* to Gosport early one morning. There it was dismantled and crated, through the courtesy of the Royal Air Force which also placed a Woodcock pursuit plane at my disposal.

===Chinese===
然而抵达之后，尽快返回美国的必要性日益明显。与霍顿大使（Ambassador Houghton）商议后——他告知我柯立芝总统正派"孟菲斯"号（Memphis）巡洋舰赴瑟堡接我回美——一天清晨，我驾驶"圣路易斯精神号"飞往戈斯波特（Gosport）。承蒙英国皇家空军（Royal Air Force）的善意，飞机在那里被拆卸装箱，他们还拨给我一架"丘鹬"式（Woodcock）驱逐机使用。

===Original===
I returned to London in the Woodcock and a few days later flew to Paris in another R.A.F. machine of the same type.

===Chinese===
我驾着"丘鹬"式返回伦敦，几天后又驾驶皇家空军的另一架同型机飞往巴黎。

===Original===
I remained overnight in Paris, and early the next morning flew a French Breguet to Cherbourg where the cruiser *Memphis* was waiting.

===Chinese===
我在巴黎过了一夜，次日一早驾驶一架法国布雷盖机（Breguet）飞往瑟堡，"孟菲斯"号巡洋舰正在那里等候。

===Original===
Admiral Burrage met me at the dock, and after going aboard the *Memphis* I became acquainted with Captain Lackey and the officers of the ship. During the trip across they extended every courtesy and did everything within their power to make the voyage a pleasant one.

===Chinese===
伯拉奇海军上将（Admiral Burrage）到码头迎接我。登上"孟菲斯"号后，我结识了拉基舰长（Captain Lackey）和舰上的军官们。横渡大西洋的一路上，他们礼数周全，竭尽所能让这次航行愉快舒适。

===Original===
A description of my welcome back to the United States would, in itself, be sufficient to fill a larger volume than this. I am not an author by profession, and my pen could never express the gratitude which I feel towards the American people.

===Chinese===
单是描写我回到美国所受的欢迎，就足以写满一部比这本书更厚的书。我并非职业作家，我的笔永远写不尽我对美国人民的感激。

===Original===
The voyage up the Potomac and to the Monument Grounds in Washington; up the Hudson River and along Broadway; over the Mississippi and to St. Louis—to do justice to these occasions would require a far greater writer than myself.

===Chinese===
溯波托马克河而上，抵达华盛顿的纪念碑广场；溯哈得孙河而上，沿百老汇一路行去；飞越密西西比河，来到圣路易斯——要为这些场面写下公允的记述，须得一位远比我高明的作家。

===Original===
Washington, New York, and finally St. Louis and home. Each of these cities has left me with an impression that I shall never forget, and a debt of gratitude which I can never repay.

===Chinese===
华盛顿、纽约，最后是圣路易斯和家。这几座城市留给我的印象终生难忘，欠下的情义我永远无法偿清。

## V New York / 五 纽约

===Original===
On Monday morning, June 13, Lindbergh rose at dawn and reached the Mayflower Hotel at 6:45 a.m. for breakfast with the National Aeronautical Association, which conferred a life membership upon him.

===Chinese===
6 月 13 日，星期一早晨，林德伯格黎明即起，于上午 6 点 45 分抵达五月花饭店（Mayflower Hotel），与全国航空协会（National Aeronautical Association）共进早餐，协会向他授予了终身会员资格。

===Original===
He reached Bolling Field outside Washington at about 7:30 a.m. Here rose the only incident to mar his otherwise flawless happiness in the welcome he had received. His plane refused to "mote." It didn't actually rebel. But there was sufficient irregularity in its engine to discourage him from risking delay when New York City was almost every minute voicing its impatience that he hurry to the celebration awaiting him there. A pursuit plane was quickly obtained from an army field and he was soon in the air with his escort of more than a score of ships.

===Chinese===
上午 7 点 30 分左右，他抵达华盛顿城外的博林机场（Bolling Field）。在他所受的近乎完美的欢迎接待中，唯一煞风景的小插曲就出在这里：他的飞机不肯"动弹"了。倒也没有真闹罢工，只是发动机的运转颇不规律，足以让他不敢冒险耽搁——纽约城几乎每分钟都在催促他，快去赴那场等着他的庆典。人们迅速从一处陆军机场调来一架驱逐机，他很快便升空了，身后跟着二十多架护航的飞机。

===Original===
The course of the group led them over Baltimore, Wilmington and Philadelphia. Eyewitnesses later reported that demonstrations took place at every one of these places as the air cavalcade went by. Of course those in the planes, thousands of feet in the air and deafened by the roar of their motors, heard nothing of the bells and whistles that saluted them as they passed.

===Chinese===
机群的航线掠过巴尔的摩（Baltimore）、威尔明顿（Wilmington）和费城（Philadelphia）。目击者后来报告说，空中车队飞临的每一处都出现了欢庆场面。当然，机上的人身处几千英尺高空，被发动机的轰鸣震得耳聋，对掠过时向他们致意的钟声与汽笛声一无所闻。

===Original===
Lindbergh arrived at Mitchel Field about noon. As he had flown in a land plane and was to be met in the lower harbor by the mayor's yacht, he had to make a quick change to an amphibian. This ship happened to be the *San Francisco* which had but recently returned from her "goodwill" flight to South America.

===Chinese===
中午时分，林德伯格抵达米切尔机场（Mitchel Field）。由于他驾驶的是陆上飞机，而市长游艇将在下港迎接，他得赶紧换上一架水陆两栖机。这架飞机恰是"旧金山"号（San Francisco），它不久前刚完成赴南美的"亲善"飞行归来。

===Original===
She took off from dry land and a few minutes later volplaned down to the water just above the Narrows.

===Chinese===
它从陆地上起飞，几分钟后滑翔降到纳罗斯水道（the Narrows）上方的水面。

===Original===
Here a sight met Lindbergh's eyes that old harbor inhabitants declare was absolutely without precedent in the marine annals of New York. Even the famous Hudson-Fulton Exposition with its vast water parades and maneuvers was exceeded.

===Chinese===
在这里，一幅景象映入林德伯格的眼帘——港口的老住户们断言，这在整个纽约的航海编年史上绝无先例。连以宏大的水上游行与操演著称的著名哈得孙—富尔顿博览会（Hudson-Fulton Exposition），也被它盖过了。

===Original===
In the sparkling sunshine of a perfect June morning was gathered half a thousand vessels of every kind and description. Excursion boats, yachts, tugs, motor boats, launches, fireboats, even dredges, formed the spectacular array of shipping gathered to meet the man who had made the proudest of surface craft, the ocean liner, a back number on the sea.

===Chinese===
在一个完美的六月清晨的粼粼日光下，聚集了近五百艘形形色色的船只。游览船、游艇、拖轮、摩托艇、汽艇、消防船，甚至挖泥船，构成了蔚为壮观的水上阵列，前来迎接这个人——他使海面船舶中最骄贵的远洋邮轮，沦为了海上的明日黄花。

===Original===
A police launch swung up to the *San Francisco* and took Lindbergh aboard. He was brought to the *Macom*, yacht of the Mayor of New York, amid a deafening chorus of whistles. Indeed, so great was the din that conversation among the welcoming committees was quite impossible and remained so throughout the hour's voyage to the Battery.

===Chinese===
一艘警察汽艇靠上"旧金山"号，把林德伯格接了过去。他被送到纽约市市长的游艇"梅科姆"号（Macom）上，震耳欲聋的汽笛声四起。的确，喧声之大，欢迎委员会成员间的交谈全然无从进行；前往巴特里（the Battery）的一小时航程中，始终如此。

===Original===
As the *Macom* moved forward the huge disorderly fleet of crowding vessels swung into rough column behind her. Massive ocean going tugs and fireboats clung close aboard to guard her from too curious craft who sought to wedge their way in toward the yacht for a better look at the bareheaded boy standing atop her pilot house.

===Chinese===
"梅科姆"号前行时，那一大群拥挤杂乱的船只随即在它身后排成松散的纵队。远洋拖轮和消防船紧贴两旁护航，挡住那些太好奇的船只——它们总想挤到游艇近旁，好看清站在驾驶室顶上那个光着头的小伙子。

===Original===
As in Washington, the air was well filled with planes. Their motors' roar lent a sort of solemn undertone to the shrieking chorus of whistles and sirens.

===Chinese===
同华盛顿一样，空中布满了飞机。发动机的轰鸣，为汽笛与警报器的尖啸大合唱添了一层庄重的低音。

===Original===
There was an interview below decks. It was not very successful. The whistles made too much noise and Lindbergh very properly refused to discuss his "feelings," which are meat and drink to the writing man.

===Chinese===
甲板下进行了一场采访，不算太成功。汽笛吵得厉害，而林德伯格又一本正经地拒绝谈论自己的"感受"——那可是摇笔杆的人赖以为生的东西。

===Original===
It was estimated that 300,000 people were massed in the vicinity of the Battery when the *Macom* hove alongside. Lining the streets clear to Central Park was a multitude that was variously estimated from 3,000,000 to 4,500,000. Scores of people were in their places before eight a.m. on upper Fifth Avenue. Lindbergh did not pass them until three p.m. Traffic was disrupted. Police control was strained to its utmost.

===Chinese===
据估计，"梅科姆"号靠上巴特里时，那一带集结了 300,000 人。从街头一直排到中央公园（Central Park）的人海，各种估计从 300 万到 450 万不等。成群结队的人在上午八点以前就占好了第五大道上段的位置，直到下午三点林德伯格才从他们面前经过。交通瘫痪，警察的调度已绷到了极限。

===Original===
As evidence of the almost unanimous turnout for the occasion, the Police Department of the City issued special instructions to all citizens about leaving their houses protected against thieves, something that hadn't been done for a generation.

===Chinese===
作为这次几乎倾城而出的佐证，市警察局向全体市民发出特别通告，叮嘱大家出门时锁好房屋、谨防盗贼——这样的事，一代人以来还是头一回。

===Original===
When the cavalcade with Lindbergh leading started up Broadway there came the famous New York "snow storm" consisting of a myriad paper bits and confetti streamers floating downward from the skyscrapers. Photographs do scant justice to the spectacle.

===Chinese===
林德伯格领头的车队驶上百老汇时，著名的纽约"暴风雪"从天而降——无数纸屑与彩带从摩天大楼上纷纷飘落。照片根本不足以传达这一奇观。

===Original===
At the City Hall Mayor Walker expressed the city's sentiments with a felicity that deserves their record here. He spoke more informally than most had spoken in Washington; by the same token he echoed through his easily forgivable eloquence much that the inarticulate thousands waiting without the lines would like to have said.

===Chinese===
在市政厅，沃克市长（Mayor Walker）把本市的心意表达得恰到好处，值得一录于此。他讲得比华盛顿的大多数演讲都随性；也正因如此，他那无伤大雅的雄辩，道出了警戒线外千千万万不善言辞的等候者想说而说不出的话。

===Original===
He struck right at the heart of things when he began:

===Chinese===
他开门见山，一语中的：

===Original===
"Let me dispense with any unnecessary official side or function, Colonel, by telling you that if you have prepared yourself with any letters of introduction to New York City they are not necessary.

===Chinese===
"上校，恕我免去一切不必要的官样礼节，直接告诉您：如果您备好了什么写给纽约市的介绍信，那大可不必了。

===Original===
"Everybody all over the world, in every language, has been telling you and the world about yourself. You have been told time after time where you were born, where you went to school, and that you have done the supernatural thing of an air flight from New York to Paris. I am satisfied that you have become convinced of it by this time.

===Chinese===
"全世界的人，用各种语言，已经把您本人向您和世人介绍了个遍。您出生在哪里、在哪里上学、您完成了从纽约到巴黎这一超乎凡人的飞行——这些话您已听过一遍又一遍。我确信，这会儿您自己都已经信了。

===Original===
"And it is not my purpose to reiterate any of the wonderful things that have been so beautifully spoken and written about you and your triumphal ride across the ocean. But while it has become almost axiomatic, it sometimes seems prosaic to refer to you as a great diplomat, because after your superhuman adventure, by your modesty, by your grace, by your gentlemanly American conduct, you have left no doubt of that. But the one thing that occurs to me that has been overlooked in all the observations that have been made of you is that you are a great grammarian, and that you have given added significance and a deeper definition to the word 'we.'

===Chinese===
"人们对您、对您跨越海洋的凯旋之行，已说过写说过那么多美妙的话，我不打算再重复了。称您为伟大的外交家几乎已成公理，有时甚至显得平淡无奇——因为在您的超人冒险之后，以您的谦逊、您的优雅、您绅士风度的美国做派，这一点已毋庸置疑。不过我想到，在人们对您的种种品评中，有一件事被忽略了：您还是一位伟大的语法学家，您给"我们"这个词增添了新的分量和更深切的定义。

===Original===
"We have heard, and we are familiar with, the editorial 'we,' but not until you arrived in Paris did we learn of the aeronautical 'we.' Now you have given to the world a flying pronoun.

===Chinese===
"社论王的那种"我们"，我们听过，也熟悉；可直到您抵达巴黎，我们才见识了航空的"我们"。如今，您献给世界一个会飞的代词。

===Original===
"That 'we' that you used was perhaps the only word that would have suited the occasion and the great accomplishment that was yours. That all-inclusive word 'we' was quite right, because you were not all alone in the solitude of the sky and the sea, because every American heart, from the Atlantic to the Pacific, was beating for you. Every American, every soul throughout the world, was riding with you in spirit, urging you on and cheering you on to the great accomplishment that is yours.

===Chinese===
"您所用的那个"我们"，也许是唯一配得上那个时刻、配得上您那桩伟大成就的词。那个无所不包的"我们"用得再对不过，因为在天空与大海的孤寂中，您并不孤单——从大西洋到太平洋，每一颗美国人的心都在为您跳动。每一个美国人、全世界的每一个灵魂，都在精神上与您同飞，激励您、鼓舞您，奔向属于您的伟大成就。

===Original===
"That 'we' was a vindication of the courage, of the intelligence, of the confidence and the hopes of Nungesser and Coli, now only alive in the prayers and the hearts of the people of the entire world. That 'we' that you coined was well used, because it gave an added significance and additional emphasis to the greatest of any and all ranks, the word of faith, and turned the hearts of all the people of the civilized world to your glorious mother, whose spirit was your spirit, whose confidence was your confidence, and whose pride was your pride; the 'we' that includes all that has made the entire world stand and gasp at your great feat, and that 'we' also sent out to the world another message and brought happiness to the people of America, and admiration and additional popularity for America and Americans by all the peoples of the European countries.

===Chinese===
"那个"我们"，是对农热塞尔与科利的勇气、智慧、信念与期望的正名——如今他们只活在全世界人民的祈祷与心中。您创造的这个词用得好极了：它为凌驾一切等级之上的那个词——信念——增添了分量与分量之外的重音；它把文明世界所有人的心，都引向了您光荣的母亲——她的精神就是您的精神，她的信心就是您的信心，她的骄傲就是您的骄傲。那个包容一切的"我们"，正是它令全世界为您的伟大功绩屏息惊叹；也正是那个"我们"，向世界发出了又一个讯息，给美国人民带来了幸福，也为美国和美国人赢得了欧洲各国人民的钦慕与亲近。

===Original===
"Colonel Lindbergh, on this very platform are the diplomatic corps, the diplomatic representatives of all the countries of the civilized world; but before you and around you are the peoples themselves of all the countries of the civilized world, foregathered in this city, the greatest cosmopolitan institution in all the world; the peoples who have come from the forty-eight States of the Union and from every country of the civilized world; and here today, as Chief Magistrate of this city, the world city, the gateway to America, the gateway through which peoples from the world have come in the search for liberty and freedom—and have found it—here today let it be written and let it be observed that the Chief Magistrate of this great city, the son of an immigrant, is here to welcome as the world's greatest hero, another son of an immigrant.

===Chinese===
"林德伯格上校，就在这座台上，坐着外交使团、文明世界各国的外交代表；而在您面前、在您四周的，是文明世界各国的民众本身，他们汇聚在这座城市——全世界最大的世界主义都会；他们来自合众国的四十八个州、来自文明世界的每一个国家。今天，作为这座城市的行政长官——这座世界之城、美国的大门，世界各地的人民从这里前来追寻自由——并且找到了自由——今天，就在这里，请让它载入史册、请让它昭然可见：这座伟大城市的行政长官，一个移民之子，正在迎接作为世界最伟大英雄的另一个移民之子。

===Original===
"What more need I call to your attention, in view of the busy life that you have been leading and have the right to expect to lead? What more can we say as we foregather in the streets of this old city? And today, not by the words alone of the Mayor, or the beautifully written words of a scroll, as you stand here I am sure you hear something even more eloquent and glorious. You can hear the heartbeats of six millions of people that live in this the City of New York. And the story they tell is one of pride, is one of admiration for courage and intelligence; is one that has been born out of and is predicated upon the fact that as you went over the ocean you inscribed on the heavens themselves a beautiful rainbow of hope and courage and confidence in mankind.

===Chinese===
"鉴于您一路奔忙、而且完全有权继续这样奔忙下去，我还需要向您多说什么呢？当我们相聚在这座老城的街头，我们还能说些什么呢？今天，不止是市长的言辞，也不止是卷轴上漂亮的文句——您站在这里，我相信您听见了更雄辩、更壮丽的声音。您听得见生活在纽约这座城里的六百万人的心跳。那心跳诉说的是自豪，是对勇气与智慧的钦佩；它源出于这样一个事实，也立足于这样一个事实——当您飞越大洋时，您在苍穹之上描绘出一道美丽的彩虹，那是希望、勇气与对人类的信心的彩虹。

===Original===
"Colonel Lindbergh, New York City is yours—I don't give it to you; you won it. New York not only wants me to tell you of the love and appreciation that it has for your great venture, but is deeply and profoundly grateful for the fact that again you have controverted all the old rules and made new ones of your own, and kind of cast aside temporarily even the weather prophets, and have given us a beautiful day.

===Chinese===
"林德伯格上校，纽约城是您的了——不是我送给您，是您赢来的。纽约不仅要我把对您伟大壮举的热爱与敬意转告给您，还由衷地深深感激您：您又一次推翻了所有旧规则，自立了新规则，连天气预报员都被您暂时撇在了一边——您给了我们一个明媚的日子。

===Original===
"So, just another word of the happiness, the distinction and the pride which the City of New York has today to find you outside this historical building, sitting side by side with your glorious mother, happy to find you both here, that we might have the opportunity and a closeup, to tell you that like the rest of the world—but because we are so much of the world, even with a little greater enthusiasm than you might find in any other place in the world—I congratulate you and welcome you into the world city, that you may look the world in the face."

===Chinese===
"最后再说说纽约市今日的欢喜、荣光与自豪：在这座历史性的建筑之外，见您与您光荣的母亲并肩而坐，得见你们二位在此，我们何其欣慰——让我们有机会近距离地告诉您：和全世界一样——但正因为我们本身就是世界的重要一角，我们的热情甚至比您在世界任何别处所能见到的还要更胜一筹——我祝贺您，欢迎您来到这座世界之城，从此您可以坦然直面整个世界。"

===Original===
Mayor Walker pinned the Medal of Valor upon the lapel of Lindbergh's coat. Whereupon Lindbergh for the first time gave in some detail his sense of the size of the welcome he had received:

===Chinese===
沃克市长把英勇勋章（Medal of Valor）别在林德伯格的衣领上。于是林德伯格第一次比较具体地谈了谈他对所受欢迎规模的感受：

===Original===
"When I was preparing to leave New York, I was warned that if we landed at Le Bourget we might receive a rather demonstrative reception. After having an hour of Le Bourget I did not believe that anyone in New York had the slightest conception of what we did receive. Again, at Brussels and at London. At London thirteen hundred of the pride of Scotland Yard were lost in the crowd at Croydon as though they had been dropped in the middle of the ocean. With the exception of a few around the car and around the plane, I never saw more than two at any one time.

===Chinese===
"我准备离开纽约时，有人提醒过我：假如我们在勒布尔热降落，可能会受到相当热烈的接待。在勒布尔热待了一个小时之后，我就不再相信纽约有谁能对实际场面有丝毫概念了。布鲁塞尔和伦敦，又是如此。在伦敦，一千三百名苏格兰场的精英在克罗伊登的人群里被冲得七零八落，就像掉进了大海中央。除了一小圈守在汽车和飞机旁的，我一次最多只见到过两名警察。

===Original===
"At Washington I received a marvelous reception. But at New York I believe that all four put together would be in about just the position of those London bobbies.

===Chinese===
"在华盛顿，我受到了极盛大的接待。可在纽约，我想前四场加在一起，恐怕也就像那帮伦敦警察一样招架不住。

===Original===
"When I landed at Le Bourget I landed looking forward to the pleasure of seeing Europe and the British Isles. I learned to speak of Europe and the British Isles after I landed in London. I had been away from America a little less than two days. I had been very interested in the things I saw while passing over southern England and France, and I was not in any hurry to get back home.

===Chinese===
"我在勒布尔热降落时，满心期待着游览欧洲与不列颠群岛的乐趣。到了伦敦我才学会把欧洲与不列颠群岛并称。那时我离开美国还不到两天，飞越英格兰南部和法国时看到的一切让我兴致盎然，一点也不急着回家。

===Original===
"By the time I had spent about a week in France and a short time in Belgium and England, and had opened a few cables from the United States, I found that I did not have much to say about how long I would stay over there."

===Chinese===
"等我在法国待了约一周、在比利时和英格兰待了短短几天，又拆了几封美国来的电报之后，我发现自己对要在那边待多久，已经说不上有什么发言权了。"

===Original===
Lindbergh paused for the laughter to subside. This point always tickled people greatly.

===Chinese===
林德伯格停了停，等笑声平息。这个梗总是让人们乐不可支。

===Original===
"So I left Europe and the British Isles with the regret that I had been unable to see either Europe or the British Isles. When I started up the Potomac from the *Memphis* I decided that I was not so sorry that I had taken the Ambassador's advice. After spending about an hour in New York I know I am not."

===Chinese===
"就这样，我怀着未能好好看看欧洲、也未能好好看看不列颠群岛的遗憾，离开了欧洲和不列颠群岛。当'孟菲斯'号载着我溯波托马克河而上时，我已经觉得听了大使的劝告并不怎么后悔。在纽约待了大约一小时之后，我知道自己确实不后悔。"

===Original===
The parade now formed again and moved up Broadway, through Lafayette Street, to Ninth and over to Fifth. At Madison Square it halted at the Shaft of Eternal Light. The ceremony was touching and impressive. The tall shaft topped by a crystal star, imprisoning light everlasting, was a fitting memorial to the men who gave up their lives in the World War. Lindbergh here laid a wreath in their memory.

===Chinese===
游行队伍重新集合，驶上百老汇，穿过拉法耶特街（Lafayette Street），到九街又折向五大道。在麦迪逊广场（Madison Square），队伍在"永恒之光碑"（Shaft of Eternal Light）前停下。仪式感人而隆重。高耸的碑柱顶着一颗水晶之星，锁住了永恒的光，是对世界大战中捐躯者恰如其分的纪念。林德伯格在此敬献花圈，追念亡者。

===Original===
Fifth Avenue had been packed with people since morning. It was now mid-afternoon. As in Washington a wave of cheering marked the progress of the car which held the city's guest of honor.

===Chinese===
第五大道从清晨起就挤满了人。此时已是午后过半。同华盛顿一样，载着本市主宾的汽车走到哪里，欢呼声的浪潮便涌到哪里。

===Original===
At St. Patrick's Cathedral he stopped, got out of his automobile and met Cardinal Hayes.

===Chinese===
在圣帕特里克大教堂（St. Patrick's Cathedral），他停下车，走出汽车，会见了海斯枢机主教（Cardinal Hayes）。

===Original===
In Central Park the official city welcome ended amid a gathering estimated at above 300,000 people. Bands were playing and automobile horns added to the din.

===Chinese===
在中央公园，该市官方的欢迎仪式在估计超过 300,000 人的集会中落幕。乐队齐奏，汽车喇叭也为这鼎沸之声添彩。

===Original===
Governor Smith of New York was waiting there with his staff on a specially built reviewing stand. He pinned on Lindbergh the State Medal of Honor: adding again to the ever lengthening list of honors. There was again an exchange of speeches met by salvos of applause. A sky writer wrote "Hail Lindy" high in the air. Policemen wrestled with swaying crowds. More than on the avenue it seemed as if the city were concentrated for a Lindbergh it would never forget.

===Chinese===
纽约州州长史密斯（Governor Smith）率属员在特建的检阅台上等候。他把州荣誉勋章（State Medal of Honor）别上林德伯格的胸前，又一枚荣誉添进了那份越列越长的清单。又是几番演讲对答，掌声如连珠炮般响起。一架空中写字机在高空写出"致敬，林迪"（Hail Lindy）。警察在人潮的推涌中奋力支撑。比起大道上，此刻整座城市仿佛更是为一个林德伯格而凝神屏息——一个它永远不会忘记的林德伯格。

===Original===
Near five the great demonstration came to an end. For a few hours the center of attraction could escape to the refuge that had been prepared for him and his mother in a private apartment. But this escape was qualified by the fact that it took a large guard to hold in check the many people who sought access to Lindbergh for one reason or another.

===Chinese===
将近五点，这场盛大的庆典落下帷幕。有几个小时，这位万众瞩目的中心人物得以退避到为他与母亲备好的一处私人公寓里栖身。但这退避是有条件的：得有一大队警卫拦住形形色色想接近林德伯格的人。

===Original===
At 8:15 p.m. he rode out on Long Island to the beautiful estate of Clarence Mackay, head of the Postal Telegraph Company. The place had been transformed into a fairyland of colored Japanese lanterns, fountains and illuminated shrubbery. Eighty of New York's most prominent people attended the dinner which was kingly in its appointments. Later several hundred guests came in for dancing.

===Chinese===
晚上 8 点 15 分，他乘车出长岛，前往邮政电报公司（Postal Telegraph Company）首脑克拉伦斯·麦凯（Clarence Mackay）的美丽庄园。那里已化作一处仙境：五彩的日本灯笼、喷泉和灯光映照的灌木。纽约最显赫的八十位人士出席了这场排场近乎帝王的晚宴。随后又有数百位宾客前来共舞。

===Original===
It would have seemed that this first terrific day might have exhausted the ardor of the city's welcome. But there followed a kaleidoscopic week that was, if anything, more trying. Not only did Lindbergh move amid a growing chorus of business offers, but his social engagements jammed tighter and tighter as the hours passed. Moreover, his plane was still in Washington, although he was scheduled to fly it to St. Louis for the weekend.

===Chinese===
照理说，这骇人的第一天总该耗尽全城欢迎的热情了。可接踵而来的是万花筒般的一周，非但不轻松，反而更折磨人。林德伯格不仅置身于日益高涨的商业开价大合唱之中，社交日程也随时间推移越排越满。何况他的飞机还在华盛顿——而按计划，周末他要驾它飞往圣路易斯。

===Original===
The City of New York gave Lindbergh a dinner of some 4,000 guests at the Hotel Commodore. It was there that Mr. Hughes spoke the following unique tribute:

===Chinese===
纽约市在科莫多饭店（Hotel Commodore）为林德伯格设宴，宾客约 4,000 人。正是在那里，休斯先生（Mr. Hughes）致了下面这篇独具一格的颂词：

===Original===
"When a young man, slim and silent, can hop overnight to Paris and then in the morning telephone his greetings to his mother in Detroit; when millions throughout the length and breadth of this land and over sea through the mysterious waves, which have been taught to obey our command, can listen to the voice of the President of the United States according honors for that achievement, honors which are but a faint reflection of the affection and esteem cherished in the hearts of the countryman of the West who distinguished America by that flight, then indeed is the day that hath no bother; then is the most marvelous day that this old earth has ever known.

===Chinese===
"当一个清瘦而沉默的青年能够一夜之间飞到巴黎，第二天早晨又能打电话向底特律的母亲问候；当这片土地上上下下、以及海外的大千民众，能够通过那些被教会服从人类意志的神秘电波，聆听合众国总统的声音、为那项成就授予荣誉——而这些荣誉不过是西部长者心底珍藏的敬爱之情的淡淡投影，正是那位西部之子以那次飞行使美国增辉——那么，这一天实在是再无烦忧的一天；那么，这就是苍茫大地所经历过的最不可思议的一天。

===Original===
"We measure heroes as we do ships by their displacement. Colonel Lindbergh has displaced everything. His displacement is beyond all calculation. He fills all our thought; he has displaced politics, Governor Smith.

===Chinese===
"我们衡量英雄，就像衡量船只，看它们的排水量。林德伯格上校排开了一切。他的排水量无法估量。他充盈了我们全部的思想；他连政治都排开了——我说的是您，史密斯州长。

===Original===
"For the time being, he has lifted us into the freer and upper air that is his home. He has displaced everything that is petty; that is sordid; that is vulgar. What is money in the presence of Charles A. Lindbergh?

===Chinese===
"眼下，他已把我们托举到他安身的、更自由的高空。他排开了一切琐碎的、卑污的、庸俗的东西。在查尔斯·A·林德伯格面前，金钱算得了什么？

===Original===
"What is the pleasure of the idler in the presence of this supreme victor of intelligence and industry? He has driven the sensation mongers out of the temples of our thought. He has kindled anew the fires on the eight ancient altars of that temple. Where are the stories of crime, of divorce, of the triangles that are never equalateral? For the moment we have forgotten. This is the happiest day, the happiest day of all days for America, and as one mind she is now intent upon the noblest and the best. America is picturing to herself youth with the highest aims, with courage unsurpassed; science victorious. Last and not least, motherhood, with her loveliest crown.

===Chinese===
"在这位智慧与勤勉的至高胜者面前，闲人的逸乐又算得了什么？他把耸人听闻的贩子赶出了我们思想的殿堂，又在那殿堂的八座古老祭坛上重新点燃了火焰。犯罪的新闻、离婚的花边、那些永远不等边的三角故事，都到哪里去了？此刻，我们已把它们忘却。这是最幸福的一天，是所有日子里美国最幸福的一天，她万众一心，正凝神于最高贵、最美好的事物。美国此刻在心中描绘的，是志向高远的青年，是无与伦比的勇气，是凯旋的科学；而最后同样重要的是——戴着最可爱冠冕的母性。

===Original===
"We may have brought peoples together. This flight may have been the messenger of goodwill, but goodwill for its beneficent effects depends upon the character of those who cherish it.

===Chinese===
"我们或许真的拉近了各国民众。这次飞行也许是一位亲善的使者，但亲善要结出善果，还取决于珍视它的人的品格。

===Original===
"We are all better men and women because of this exhibition in this flight of our young friend. Our boys and girls have before them a stirring, inspiring vision of real manhood. What a wonderful thing it is to live in a time when science and character join hands to lift up humanity with a vision of its own dignity.

===Chinese===
"因为我们这位年轻朋友此次飞行中的表现，我们所有人都成了更好的男人和女人。我们的儿女眼前，有了一幅激荡人心、催人奋发的真正男子汉的图景。生活在一个科学与品格携手、以人自身尊严的愿景提升人类的时代，是多么美妙的事情。

===Original===
"There is again revealed to us, with a startling suddenness, the inexhaustible resources of our national wealth. From an unspoiled home, with its traditions of industry, of frugality and honor, steps swiftly into our gaze this young man, showing us the unmeasured treasures in our minds of American character.

===Chinese===
"我们民族财富的取之不竭，又如此惊人地蓦然展现在我们眼前。这个青年从一座未染尘俗的家门里翩然走进我们的视野——那门户承袭着勤勉、节俭与荣誉的传统——他让我们看到了美式品格这座无可估量的心灵宝库。

===Original===
"America is fortunate in her heroes; her soul feeds upon their deeds; her imagination revels in their achievements. There are those who would rob them of something of their lustre, but no one can debunk Lindbergh, for there is no bunk about him. He represents to us, fellow-Americans, all that we wish—a young American at his best."

===Chinese===
"美国有幸拥有自己的英雄；她的灵魂以英雄的作为为食粮；她的想象在英雄的成就中尽情驰骋。总有人想剥去英雄身上的几分光彩，但谁也无法"戳穿"林德伯格，因为他身上没有一丝虚妄可供戳穿。同胞们，他代表着我们全部的期望——一个处于最佳状态的美国青年。"

===Original===
Only by reducing this record to catalog form could it possibly be made to include a fully detailed description of Lindbergh's four amazing days in New York. Every night there was a banquet. Every day there was a festive lunch. Not hundreds, but thousands attended these entertainments; and at the speaker's table there always sat distinguished men whose names were household words among Americans.

===Chinese===
只有把这份记录压缩成流水账的形式，才可能完整详尽地囊括林德伯格在纽约那惊人的四天。每晚必有宴会，每天必有庆典午宴。出席这些活动的不是成百，而是成千上万人；主讲席上总坐着名震遐迩的显赫人物，他们的名字在美国人家喻户晓。

===Original===
Lindbergh spoke at every banquet. Recurrently he paid gracious thanks to those who had helped make his visit such a gorgeous success; he usually ended by speaking on behalf of aviation, the welfare of which he never forgot even in the most crowded moments of his days.

===Chinese===
每场宴会上林德伯格都致辞。他反复向那些把他的访问装点得如此圆满的人们致以亲切的谢意；通常在结尾，他总要为航空事业说几句——即便在日程最满的时刻，他也从未忘记航空的福祉。

===Original===
The Merchants' Association gave him a gigantic luncheon. The Aeronautical Chamber of Commerce entertained him at a banquet that filled to overflowing the famous ball room of the Waldorf.

===Chinese===
商人协会（Merchants' Association）为他举办了盛大的午宴。航空商会（Aeronautical Chamber of Commerce）设宴款待他，宾客挤爆了华尔道夫饭店（Waldorf）著名的大宴会厅。

===Original===
On Wednesday night he gave an exhibition of his endurance that once more reminded the world it was fêting no ordinary hero. After dining on Rodman Wanamaker's yacht and seeing a special performance of a light opera, Lindbergh attended a charity benefit at one of the big theatres. About 1:30 a.m. he escaped through a back door and hurried to Mitchel field. Although still in his evening clothes he borrowed a helmet and hopped off for Washington at 3:05 a.m. By 7:30 a.m. he was back in New York with his own plane.

===Chinese===
星期三夜里，他上演了一场耐力秀，再次提醒世界：它款待的绝不是寻常英雄。在罗德曼·沃纳梅克（Rodman Wanamaker）的游艇上用完晚餐、看完一场轻歌剧专场之后，林德伯格又出席了一家大型剧院的慈善义演。凌晨 1 点 30 分左右，他从后门脱身，赶往米切尔机场。虽还穿着晚礼服，他借来一顶飞行帽，凌晨 3 点 05 分起飞前往华盛顿。到早晨 7 点 30 分，他已带着自己的飞机回到了纽约。

===Original===
His last day was too crowded for him to take a nap after his sleepless night. He went to Brooklyn where above a million people gave him another moving welcome. He kept a public luncheon date. He attended a large tea and reception at the Waldorf Hotel where Raymond Orteig presented him with the $25,000 prize that had long stood for the first flight from New York to Paris. At eight, a little tired but still as fresh looking as ever, he followed Charles Schwab in speaking before a massed aviation banquet that included many leading pilots of the world.

===Chinese===
不眠之夜后的最后一天，日程满得连打个盹的工夫都没有。他去了布鲁克林（Brooklyn），上百万人给了他又一次动人的欢迎。他如约出席了一场公众午宴，又在华尔道夫饭店参加了一场盛大的茶会与招待会——雷蒙德·奥泰格在那里把 25,000 美元奖金颁给了他，这笔奖金早已成为纽约—巴黎首飞的象征。八点钟，他略带倦容、却依旧神采奕奕，继查尔斯·施瓦布（Charles Schwab）之后，在一场群英荟萃的航空界盛大宴会上致辞——在座的包括世界上许多一流飞行员。

## I Paris / 一 巴黎

===Original===
Charles A. Lindbergh was the "dark horse" of the New York to Paris flight; also he flew alone. These two facts, combined with the tragic disappearance of the French transatlantic fliers, Nungesser and Coli, shortly before he left New York, emphasized the suspense with which Paris awaited his arrival.

===Chinese===
查尔斯·A·林德伯格是纽约—巴黎飞行中的一匹"黑马"，何况他是独自飞行。这两点，加上法国跨大西洋飞行员农热塞尔与科利在他离开纽约前不久悲剧性的失踪，更凸显了巴黎等待他到来时的那份悬念。

===Original===
He landed safely on a dark night about on schedule time. This was the culmination of what might be called the mechanical aspect of his success.

===Chinese===
他在一个漆黑的夜晚安全着陆，时间几乎分毫不差。这可以称作他成功中"机械层面"的顶点。

===Original===
In consequence of these unique but rather simple circumstances it was natural that there should follow a good deal of notoriety for the flier. Already the so-called "transatlantic Air Race" had received much advertising. Several planes had been grooming for the long flight; and there had been much speculation about the practicability of such an effort. Lindbergh's landing figuratively rang the bell as the winner came under the wire.

===Chinese===
由于这些独特而又相当简单的情况，这位飞行员随之声名大噪，实属自然。所谓"跨大西洋飞行竞赛"早已广为宣传，好几架飞机都在为这次长途飞行厉兵秣马，关于此举是否可行的猜测也众说纷纭。林德伯格的降落，形象地说，便是赛马夺冠冲线时铃响的一刻。

===Original===
The first man over was bound to be recognized as an audacious pioneer. Without regard for his character, creed or aspirations the world was going to come forward and say "Well done!"

===Chinese===
第一个飞过去的人，注定会被公认为胆识过人的先驱。世界会走上前来道一声"干得好！"——全然不管他的品性、信仰或志向如何。

===Original===
The first man to fly from New York to Paris was bound to be fêted and decorated. He would tell the story of his flight and there would be ephemeral discussion of its bearing on the future of aviation. Wild speculation about the world being on the brink of a great air age would follow.

===Chinese===
第一个从纽约飞到巴黎的人，注定要受宴请、获勋章。他会讲述自己飞行的故事，人们对这次飞行之于航空未来的意义会有短暂的讨论。随之而来的，是关于世界正站在伟大航空时代门槛上的狂热臆想。

===Original===
The first man to fly from New York to Paris was bound to excite the admiration of his own countrymen. He would be met on his return by committees, have to make some speeches at banquets and receive appropriate decorations for his valor.

===Chinese===
第一个从纽约飞到巴黎的人，注定会激起同胞的赞美。归国时会有各委员会相迎，得在宴会上讲几句话，并因他的英勇领受相应的勋章。

===Original===
The first man to fly from New York to Paris would write several magazine articles and a book. He might make some money by lecturing. He would be offered contracts for moving pictures, jobs as manager of something or other, and honorary memberships in a hundred organizations of more or less doubtful value.

===Chinese===
第一个从纽约飞到巴黎的人，会写几篇杂志文章和一本书。他或许能靠巡回演讲挣些钱。会有人向他提出电影合同、这样那样经理的职位，以及一百个价值多少有些可疑的组织的名誉会员资格。

===Original===
Then someone would break a homerun record or commit a murder; whereupon the world would forget with pitiless promptness the first man to fly the broad Atlantic.

===Chinese===
然后某个人会打破一项全垒打纪录，或者犯下一桩凶案；于是世界会以毫不留情的干脆，把第一个飞越浩瀚大西洋的人忘得一干二净。

===Original===
Who, by the way, can name the dauntless pilots that circled the globe by air not so many months ago?

===Chinese===
顺便问一句，谁能说出不过几个月前驾机环绕地球的那几位无畏飞行员的名字？

===Original===
The reason Lindbergh's story is different is that when his plane came to a halt on Le Bourget field that black night in Paris, Lindbergh the man kept on going.

===Chinese===
林德伯格的故事之所以不同，在于当他的飞机在巴黎那个漆黑之夜停落在勒布尔热机场之后，林德伯格这个人却没有停下脚步。

===Original===
The phenomenon of Lindbergh took its start with his flight across the ocean; but in its entirety it was almost as distinct from that flight as though he had never flown at all.

===Chinese===
"林德伯格现象"发端于他的越洋飞行；但就其全部而言，它与那次飞行几乎是两回事，仿佛他压根儿没有飞过一样。

===Original===
It is probable that in the three ensuing weeks Lindbergh loosed the greatest torrent of mass emotion ever witnessed in human history.

===Chinese===
很可能，在随后的三个星期里，林德伯格掀起了人类有史以来所见过的最大一场民众情感的洪流。

===Original===
This narrative is a record of events, not an analysis. It therefore cannot pretend to explain the "phenomenon of Lindbergh." Whether it was his modesty or his looks or his refusal to be tempted by money or by fame that won him such a following we cannot say. Perhaps the world was ripe for a youth with a winning smile to flash across its horizon and by the brilliance of his achievement momentarily to dim the ugliness of routine business, politics and crime. Many said that his sudden meteor-like appearance from obscurity was an act of Providence.

===Chinese===
这篇记叙是事件的实录，不是分析。因此它不敢妄称能解释"林德伯格现象"。究竟是他的谦逊、他的容貌，还是他不为金钱名位所动的品格，为他赢得了这样的拥戴，我们无从断言。也许这个世界正当其时，期待一个带着动人微笑的青年掠过它的天际，以他成就的光辉，一时遮蔽了日常商业、政治与罪行的丑陋。许多人说，他如流星般从无名之境骤然现身，乃是天意的安排。

===Original===
Whatever the reason for it all, the fact remains that there was a definite "phenomenon of Lindbergh" quite the like of which the world had never seen. This strange phenomenon is the opening fact of our simple narrative of events culled from a list far too long to include in the space allowed.

===Chinese===
无论这一切缘何而起，事实终归是：确曾有过一种"林德伯格现象"，世界从未见过它的同类。这个奇特的现象，是我们这篇朴实记事的第一个事实——诸般事件采撷自一份长得远非篇幅所能容纳的清单。

===Original===
All who followed press accounts of the flier's adventures after landing agree that his "meteor" did not glow in its full radiance at first. There was a faint but unmistakable artificiality in the news reports on this side of the Atlantic immediately following his arrival in Paris. To be sure, unstinted praise was poured on his courage and on the skill of his unprecedented flight. But the true Lindbergh had not yet impressed itself upon America.

===Chinese===
凡是追踪过报刊对这位飞行员落地后种种经历报道的人，都承认他的"流星"起初并未放出全部光芒。他抵达巴黎之后，大西洋这一岸紧接着出现的新闻报道里，有一种虽淡薄却分明可辨的做作。不错，人们把毫不吝惜的赞颂倾倒在他的勇气和他史无前例的飞行技艺上，但真正的林德伯格，还没有烙进美国的人心。

===Original===
His personality caught the French at the very moment when their natural enthusiasm for his deed was at its height. It was like pushing a swing just when it has started downward.

===Chinese===
他的人格魅力，恰好在法国人对他的壮举天然的热情达到顶点的那一刻扣住了他们。这就像秋千刚开始下摆时顺势一推。

===Original===
Two French aviation officers extricated him from the milling crowd at Le Bourget on arrival night and succeeded in getting him to the American Embassy where newspaper men located him at 1:30 a.m. The journalists naturally found the flier tired after having had practically no sleep for nearly sixty hours. But he was far from exhausted and he had no maudlin recital for the pencil-pushers who so eagerly surrounded him.

===Chinese===
抵达当晚，两名法国航空军官把他从勒布尔热乱作一团的人群中解救出来，成功将他送到美国大使馆，记者们凌晨 1 点 30 分在那里找到了他。将近六十个小时几乎没合眼，记者们自然发现这位飞行员一脸倦容。但他远没有到精疲力竭的地步，对那些急切围拢来的摇笔杆的人，他也没有感伤的冗长絮叨。

===Original===
He awakened near noon next day. After breakfast he went out on a balcony in response to crowds in the street and for the first time after his triumph stood face to face in daylight with citizens of France. There was a burst of applause. As we have said, the first man to have flown from New York to Paris, was bound to get just this applause. Then something else happened.

===Chinese===
次日临近中午他才醒来。早餐后，他应街上人群的呼唤走上阳台，胜利之后第一次在白昼与法兰西的公民面对面。掌声骤然响起。如前所说，第一个从纽约飞到巴黎的人，注定会赢得这样的掌声。接着，别的事情发生了。

===Original===
We talked to one of the Diplomatic Corps who witnessed this first public appearance. He said: "The people kept on cheering and clapping and waving their hats or handkerchiefs; but I suddenly had a feeling they were applauding mechanically, as if their attention were rooted on something that fascinated them.

===Chinese===
我们同一位目睹这首次公开露面的外交使团成员谈过。他说："人们不停地欢呼、鼓掌、挥舞帽子或手帕；可我突然有一种感觉：他们的喝彩是机械的，仿佛注意力扎根在某个令他们着迷的东西上。

===Original===
"I glanced up at Lindbergh to see if he were doing anything he shouldn't do. No, he was just smiling and his ruddy face was alight with appreciation.

===Chinese===
"我抬眼看了看林德伯格，瞧他是不是有什么不得体的举动。没有，他只是微笑着，红润的脸上洋溢着感激的光彩。

===Original===
"I looked from Lindbergh to the crowd. Then I realized that something was going on right before my eyes that I couldn't see. Lindbergh's personality was reaching out and winning the French just as surely as his flight had reached out and found their city."

===Chinese===
"我的目光从林德伯格移向人群。这时我才意识到，就在我眼前正进行着某种我看不见的东西。林德伯格的人格正在伸出手去赢得法国人，正如他的飞行曾经伸出手去、找到了他们的城市。"

===Original===
That was the beginning of the "phenomenon of Lindbergh." It grew in a steady crescendo as the days passed. We saw it full force in Washington. We saw it reach incredible heights in New York.

===Chinese===
那就是"林德伯格现象"的开端。随着日子一天天过去，它稳步增强，一浪高过一浪。我们在华盛顿看到了它全部的力量。我们在纽约看到它达到了难以置信的高度。

===Original===
Procession of events fitted into and abetted development of the situation. There was the telephone conversation from Paris to his mother in Detroit four thousand miles away. *His mother*: the world rolled the two words around its collective tongue as might a wine connoisseur his nectar.

===Chinese===
一连串事件环环相扣，推波助澜。有那通从巴黎打到四千英里外底特律、给他母亲的电话。「他的母亲」：世人把这两个字放在集体舌尖上细细品咂，好似品酒名家含啜玉液琼浆。

===Original===
He called on Madame Nungesser, another mother, whose equally brave son had disappeared but a few days before in the stormy wastes of the same ocean he had crossed. Their exchange was brief, but the whole world listened and wiped away a tear. In simple compassion Lindbergh told the mother not to give up hope. You have to know the boy to feel a fraction of the reassurance he must have conveyed.

===Chinese===
他拜望了农热塞尔夫人（Madame Nungesser）——又一位母亲，她同样英勇的儿子不过几天前失踪在他所飞越的同一片怒海之中。他们的交谈很简短，全世界却都在倾听，并拭去一滴眼泪。林德伯格怀着质朴的同情，叮嘱母亲不要放弃希望。你得认识这个小伙子，才能体会他必定传递给她的那份慰藉的万一。

===Original===
He visited the blind and crippled veterans of the great war. He smiled at them; which was enough for those who could see, who in turn ransacked their expressive tongue to explain "*le joli Lindbergh*" to those who couldn't.

===Chinese===
他看望了大战中失明和伤残的老兵。他对他们微笑；对看得见的人来说这已足够，他们又搜肠刮肚，用那富有表现力的法语向看不见的战友解释什么叫"漂亮的林德伯格"（le joli Lindbergh）。

===Original===
He called on the President of the Republic. He was dressed in plain clothes but the meeting was full of affability on both sides, with Sheldon Whitehouse of the Embassy acting as interpreter. The President pinned the Cross of the Legion of Honor upon the lapel of the boy's borrowed suit and kissed him on both cheeks.

===Chinese===
他拜会了法兰西共和国总统。他一身便装，但会见的双方都满含亲切，大使馆的谢尔登·怀特豪斯（Sheldon Whitehouse）担任翻译。总统把荣誉军团勋章（Cross of the Legion of Honor）别在这位小伙子借来的西装翻领上，并亲吻了他的双颊。

===Original===
By this time France was alive to Lindbergh; America was waking up.

===Chinese===
到这时，法国已为林德伯格而沸腾；美国也正在醒来。

===Original===
At the Aero Club of France he made his first speech. His precise laconic diction was one more step forward in the phenomenon of Lindbergh. The speech was printed widely in America. The Club was jammed that day and Minister of War Paul Painlevé, surrounded by fifty of the leading aviators of France, received the guest of honor. When the time came Herrick quietly leaned over and told Lindbergh he must respond. Whereupon the latter rose and said that Nungesser and Coli had attempted a far greater thing than he when they took off from Paris for New York. Their difficulties had far exceeded his. In any event he urged France not to give up hope. Nothing could have been more tactful.

===Chinese===
在法国航空俱乐部（Aero Club of France），他发表了第一次演讲。他精确而简洁的措辞，是林德伯格现象又向前迈出的一步。演讲在美国被广泛刊载。那天俱乐部里挤得水泄不通，陆军部长保罗·潘勒韦（Paul Painlevé）在法国五十位一流飞行员的簇拥下迎接贵宾。到了该致答词的时候，赫里克悄悄俯身告诉林德伯格他必须讲几句。于是他站起身来，说农热塞尔与科利从巴黎起飞前往纽约时，所尝试的伟业远比他的伟大，他们的困难远远超过他的。无论如何，他恳请法国不要放弃希望。再没有比这更得体的了。

===Original===
Ambassador Herrick's speech which followed emphasized the strengthened goodwill between France and America. "This young man from out of the west brings you better than anything else the spirit of America," he said. "His exploit shows you that the heart of the United States beats for France. It was needed at this moment that the love of these two great people should manifest itself, and it is this young boy who has brought that about. After his European trip is over he will go back to America and he will be able to tell them as no other man could that France really loves the people of the United States."

===Chinese===
随后赫里克大使的演讲强调了法美之间增进的善意。"这位来自西部的青年，带给你们的是胜过一切的美国的精气神，"他说，"他的壮举告诉你们，美利坚合众国的心为法兰西而跳动。此刻正需要这两个伟大民族的爱得以彰显，而促成这一点的，正是这个大孩子。欧洲之行结束后，他将回到美国，并能以任何人都无法企及的方式告诉国人：法国真心热爱美国人民。"

===Original===
Thus was the idea of "ambassadorship without portfolio" initiated. When press and people, and especially statesmen, began to see how the current strain between France and America was slackening as a result of Lindbergh's visit, the idea grew doubly strong.

===Chinese===
"无任所大使"的观念由此发端。当报界与民众、尤其是政治家们开始看到，法美之间当下的紧张正因林德伯格的访问而缓和，这一观念便愈发深入人心。

===Original===
On the following day he went to a large luncheon of 600 Americans at the American Club. On Wednesday he visited the French Chamber of Deputies. There was no session in progress, yet most of the members present followed him to the reception room of the President's residence. Like ferment in wine, Lindbergh's personality was working hour by hour.

===Chinese===
第二天，他出席了美洲俱乐部（American Club）600 名美国人的大型午宴。星期三，他参观了法国众议院。当时并未开会，但在场的多数议员仍跟随他来到议长官邸的接待厅。如酒中之酵，林德伯格的人格一小时一小时地发挥着作用。

===Original===
Here again the increased cordiality between France and America became the keynote of the interchange. The adored General Gouraud said: "It is not only two continents that you have united, but the hearts of all men everywhere in admiration of the simple courage of a man who does great things. … You and your youth belong to that glorious band of which M. Bleriot standing here beside you was one, and which has opened the great spaces. We greet you also in the name of those others of your countrymen who, in the Lafayette Escadrille, died here for France—who, like you, helped to frame that unalterable fraternity, that indissoluble friendship which unites our two peoples."

===Chinese===
在这里，法美之间与日俱增的亲善再度成为交谈的主调。受人爱戴的古罗将军（General Gouraud）说："你连接起来的不只是两大洲，还有普天之下众人的心，他们钦佩一个人成就伟业时那份朴素的勇气。……你和你的青春，属于那支光荣的队伍——此刻站在你身边的布莱里奥先生（M. Bleriot）便是其中一员——正是这支队伍开辟了辽阔的天空。我们还以你另一些同胞的名义向你致意，他们在拉法耶特飞行中队（Lafayette Escadrille）中在此地为法兰西捐躯——他们和你一样，帮助铸就了那份不可更易的手足之情、联结我们两个民族的牢不可破的友谊。"

===Original===
In like vein but with an eye to practicality Lindbergh replied:

===Chinese===
林德伯格以同样的格调、却着眼于实际地答道：

===Original===
"Gentlemen, 132 years ago Benjamin Franklin was asked: 'What good is your balloon? What will it accomplish?' He replied: 'What good is a new born child?' Less than twenty years ago when I was not far advanced from infancy M. Bleriot flew across the English Channel and was asked 'What good is your aeroplane? What will it accomplish?' Today those same skeptics might ask me what good has been my flight from New York to Paris. My answer is that I believe it is the forerunner of a great air service from America to France, America to Europe, to bring our peoples nearer together in understanding and in friendship than they have ever been."

===Chinese===
"诸位，132 年前，本杰明·富兰克林（Benjamin Franklin）被人问起：'你的气球有什么用？它能成就什么？'他答道：'一个新生儿有什么用？'不到二十年前，当我离开襁褓还没有多久的时候，布莱里奥先生飞越了英吉利海峡，人们问他：'你的飞机有什么用？它能成就什么？'今天，还是那批怀疑派，或许要问我，从纽约到巴黎的这次飞行有什么用。我的回答是：我相信它是一场伟大空运的先声——从美国到法国、从美国到欧洲，让两国人民在理解与友谊中空前地接近。"

===Original===
The speaker's abrupt but unmistakable sincerity made a profound impression upon his hearers.

===Chinese===
演讲者那近乎突兀却分明可感的诚挚，给听众留下了深刻的印象。

===Original===
It is impossible to do justice to the full Paris visit. Yet it is not difficult even now to sense the ever-increasing aura of popularity and affection that surrounded Lindbergh wherever he went.

===Chinese===
要对巴黎之行的全程作公允的记述是不可能的。然而即便在今天，也不难感受到那圈日益增涨的声望与爱戴的光环——林德伯格走到哪里，它便环绕到哪里。

===Original===
He lunched with Bleriot, the first man to fly across the English Channel, who presented him with a piece of the propeller of that famous plane of early days. He had a notable visit with Marshal Foch. He went to the Invalides surrounded by an admiring crowd. He went to the home of Marshal Joffre. He attended a formal lunch with Minister Briand.

===Chinese===
他与布莱里奥共进午餐——此人正是第一个飞越英吉利海峡的人，赠给他早年那架著名飞机的一片螺旋桨残件。他与福煦元帅（Marshal Foch）作了一次难忘的会面。他在仰慕人群的簇拥下游览了荣军院（the Invalides）。他到霞飞元帅（Marshal Joffre）府上拜访。他还出席了与白里安部长（Minister Briand）的正式午宴。

===Original===
Meanwhile a growing avalanche of mail was descending upon the Embassy. There were startling business offers running into millions of dollars. Cables from all parts of the world urged Lindbergh to write this or that, or agree to appear at highly remunerative rates under any and all circumstances. He did not handle this mail or accept any of these offers. He could not do the former, and he would not do the latter. But he was not cynical; only gravely dubious about the results of his original enterprise getting so far out of his control.

===Chinese===
与此同时，一场越来越大的邮件雪崩正向大使馆倾泻而下。其中有令人咋舌的商业开价，动辄数百万美元。来自世界各地的电报敦促林德伯格写这写那，或者应允在任何场合、以报酬极高的身价露面。这些信件他没有处理，这些开价他一概没有接受。前者他无暇顾及，后者他不愿为之。但他并非愤世嫉俗，只是为自己最初的事业竟落到如此不受掌控的境地，怀着深深的疑虑。

===Original===
On Thursday of that Paris week came the official reception by the City. By this time the popularity of the boy held full sway. It is said that half a million people lined the streets through which the flier drove in company with his host, the Ambassador.

===Chinese===
