In one particular instance, after several futile attempts to get down, the cadet began circling around overhead. His apparent idea was to clinch the chances of landing on his next attempt by waiting until the gasoline ran out. His instructor was out in the field trying to flag him down without the slightest success and for half an hour we watched the ship intently for the first signs of a lowering gas supply; hoping that the fuel would not hold out much longer as the morning flying period was nearly over and we were all anxious to see him land. After half an hour, however, he apparently regained enough courage and determination to make a last attempt at a landing, which turned out much more successfully than the others.

===Chinese===
飞行训练中最激动人心的时刻，大概要数单飞开始的那段日子。教官会爬出座舱，在方向舵上系一条白手帕作危险信号——表示机上多半无人有效掌控——然后示意学员起飞。有的飞机起飞利落，绕场一周，降落得相当像样；有的则一落地就弹跳成串，非得再试第二回、第三回，机轮才能贴住地面超过一瞬。有一桩特别的例子：几番落地无果之后，那位学员索性在头顶上盘旋不去，他的主意显然是等汽油烧光，稳操下次降落的胜券。他的教官站在场里挥旗想叫他下来，毫无成效；整整半小时，我们紧盯那架飞机，等着油量将尽的头一个征兆，巴望着燃料千万别再撑下去——因为上午的飞行时段快结束了，我们都急着看他落地。不过半小时后，他显然重新鼓足了勇气与决心，做了最后一次降落尝试，结果比先前几次都体面得多。

===Original===
When the solo flights were more or less successfully completed the flying instruction was divided into two periods of forty-five minutes each. One of these was used for dual and the other for solo practice.

===Chinese===
单飞大体告成之后，飞行教学分成各四十五分钟的两个时段：一段用于双座带飞，一段用于单飞练习。

===Original===
The instructor would attempt to smooth out the rough points in his students' flying and demonstrate the method of going through new maneuvers so that the cadet could be given the opportunity to go up alone and try out the maneuvers for himself.

===Chinese===
教官着力磨平学员飞行中的毛糙之处，示范新机动的要领，好让学员有机会独自上天，亲自演练这些动作。

===Original===
One of the first lessons was the "three sixty"—so named because its completion required a total change in direction of three hundred and sixty degrees. The cadet would take off and climb to eight hundred or a thousand feet. The higher he went the less difficulty he had in properly completing the maneuver. Then he would fly into the wind directly over a landing "T" in the center of the field. As the plane passed over the "T" he throttled his motor and made a quick bank either to the right or left depending upon his preliminary instructions. The object was to make a complete circle and land without using the motor, bringing the plane to a stop beside the "T."

===Chinese===
最初的科目之一是"三百六"（three sixty）——因完成它需要方向总共改变三百六十度而得名。学员起飞后爬升到八百或一千英尺：飞得越高，规规矩矩完成这个机动就越容易。然后顶着风飞到机场中央的降落"T"字布正上方，飞机一越过"T"字便收油门，按预先的嘱咐向左或向右迅速压坡度。目标是转完一整圈、不用发动机便降下，并让飞机停在"T"字旁。

===Original===
"One eightys" were the next requirement and they were probably the cause of more crashes than any other maneuver. They were started in the same manner as the "three sixty," but with the plane heading down wind and at only five hundred feet altitude. They required quick manipulation of controls and a steep bank into the field just before landing.

===Chinese===
接下来是"一百八"（one eighty），它大概是酿成事故最多的机动。起步方式与"三百六"相同，只是飞机顺风进入，且高度只有五百英尺。它要求操纵敏捷，并在临落地前向场内压出陡峭的坡度。

===Original===
Next came acrobatics. Loops, spins, barrel rolls, Jenny Immelmans, figure-eights, wing-overs, and reversements, every one of which each cadet had to master thoroughly during his course at Brooks.

===Chinese===
再往下是特技：筋斗、螺旋、桶滚、"珍妮"式殷麦曼、8字飞行、跃升倒转和回转（reversement）——每一项，每个学员在布鲁克斯期间都必须练到烂熟。

===Original===
After the first few weeks had passed we became more or less accustomed to life in the cadet detachment, and found a little time now and then to look around the country and even spend a night in San Antonio. Our examinations were purposely given on Saturday morning so that we would not spend the weekend studying. It was well known that too much studying affected a cadet's flying and the school schedule was arranged with that in mind.

===Chinese===
最初几周过去，我们多少习惯了学员队的生活，时不常还能挤出点工夫到乡间转转，甚至去圣安东尼奥过上一夜。考试特意安排在星期六上午，免得我们把周末耗在书本上。用功过度有损学员的飞行，这是人所共知的，课程表正据此编排。

===Original===
Our day began with first call at five-forty-five and flying started about seven. At eleven we returned to the barracks and from one to five o'clock was devoted to ground school. After supper we could study until bed check at ten o'clock. Plenty of sleep is a necessity for the student pilot, and that fact is recognized nowhere more than in the army schools. Every week night at ten p.m. the cadet officer of the day checks each bunk and turns in the names of any vacant ones. Some of our academic subjects, such as aerodynamics and machine guns required nearly all of our time after school because of approaching examinations, whereas others were comparatively easy and the classroom instruction was sufficient in itself. When we were not studying there were always plenty of other things to attract our attention. If one of the boys left the post, as sometimes happened, he often returned to find his belongings heaped together in the middle of the floor with the army cots piled on top. Several times some cadet returned at midnight to find his equipment carefully transferred and set up on the roof or in the mess-hall. Another one of the favorite sports was to put a hose in the bed of a sound sleeper at two a.m. or, if he slept with his mouth open, to fill it from a tube of shaving cream or hair grease.

===Chinese===
我们的一天从五点四十五分的头遍号开始，七点左右开飞。十一点回营房，一点到五点用于地面学校。晚饭后可以自习，十点查铺就寝。充足的睡眠是飞行学员的必需，这一点哪儿都不如陆军学校认识得透彻。每个工作日晚间十点，学员值日军官逐铺查点，空铺者记名上报。有些课程，如空气动力学和机枪，因考试临近几乎占去了课后全部时间；另一些则比较轻松，课堂讲授足矣。不学习的时候，招引我们注意的花样从来不少。哪个小伙子擅离驻地——这种事时有发生——回来常会发现家当堆在地板中央，行军床压在最上头。有几次，某位学员半夜归来，发现自己的铺盖被小心翼翼地搬到了屋顶或饭堂里原样支好。另一种深受喜爱的运动，是凌晨两点往熟睡者的被窝里塞水管，或者，要是他张着嘴睡，就往嘴里挤一管剃须膏或发油。

===Original===
One of the fellows found a scorpion in his bed and each night for a week thereafter looked through the bedding for another, but finally became careless and forgot to look. His nearest neighbor promptly placed a number of grasshoppers between the sheets near the foot of the bed.

===Chinese===
有个伙伴在床上发现过一只蝎子，此后一连一周，每晚都要把被褥翻查一遍看有没有第二只，可最后还是大意了，忘了查。他的紧邻便不失时机地把几只蚱蜢塞进了床脚的被单之间。

===Original===
Another evening it was reported that three polecats had crawled into a culvert in front of the barracks. For an hour we attempted to smoke them out. When that failed the fire department was called and we washed them out. The smoke had evidently taken effect, however, and soon three dead polecats came floating out from the culvert. The next problem was how to make use of such possibilities. That question was worthy of a most careful consideration. After a survey of the barracks we found that our cadet first sergeant was in San Antonio. There was scarcely one of us who did not have some small score to settle with him so we took one of the pillows from his bed and after removing the pillow case, placed it behind one of the polecats. The desired results were then obtained by stepping on the back end of the cat, and after cautiously inserting the pillow back in its case, we replaced it on the first sergeant's bunk. The results were far above expectation. One by one the occupants of that bay arose and carried their cots outside, until by midnight, when our sergeant returned, there were only a half dozen bunks left including his own. By that time the odor had permeated through the other bedding and he was unable to locate the pillow as being the primary cause of offense. Any night for nearly two weeks thereafter our first sergeant and his cot might be located out behind the barracks, and the inspection of quarters, which was to have been held the following morning, was postponed indefinitely.

===Chinese===
又一个傍晚，有人报告三只臭鼬钻进了营房前的涵洞。我们熏了一个钟头想把它们熏出来，未果，又喊来消防队用水冲。看来烟已经起了作用，不多时三只死臭鼬顺水漂出了涵洞。接下来的问题是：这样的好材料该如何利用。这个问题值得最审慎的研究。勘察一番营房后，我们发现学员上士去了圣安东尼奥。我们中间难得有谁不欠他点小账，于是我们从他床上取下一只枕头，褪去枕套，垫在其中一只臭鼬身后。朝那鼬的尾部一踩，想要的效果便得到了；再把枕头小心翼翼地塞回枕套，放回上士的铺上。效果远远超出预期。那个隔间的人一个接一个爬起来，搬着床到外面去住；到半夜我们那位上士归来时，全间只剩六张床，包括他自己的。此时臭气已浸透其余被褥，他实在查不出那只枕头才是罪魁祸首。此后近两个星期里的任何一夜，我们的一等军士和他的行军床都可能安置在营房后头；而原定次日上午举行的内务检查，则被无限期推迟了。

===Original===
During our last six weeks at Brooks, life became much less difficult. Most of us who had survived the check pilots and "Benzine Board" were reasonably sure of graduating and although our studies were just as exacting as ever, we were able to absorb them much more easily. Also we had passed our primary flying tests and were making cross country flights in TW-3's; and learning formation flying in Voughts. And finally we were given a few hours in De Havilands in preparation for the advanced training at Kelly.

===Chinese===
在布鲁克斯的最后六个星期，日子好过多了。我们这些闯过考核飞行员和"汽油委员会"的人大多稳操毕业的胜券，功课虽一如既往地严苛，吸收起来却容易多了。初级飞行考试也已通过，我们驾驶 TW-3 做长途转场，用沃特式（Vought）学编队飞行。最后，我们还飞了几个小时德·哈维兰，为凯利的高级训练做准备。

===Original===
We were paired up for the cross country flights. One of us flew on the way out, while the second acted as observer. On the return flight we traded about, so that each achieved an equal amount of experience, both as an observer and as a pilot. These trips were usually laid out in a triangular course, and included landing at each corner of the triangle.

===Chinese===
长途转场按两人一组编配。去程一人驾驶，另一人担任观测员；回程轮换，使每人在观测员与飞行员两个角色上得到均等的历练。这类飞行通常沿三角形航线安排，并在三角形的每个角上各降落一次。

===Original===
While on one of our first trips from the home airdrome, we landed in the designated field alongside of a road just as a load of watermelons was passing by, so we carried several of them back to the Detachment in our plane.

===Chinese===
首次离驻外出转场时，我们刚在路边指定的场地上落稳，恰逢一车西瓜经过，便顺手捎了几个回去，慰劳全体学员队。

===Original===
Always there was some new experience, always something interesting going on to make the time spent in Brooks and Kelly one of the banner years in a pilot's life. The training is rigid and difficult but there is none better. A cadet must be willing to forget all other interest in life when he enters the Texas flying schools and he must enter with the intention of devoting every effort and all of the energy during the next twelve months towards a single goal. But when he receives the wings at Kelly a year later he has the satisfaction of knowing that he has graduated from one of the world's finest flying schools.

===Chinese===
总有新的经历，总有有趣的事情发生，使在布鲁克斯和凯利度过的这段时光成为飞行员一生中的鼎盛年华之一。训练严酷艰难，却无出其右者。跨进得克萨斯的飞行学校，学员就必须甘愿忘却生活中其他一切趣味，抱定把此后十二个月的全部心血与气力投向唯一目标的决心。而当一年之后他在凯利接过翼章时，他可以心满意足地知道：自己毕业于世界上最优秀的飞行学校之一。

## VIII Two Emergency Jumps / 八 两次应急跳伞

===Original===
By the first of April our organization was well under way and about a week before the inauguration day we took two planes over the route to make any final arrangements necessary.

===Chinese===
到4月1日，我们的组织工作已全面铺开。开航日前约一周，我们带了两架飞机沿航线走了一趟，做好一切必要的最后安排。

===Original===
On April fifteenth at 5:50 a.m. I took off from the Air Mail Field at Maywood on the first southbound flight, and that afternoon we sent two ships north with the inauguration mail from St. Louis, Springfield and Peoria.

===Chinese===
4月15日上午5点50分，我从梅伍德（Maywood）的航空邮政机场起飞，执行首次南向航班；当天下午，我们又将两架飞机派往北线，载运圣路易斯、斯普林菲尔德和皮奥里亚的开航邮件。

===Original===
During the summer months most of our route was covered during daylight, but as winter approached the hours of night flying increased until darkness set in a few minutes after we left the field at St. Louis.

===Chinese===
夏季几个月，航线的大部分航程都能在白天飞完；但随着冬季临近，夜航的时数不断增加，直到从圣路易斯机场起飞几分钟后天就黑透了。

===Original===
With night flying and bad weather our troubles began. Our route was not lighted at first and the intermediate airports were small and often in poor condition. Our weather reports were unreliable and we developed the policy of taking off with the mail whenever local weather conditions permitted. We went as far as we could and if the visibility became too bad we landed and entrained the mail.

===Chinese===
夜航加坏天气，麻烦接踵而至。起初航线沿途没有灯光导航，中途机场狭小且状况常常不佳。气象报告不可靠，我们便形成了一条方针：只要当地天气允许，就带着邮件起飞。能飞多远飞多远，一旦能见度过差，就落地把邮件转交铁路。

===Original===
One of the worst conditions we met with was in flying from daylight into darkness. It was not difficult to fly along with a hundred foot ceiling in the daytime, but to do so at night was an entirely different matter, and after the night set in, if the weather became worse, it was not possible to turn around and return to daylight.

===Chinese===
我们遭遇过的最恶劣的状况之一，便是从白昼飞入黑夜。白天在百英尺云幕下贴地赶路还不算难，夜里如法炮制就完全是另一回事了；而且夜幕一旦落下，天气若再恶化，就不可能掉头飞回白昼里去。

===Original===
With all of our difficulties, however, the mail went through with surprising regularity. During the first five months of operation we made connections on over ninety-eight percent of our trips.

===Chinese===
然而纵有千难万难，邮件的通达依然准得惊人。开航头五个月，我们的航班衔接率超过百分之九十八。

===Original===
There are only two conditions which delay the air mail: fog and sleet. If the fog is light or local, and the sleet not too heavy, the planes continue even then. But when the ground becomes invisible and the fog covers the terminal fields, or when sleet freezes thickly on wings and wires, the planes cannot continue. In such cases the mail is entrained and usually reaches its destination at least as soon as it would have if sent by train in the first place.

===Chinese===
能延误航空邮件的只有两种天气：雾和冻雨。雾若轻淡或只限于局部，冻雨也不算太猛，飞机照飞不误。可一旦地面隐没不见、大雾罩住终点机场，或冻雨在机翼和张线上结成厚冰，飞机就无法继续前行了。这种时候邮件便转交铁路，通常并不比一开始就走火车到得更晚。

===Original===
Almost every day, in some section of the United States, mail pilots are flying over fog and through storms and rain to bring their ships through on schedule time. The mail plane is seldom delayed and then only by impossible weather conditions. In the future these delays will become fewer as radio navigation and instruments for blind flying improve, until it will be possible for the pilots to keep to their schedules under the worst conditions and in comparative safety.

===Chinese===
几乎每一天，在合众国的某个地段，航邮飞行员都在雾上飞行、在风暴和雨中穿行，让飞机准点抵达。邮机很少误点，即便误点，也只因天气实在不允许。随着无线电导航和盲飞仪表的改进，这样的延误今后会越来越少，直到飞行员在最恶劣的条件下也能相对安全地守住时刻表。

===Original===
Another hazard, during certain times of year, is the formation of ice. This will gather on all parts of the plane but mainly on the wires, propeller, and entering edge of the wings. If it forms slowly from a fog or light rain, a plane may be able to continue on its course for some time, but if a heavy sleet storm is encountered the ice may form so rapidly that a ship cannot stay in the air over five minutes before it is so loaded down that the pilot will be unable to keep from losing altitude even with his motor wide open.

===Chinese===
一年中某些时节的另一重危险是结冰。冰会在飞机各个部位聚积，但主要集中在张线、螺旋桨和机翼前缘上。若是雾或细雨缓慢凝成，飞机或许还能沿航线撑上一阵；可一旦撞上猛烈的冻雨，结冰可能快到飞机在空中撑不过五分钟，就被压得即使全开油门也止不住掉高度。

===Original===
The actual weight of ice is not as important as the loss in efficiency of the wing, due to the changed airfoil caused by ice gathering on the entering edge.

===Chinese===
冰的实际重量倒在其次，更要命的是机翼效率的损失——前缘积冰改变了翼型的缘故。

===Original===
Still more loss is caused from the ice forming on the propeller itself. The blades take on a thick coating which continues to increase in depth until the ice from one of the blades is thrown off by centrifugal force. When this happens an excessive vibration sets in and continues until the opposite blade has thrown off its coating.

===Chinese===
螺旋桨本身结冰造成的损失更大。桨叶裹上厚厚一层冰，越结越厚，直到其中一片桨叶上的冰被离心力甩脱。这一甩，剧烈的震动便随之而来，一直持续到对面的桨叶也甩掉冰壳为止。

===Original===
One of the dangers which a mail pilot faces in flying at night through bad weather and low visibility is in suddenly losing track of the ground due to a fog bank lower than the rest. If he has been flying very close to the ground it is not advisable to go lower, and often the only alternative is to climb up through the fog and attempt to find a hole somewhere to spiral down through.

===Chinese===
航邮飞行员夜间在恶劣天气和低能见度中飞行所面临的危险之一，是忽然被一片比别处更低的雾带切断了对地面的参照。倘若他一直飞得很低，再往下压并不明智，而唯一的出路往往是向上钻出雾层，再设法找到一处空隙盘旋降下去。

===Original===
Being caught in a fog at night was the cause of two of my forced jumps, the official reports of which follow:

===Chinese===
我在夜间被困雾中，曾因此两度被迫跳伞，官方报告照录如下：

===Original===
"I took off from Lambert-St. Louis Field at 4:25 p.m., September 16, 1926, and after an uneventful trip arrived at Springfield, IL, at 5:10 p.m., and Peoria, IL, at 5:55 p.m.

===Chinese===
"我于1926年9月16日下午4点25分自兰伯特—圣路易斯机场起飞，一路无事，于下午5点10分抵达伊利诺伊州斯普林菲尔德，下午5点55分抵达伊利诺伊州皮奥里亚。

===Original===
"I left the Peoria Field at 6:10 p.m. There was a light ground haze, but the sky was practically clear with but scattered cumulus clouds. Darkness was encountered about 25 miles northeast of Peoria, and I took up a compass course, checking on the lights of the towns below until a low fog rolled in under me a few miles northeast of Marseilles and the Illinois River.

===Chinese===
"我于下午6点10分离开皮奥里亚机场。地面薄雾轻笼，天空基本晴朗，只有零散的积云。约在皮奥里亚东北25英里处入夜，我转入罗盘航向，借着下方城镇的灯光核对位置，直到一团低雾在马赛利斯（Marseilles）和伊利诺伊河东北几英里处从我机下涌起。

===Original===
"The fog extended from the ground up to about 600 feet, and, as I was unable to fly under it, I turned back and attempted to drop a flare and land. The flare did not function and I again headed for Maywood (Chicago's air mail port) hoping to find a break in the fog over the field. Examination disclosed that the cause of the flare failure was the short length of the release lever and that the flare might still be used by pulling out the release cable.

===Chinese===
"雾从地面一直漫到约600英尺高。我无法从雾下飞过，便折返回来，试图投一枚照明弹后降落。照明弹没有点火，我只好再度朝梅伍德（芝加哥的航空邮政港）飞去，指望机场上空的雾有个豁口。检查发现，照明弹失灵的原因是释放杆过短，改用手拽释放钢索仍可将其使用。

===Original===
"I continued on a compass course of 50 degrees until 7:15 p.m. when I saw a dull glow on top of the fog, indicating a town below. There were several of these light patches on the fog, visible only when looking away from the moon, and I knew them to be towns bordering Maywood. At no time, however, was I able to locate the exact position of the field, although I understand that the searchlights were directed upward and two barrels of gasoline burned in an endeavor to attract my attention. Several times I descended to the top of the fog, which was 800 to 900 feet high, according to my altimeter. The sky above was clear with the exception of scattered clouds, and the moon and stars were shining brightly. After circling around for 35 minutes I headed west to be sure of clearing Lake Michigan, and in an attempt to pick up one of the lights on the Transcontinental.

===Chinese===
"我保持50度罗盘航向继续飞，到下午7点15分，看见雾顶泛出一团微光，表明下方有座城镇。雾面上这样的亮斑有好几处，只有背对月亮望去才看得见，我知道那是梅伍德周边的城镇。但我始终没能确定机场的确切位置——尽管听说地面把探照灯直指天空，还烧起两桶汽油，想引起我的注意。有好几次我下降到雾顶，按我的高度表，雾顶高800至900英尺。上面的天空除零散云外一派澄澈，月明星亮。盘旋35分钟后，我转向西飞，以确保避开密歇根湖，并试图找到横贯大陆航线上的一处灯光。

===Original===
"After flying west for fifteen minutes and seeing no break I turned southwest hoping to strike the edge of the fog south of the Illinois River. My engine stopped at 8:20 p.m., and I cut in the reserve. I was at that time only 1,500 feet high, and as the engine did not pick up as soon as I expected I shoved the flashlight in my belt and was about to release the parachute flare and jump when the engine finally took hold again. A second trial showed the main tank to be dry, and accordingly a maximum of twenty minutes' flying time left.

===Chinese===
"向西飞了十五分钟仍不见豁口，我转向西南，指望在伊利诺伊河以南撞上雾的边缘。晚8点20分，发动机熄了火，我接通备用油箱。当时我只有1500英尺高，发动机却没有如我预期的那样迅速恢复，我把手电筒插进腰带，正要释放照明弹跳伞时，发动机终于又咬上了劲。再试一次，发现主油箱已经干了，至多还剩二十分钟的飞行时间。

===Original===
"There were no openings in the fog and I decided to leave the ship as soon as the reserve tank was exhausted. I tried to get the mail pit open with the idea of throwing out the mail sacks, and then jumping, but was unable to open the front buckle. I knew that the risk of fire with no gasoline in the tanks was very slight and began to climb for altitude when I saw a light on the ground for several seconds. This was the first light I had seen for nearly two hours, and as almost enough gasoline for fifteen minutes' flying remained in the reserve, I glided down to 1,200 feet and pulled out the flare release cable as nearly as I could judge over the spot where the light had appeared. This time the flare functioned but only to illuminate the top of a solid bank of fog, into which it soon disappeared without showing any trace of the ground.

===Chinese===
"雾里没有一丝空隙，我决意等备用油箱一耗尽就离机。我本想打开邮舱，把邮袋先抛出去再跳，却打不开前面的卡扣。我知道油箱空了以后起火的风险很小，便开始爬高，这时我看见地面上有一盏灯亮了几秒钟。这是近两个小时里我见到的第一盏灯；备用油箱里还剩差不多够飞十五分钟的汽油，我于是下滑到1200英尺，尽量对准那灯光出现过的地方，拉出了照明弹的释放钢索。这一回照明弹倒是燃了，却只照亮一团密实雾层的顶部，很快便隐入雾中，没有照出地面的半点踪影。

===Original===
"Seven minutes' gasoline remained in the gravity tank. Seeing the glow of a town through the fog I turned towards open country and nosed the plane up. At 5,000 feet the engine sputtered and died. I stepped up on the cowling and out over the right side of the cockpit, pulling the rip cord after about a 100-foot fall. The parachute, an Irving seat service type, functioned perfectly; I was falling head downward when the risers jerked me into an upright position and the chute opened. This time I saved the rip cord. I pulled the flashlight from my belt and was playing it down towards the top of the fog when I heard the plane's engine pick up. When I jumped it had practically stopped dead and I had neglected to cut the switches. Apparently when the ship nosed down an additional supply of gasoline drained to the carburetor. Soon she came into sight, about a quarter mile away and headed in the general direction of my parachute. I put the flashlight in a pocket of my flying suit preparatory to slipping the parachute out of the way if necessary. The plane was making a left spiral of about a mile diameter, and passed approximately 300 yards away from my chute, leaving me on the outside of the circle. I was undecided as to whether the plane or I was descending the more rapidly and glided my chute away from the spiral path of the ship as rapidly as I could. The ship passed completely out of sight, but reappeared in a few seconds, its rate of descent being about the same as that of the parachute. I counted the five spirals, each one a little further away than the last, before reaching the top of the fog bank.

===Chinese===
"重力油箱里还剩七分钟的汽油。看见雾下透出一座城镇的光晕，我转向旷野，拉起机头。到5000英尺，发动机噗噗两声，停了。我蹬上整流罩，从座舱右侧翻出去，坠落约100英尺后拉开了开伞绳。这具欧文（Irving）座式军用降落伞工作得完美无缺；我正头朝下坠落，吊带猛地把我拽成直立，伞张开了。这一次我留住了开伞绳。我从腰带上拔出手电，正朝雾顶照下去，忽听飞机的发动机重新响了。跳伞时它实际上已经彻底停转，我却忘了切断电门。显然飞机一头俯下时，又有一股汽油流进了化油器。不多时它便映入眼帘，在约四分之一英里外，大体朝着我降落伞的方向飞来。我把手电揣进飞行服口袋，准备必要时把降落伞滑出它的航道。飞机正划着直径约一英里的左螺旋，从离我的伞约300码处掠过，把我留在了圈外。我一时拿不准是飞机降得快还是我降得快，便竭力操纵降落伞避开飞机的螺旋轨迹。飞机一度完全消失，几秒钟后又浮现出来，下降率与降落伞大体相当。在到达雾顶之前，我数完了它的五圈螺旋，一圈比一圈离我更远。

===Original===
"When I settled into the fog I knew that the ground was within 1,000 feet and reached for the flashlight, but found it to be missing. I could see neither earth nor stars and had no idea what kind of territory was below. I crossed my legs to keep from straddling a branch or wire, guarded my face with my hands and waited. Presently I saw the outline of the ground and a moment later was down in a cornfield. The corn was over my head and the chute was lying on top of the corn stalks. I hurriedly packed it and started down a corn row. The ground visibility was about 100 yards. In a few minutes I came to a stubble field and some wagon tracks which I followed to a farmyard a quarter mile away. After reaching the farmyard I noticed auto headlights playing over the roadside. Thinking that someone might have located the wreck of the plane I walked over to the car. The occupants asked whether I had heard an airplane crash and it required some time to explain to them that I had been piloting the plane, and yet was searching for it myself. I had to display the parachute as evidence before they were thoroughly convinced. The farmer was sure, as were most others in a 3-mile radius, that the ship had just missed his house and crashed nearby. In fact, he could locate within a few rods the spot where he heard it hit the ground, and we spent an unsuccessful quarter hour hunting for the wreck in that vicinity before going to the farmhouse to arrange for a searching party and telephone St. Louis and Chicago.

===Chinese===
"沉入雾中的那一刻，我知道地面就在1000英尺之内，伸手去掏手电，却发现手电不见了。我看不见大地也看不见星星，完全不知道下面是什么地形。我盘起双腿，免得跨坐在树枝或电线上，双手护住脸，等着。不多时，我看见了地面的轮廓，转眼便落进了一片玉米地。玉米高过头顶，降落伞摊在玉米秆上。我匆匆把伞收拢打包，顺着一条玉米垄走出去。地面能见度约100码。几分钟后，我走到一片茬地和几道车辙旁，循着车辙走到四分之一英里外的一处农院。到了农院，我注意到汽车前灯在路边扫来扫去。想着或许有人已经找到飞机残骸，我便朝那辆车走去。车上的人问我是否听见飞机坠毁，我费了好一番口舌才让他们明白：驾驶那架飞机的正是我，而我自己也正在找它。直到我把降落伞亮出来作证，他们才彻底信服。这位农夫——连同方圆3英里内的大多数人——都笃信飞机擦着他家房顶过去，就坠在附近。他甚至能在几杆地之内指出他听见飞机砸地的位置。我们在那一带徒劳地搜寻了一刻钟，才回农舍去组织搜寻队，并给圣路易斯和芝加哥挂电话。

===Original===
"I had just put in the long distance calls when the phone rang and we were notified that the plane had been found in a cornfield over two miles away. It took several minutes to reach the site of the crash, due to the necessity of slow driving through the fog, and a small crowd had already assembled when we arrived. The plane was wound up in a ball-shaped mass. It had narrowly missed one farmhouse and had hooked its left wing in a grain shock a quarter mile beyond. The ship had landed on the left wing and wheel and skidded along the ground for 80 yards, going through one fence before coming to rest in the edge of a cornfield about 100 yards short of a barn. The mail pit was laid open and one sack of mail was on the ground. The mail, however, was uninjured.

===Chinese===
"长途电话刚挂通，铃就响了，有人通知说飞机已在两英里外的一片玉米地里找到。雾中只能缓行，赶了好几分钟才到坠机现场，我们抵达时已聚起一小群人。飞机绞成了一团球状残骸。它险险擦过一座农舍，左翼又钩在再过去四分之一英里处的一个禾捆堆上。飞机以左翼和机轮触地，贴着地面滑了80码，撞穿一道围栏，最后停在玉米地边上，离一座谷仓还差约100码。邮舱豁开，一袋邮件散在地上，但邮件本身安然无恙。

===Original===
"The sheriff from Ottawa arrived, and we took the mail to the Ottawa Post Office to be entrained at 3:30 a.m. for Chicago."

===Chinese===
"渥太华（Ottawa）来的治安官到了，我们把邮件送进渥太华邮局，于凌晨3点30分转火车运往芝加哥。"

===Original===
When the wreck was inspected a few days later it was discovered that a mechanic had removed the 110 gallon gasoline tank to repair a leak and had replaced it with an 85 gallon tank without notifying anyone of the change. Consequently instead of being able to return to our field at Peoria, IL, and clear visibility, I ran out of fuel while still over the fog bank.

===Chinese===
几天后检查残骸时才发现，一名机械师曾拆下110加仑的油箱去修漏，换上了一个85加仑的油箱，却没有把这一变动通知任何人。结果，我非但没能返回伊利诺伊州皮奥里亚我们的机场、回到晴空之下，反而在雾层上空就耗尽了燃料。

===Original===
The circumstances surrounding my fourth emergency parachute jump were almost similar to those of the third. I took off from the Lambert-St. Louis Field at 4:20 p.m., made a five minute stop at Springfield, IL, an hour later to take on mail, and then headed for Peoria. Weather reports telephoned to St. Louis earlier in the afternoon gave flying conditions as entirely passable. About twenty-five miles north of Springfield darkness was encountered, the ceiling had lowered to around 400 feet and a light snow was falling. At South Pekin the forward visibility of ground lights from a 150 ft. altitude was less than half a mile, and over Pekin the town lights were indistinct from 200 ft. above. After passing Pekin the plane was flown at an altimeter reading of 600 feet for about five minutes, when the lightness of the haze below indicated that it was over Peoria. Twice I could see lights on the ground and I descended to less than 200 feet before they disappeared from view. I tried to bank around one group of lights, but was unable to turn quickly enough to keep in sight.

===Chinese===
我第四次应急跳伞的经过与第三次几乎如出一辙。我于下午4点20分从兰伯特—圣路易斯机场起飞，一小时后在伊利诺伊州斯普林菲尔德停了五分钟搭载邮件，随即飞往皮奥里亚。当天下午早些时候发给圣路易斯的天气电话报告都说飞行条件完全过得去。在斯普林菲尔德以北约二十五英里处入夜，云幕压到400英尺上下，飘起了小雪。在南皮京（South Pekin），从150英尺高度前望地面灯光，能见度不足半英里；飞到皮京（Pekin）上空，从200英尺高度看镇上灯光已然模糊。过了皮京，飞机按高度表600英尺飞了约五分钟，下方雾霭的浅淡表明已到皮奥里亚上空。有两回我看见了地面灯光，降到不足200英尺时灯光又没入视野之外。我试着绕着一片灯光压坡度兜过去，却转得不够快，没能盯住它。

===Original===
After circling in the vicinity of Peoria for 30 minutes, I decided to try and find better weather conditions by flying northeast towards Chicago. I had ferried a ship from Chicago to St. Louis in the early afternoon, at which time the ceiling and visibility were much better near Chicago than anywhere else along the route. Enough gasoline for about an hour and ten minutes' flying remained in the gas tank, and 20 minutes in the reserve, hardly enough to return to St. Louis even had I been able to navigate directly to the field by dead reckoning and flying blind the greater portion of the way. The territory towards Chicago was much more favorable for a night landing than that around St. Louis.

===Chinese===
在皮奥里亚附近盘旋30分钟后，我决定朝东北往芝加哥方向飞，碰碰更好的天气。当天下午早些时候，我刚从芝加哥调机来圣路易斯，那时芝加哥附近的云幕和能见度都比沿线任何地方好得多。主油箱里的汽油还够飞约一小时十分钟，备用油箱20分钟——即便我能靠航位推算直飞机场、且大半路程盲飞，这点油也很难撑回圣路易斯了。朝芝加哥去的地形，也比圣路易斯周边有利于夜间迫降。

===Original===
For the next half hour the flight northeast was at about 2,000 feet altitude and then at 600 feet. There were now numerous breaks in the clouds and occasionally ground lights could be seen from over 500 feet. After passing over the light of a small town a fairly clear space in the clouds was encountered. I pulled up to about 600 feet, released the parachute flare, whipped the ship around to get into the wind and under the flare which lit at once. Instead of floating down slowly, however, it dropped like a rock. I could see the ground for only an instant and then there was total darkness. Meantime the ship was in a steep bank, and being blinded by the intense light I had trouble righting it. An effort to find the ground with the wing lights was in vain as their glare was worse than useless in the haze.

===Chinese===
此后半小时，东北航程先在约2000英尺高度、后在600英尺高度进行。这时云层出现了许多豁口，偶尔从500多英尺的高度也能看见地面灯光。飞过一座小镇的灯光之后，云中遇到了一片相当晴空。我拉起到约600英尺，放出照明弹，猛地掉头对风，钻到照明弹下方——它应声点亮。然而它并不缓缓飘降，而是像石头一样直坠而下。我只来得及看地面一眼，随即坠入一片漆黑。此时飞机正处于陡峭坡度中，我被强光晃得眼花，费了番周折才把它改平。想借翼灯找地面也是徒劳，它们的眩光在雾霭中非但无用，反而更糟。

===Original===
When about ten minutes of gas remained in the pressure tank and still not the faintest outline of any object on the ground could be seen, I decided to leave the ship rather than attempt to land blindly. I turned back southwest toward less populated country and started climbing in an attempt to get over the clouds before jumping. The main tank went dry at 7:50 p.m. and the reserve twenty minutes later. The altimeter then registered approximately 14,000 feet, yet the top of the clouds was apparently several thousand feet higher. Rolling the stabilizer back, I cut out the switches, pulled the ship up into a stall and was about to go over the right side of the cockpit when the right wing began to drop. In this position the plane would gather speed and spiral to the right, possibly striking the parachute after its first turn. I returned to the controls, righted the plane and then dove over the left side of the cockpit while the air speed registered about 70 miles per hour and the altimeter 13,000 feet. The rip cord was pulled immediately after clearing the stabilizer. The Irving chute functioned perfectly. I left the ship head first and was falling in this position when the risers whipped me around into an upright position and the chute opened. The last I saw of the DH was as it disappeared into the clouds just after the chute opened. It was snowing and very cold. For the first minute or so the parachute descended smoothly and then commenced an excessive oscillation which continued for about five minutes and which could not be checked. The first indication of the nearness of the chute to the ground was a gradual darkening of the space below. The snow had turned to rain and, although the chute was thoroughly soaked, its oscillation had greatly decreased. I directed the beam from my 500 ft. spotlight downward, but the ground appeared so suddenly that I landed directly on top of a barbed wire fence without seeing it. The fence helped to break the fall and the barbs did not penetrate my heavy flying suit. The chute was blown over the fence and was held open for some time by the gusts of wind before collapsing.

===Chinese===
当压力油箱里的汽油只剩约十分钟、而地面上仍看不出任何物体的最模糊轮廓时，我决定弃机，不再尝试盲目降落。我转向西南、朝人烟较稀的地带回飞，并开始爬升，想在跳伞前飞到云层之上。晚7点50分主油箱干了，二十分钟后备用油箱也干了。此时高度表指示约14000英尺，可云顶显然还在几千英尺之上。我把水平尾翼调向后位，切断电门，将飞机拉入失速，正要从座舱右侧翻出去，右翼却开始下坠。在这个姿态下，飞机会增速并向右盘旋，转过第一圈后就可能撞上降落伞。我回到操纵位，把飞机改平，随即从座舱左侧俯冲跃出——此刻空速表指示约每小时70英里，高度表13000英尺。一脱离水平尾翼我便拉动了开伞绳。欧文降落伞工作得完美无缺。我头朝下离机，正以这个姿势坠落时，吊带把我猛甩成直立，伞张开了。伞张开后，我最后望见那架 DH 没入云中。天正下雪，寒气砭骨。头一分钟光景降落伞缓缓而降，随后开始剧烈摆动，持续了约五分钟，怎么也稳不住。降落伞临近地面的第一个迹象，是下方空间渐渐转暗。雪已转雨，伞虽湿透，摆幅却已大为收敛。我把那支照距500英尺的手电向下照去，可地面来得太突然，我一头落在铁丝网顶上，压根没看见它。围栏帮我卸去了坠势，倒刺也没能扎透我厚重的飞行服。降落伞被风吹过围栏，在一阵阵狂风里张了许久才瘪下来。

===Original===
After rolling the chute into its pack I started towards the nearest light. I soon came to a road, walked about a mile to the town of Covell, IL, and telephoned a report to St. Louis. The only information I could obtain in regard to the crashed plane was from one of a group of farmers in the general store, who stated that his neighbor had heard the plane crash but could only guess at its general direction. An hour's search proved without avail. I left instructions to place a guard over the mail in case the plane was found before I returned and went to Chicago for another ship. On arriving over Covell the next morning I found the wreck with a small crowd gathered around it, less than 500 feet back of the house where I had left my parachute the night before. The nose and the wheels had struck the ground at the same time, and after sliding along for about 75 feet it had piled up in a pasture beside a hedge fence. One wheel had come off and was standing inflated against the wall on the inside of a hog house a hundred yards further on. It had gone through two fences and the wall of the house. The wings were badly splintered, but the tubular fuselage, although badly bent in places, had held its general form even in the mail pit. The parachute from the flare was hanging on the tailskid.

===Chinese===
我把降落伞卷进伞包，朝最近的灯光走去。不久便上了公路，步行约一英里到了伊利诺伊州的科维尔镇（Covell），给圣路易斯挂电话报了情况。关于坠毁的飞机，我唯一能打探到的消息来自杂货店里的一群农夫：其中一位说，他的邻居听见了飞机坠地，却只能估摸个大概方向。搜了一个小时，一无所获。我留下指示：万一在我回来之前找到飞机，务必派人看守邮件；随后我便去芝加哥另取飞机。第二天早晨飞临科维尔上空时，我找到了残骸，旁边围着一小群人——就在我前一晚寄存降落伞的那座房子后面不到500英尺处。机头和机轮同时触地，贴地滑了约75英尺后，在树篱围栏旁的一片牧场上撞作一团。一只机轮甩脱，完好充着气，立在百码开外一间猪舍的内墙边；它一路撞穿了两道围栏和猪舍的墙。机翼碎裂得厉害，管构机身虽有多处严重弯折，却大体保住了原形，连邮舱也不例外。照明弹的降落伞还挂在尾橇上。

===Original===
There were three sacks of mail in the plane. One, a full bag from St. Louis, had been split open and some of the mail oil-soaked but legible. The other two bags were only partially full and were undamaged.

===Chinese===
机上有三袋邮件。一袋是从圣路易斯来的满袋，被撕裂了口，部分邮件浸了油但仍可辨认。另外两袋只装了半满，完好无损。

===Original===
It was just about at this time, or shortly after, that I first began to think about a New York–Paris flight. But before discussing the events leading up to that flight, it might be well to say a few words about the future possibilities of commercial aviation.

===Chinese===
正是在这段时期前后，我头一回开始琢磨纽约—巴黎飞行的事。不过在讲述促成那次飞行的一段经过之前，不妨先就商业航空日后的前景说上几句。

===Original===
In comparing aviation to other forms of transportation it should be born in mind that the flying machine has been in existence less than twenty-five years. The Wright Brothers made their first flight at Kitty Hawk, North Carolina, in 1903. Yet in 1927 air liners are operating regularly over long distances and under all conditions.

===Chinese===
把航空同其他交通方式相比较时，应当记住：飞行机器问世尚不足二十五年。莱特兄弟于1903年在北卡罗来纳州基蒂霍克（Kitty Hawk）完成了首次飞行。然而到了1927年，定期航线客机已在长途和各种条件下常态运营。

===Original===
The first airplane was a frail machine capable of operation only in good weather. Even with the utmost care, flying in the early days of aviation was a dangerous profession at best.

===Chinese===
最初的飞机是一种脆弱的机器，只配在好天气里飞行。即便小心翼翼，航空早期的飞行充其量也是一门高风险的行当。

===Original===
Today the properly operated commercial airline compares favorably in safety with any other means of transportation.

===Chinese===
今天，经营得当的商业航线，其安全性足以同任何其他交通方式一较短长。

===Original===
Shipping has reached its present stage after thousands of years of development. Railroads, less than a century ago, stopped their trains at night on the grounds that operation in darkness was unsafe. Automobiles, after nearly forty years of progress, are still dependent on good roads.

===Chinese===
航运是历经几千年发展才达到今天的水平。不到一个世纪之前，铁路还曾以黑暗中行车不安全为由，一到夜里便停开火车。汽车发展了近四十年，仍离不开好路。

===Original===
The airplane, in less than quarter of a century, has taken its place among the most important methods of travel and now, where time is paramount and territory inaccessible, it stands at the head of its competition.

===Chinese===
而飞机在不到四分之一个世纪里，便已跻身最重要的旅行方式之列；如今，在时间至上、地域闭塞之处，它更是傲视同侪。

===Original===
Development up to the present time has been largely military. The cost of aeronautical engineering and construction has been so great that commercial companies have not been able to afford to experiment with their own designs. While the airplane was still an experiment the financial returns from aeronautical projects were only too often less than the cost of operation. Consequently the early development was largely sponsored by the government, with the result that the planes were designed for use in warfare rather than for safety and economy of operation. Extreme safety, in the military machine, must be sacrificed for maneuverability. Economy of operation was replaced by military design.

===Chinese===
迄今为止的发展主要是军事性的。航空工程与制造的耗费如此之巨，商业公司无力拿自己的设计去做试验。在飞机尚属试验品的年代，航空项目的财务回报往往还抵不上运营成本。因此早期发展主要由政府资助，结果是飞机都为战争而设计，而非为安全与运营的经济性设计。在军用机器上，极致的安全必须为机动性让路；运营经济性则让位于军用设计。

===Original===
Commercial aviation, in the United States, has been retarded in the past by lack of government subsidy, but the very lack of that subsidy will be one of its greatest assets in the future. A subsidized airline is organized with the subsidy as a very large consideration. The organization exists on the subsidy and its growth is regulated by the subsidy. Years will be required before the point of independence is reached and the receipts become larger than the expenditures.

===Chinese===
在美国，商业航空过去因缺少政府补贴而受掣肘，但恰恰是这份补贴的缺席，将成为它日后最大的资产之一。受补贴的航空公司是围着补贴这桩大买卖组建的：组织靠补贴维持，增长也由补贴调节。要达到自立的临界点、要收入超过支出，非得熬上多年不可。

===Original===
On the other hand, an airline organized without regard to an external income is in a position to expand along with the demands for service. If the traffic becomes great enough to require more or bigger planes, a larger profit ensues, instead of an increased subsidy being required or the fare being raised to hold down the demand.

===Chinese===
反观一家不仰赖外部收入的航空公司，便能随服务需求的增长而扩张。一旦运量大到需要更多或更大的飞机，接踵而至的是更丰厚的利润，而不是追加补贴，也不是抬价压需求。

===Original===
The airplane has now advanced to the stage where the demands of commerce are sufficient to warrant the building of planes without regard to military usefulness. And with the advent of the purely commercial airplane comes an economy of operation which places operating organizations on a sound financial basis.

===Chinese===
飞机如今已进步到这般境地：商业需求足以支撑飞机的制造，而无须顾虑其军事用途。随着纯商用飞机的问世，一种运营经济性随之而来，使运营企业立于健全的财务基础之上。

===Original===
Undoubtedly in a few years the United States will be covered with a network of passenger, mail and express lines.

===Chinese===
毫无疑问，几年之内，合众国将被一张客运、邮运与快运线路的网络覆盖。

===Original===
Transatlantic service is still in the future. Extensive research and careful study will be required before any regular schedule between America and Europe can be maintained. Multi-motored flying boats with stations along the route will eventually make transoceanic airlines practical but their development must be based on a solid foundation of experience and equipment.

===Chinese===
跨大西洋航运尚在未来。要在美洲与欧洲之间维持任何定期班期，还需广泛的研究与审慎的筹划。多发动机飞船配上沿线航站，终将使跨洋航线成为现实，但其发展必须建立在经验与装备的坚实根基之上。

## IV Washington / 四 华盛顿

===Original===
It is probable that when Lindbergh reached America he got the greatest welcome any man in history has ever received; certainly the greatest when judged by numbers; and by far the greatest in its freedom from that unkind emotion which in such cases usually springs from one people's triumph over another.

===Chinese===
很可能，林德伯格抵达美国时所受的欢迎，是历史上任何人都不曾得到过的；论人数肯定是最多的；而最难得的是，它全然不见那种在一个民族战胜另一个民族时通常随之而生的刻薄情绪。

===Original===
Lindbergh's victory was all victory; for it was not internecine, but that of our human species over the elements against which for thousands of centuries man's weakness has been pitted.

===Chinese===
林德伯格的胜利是纯粹的胜利；因为它并非同类的自相残杀，而是我们人类对大自然的胜利——千万个世纪以来，人类的孱弱一直与大自然苦苦相搏。

===Original===
The striking part of it all was that a composite picture of past homecoming heroes wouldn't look any more like Charles Lindbergh did that day of his arrival in Washington than a hitching post looks like a green bay tree.

===Chinese===
这一切中最惊人的是：把历代凯旋归来的英雄拼成一幅合成的画像，也不会比一根拴马桩更像繁茂的月桂树那样，更像抵达华盛顿那天的查尔斯·林德伯格。

===Original===
Caesar was glum when he came back from Gaul; Napoleon grim; Paul Jones defiant; Peary blunt; Roosevelt abrupt; Dewey deferential; Wilson brooding; Pershing imposing. Lindbergh was none of these. He was a plain citizen dressed in the garments of an everyday man. He looked thoroughly pleased, just a little surprised, and about as full of health and spirits as any normal man of his age should be. If there was any wild emotion or bewilderment in the occasion it lay in the welcoming crowds, and not in the air pilot they were saluting.

===Chinese===
恺撒从高卢归来时面色阴沉；拿破仑冷峻严酷；保罗·琼斯桀骜不驯；皮尔里直率生硬；罗斯福急切直截；杜威恭谨谦抑；威尔逊沉思忧郁；潘兴威严赫赫。林德伯格全不是这样。他是个穿着寻常衣装的平民百姓。他看起来满心欢喜，略带惊讶，浑身上下的健康与朝气，正像他这个年纪的正常人该有的样子。如果说这场面里有什么狂热的情绪或惶惑，那都在欢迎的人群之中，而不在他们致敬的这位飞行员身上。

===Original===
The cruiser *Memphis*, on which Lindbergh travelled, passed through the Virginia Capes on her way to Washington a few minutes after five p.m. of the afternoon of June 10. Here Lindbergh got the first taste of what was to come.

===Chinese===
载着林德伯格的"孟菲斯"号巡洋舰，于 6 月 10 日下午五点过几分驶过弗吉尼亚角（Virginia Capes），取道前往华盛顿。在这里，林德伯格初次尝到了即将到来的一切的滋味。

===Original===
A convoy of four destroyers, two army blimps from Langley Field and forty airplanes of the Army, Navy and Marine Corps accompanied the vessel as she steamed up Chesapeake Bay. As the night fell they wheeled toward their various bases and were soon lost to view. They gave no salute; and, for all the casual observer might have noted, they were merely investigating this newcomer to their home waters. But they left an indelible impression upon those in the *Memphis* that the morrow was to be extraordinary.

===Chinese===
四艘驱逐舰、两艘来自兰利机场（Langley Field）的陆军软式飞艇，以及四十架陆军、海军和海军陆战队的飞机，一路护送这艘军舰驶上切萨皮克湾（Chesapeake Bay）。夜幕降临时，它们转向各自的基地，很快没入暮色。它们没有行礼；在漫不经心的旁观者看来，它们不过是来查看一下闯入自家水域的这个新客。但它们给"孟菲斯"号上的人留下了不可磨灭的印象：明天将是不平凡的一天。

===Original===
Saturday June 11, 1927, dawned hot and clear in Washington. It was evident early in the day that something far out of the city's peaceful summer routine was going to happen. Streets were being roped off. Special policemen were going to their posts. Airplanes flew about overhead. Citizens began gathering in little clumps up and down Pennsylvania Avenue, many seating themselves on fruit boxes and baskets as if for a long wait.

===Chinese===
1927 年 6 月 11 日，星期六，华盛顿的清晨炎热而晴朗。一大早就看得出，一件远超出这座首都平静夏日惯例的事情即将发生。街道拉起了警戒绳，特派警察各就各位，飞机在头顶盘旋。市民们开始在宾夕法尼亚大道（Pennsylvania Avenue）沿线成小群地聚集，许多人坐在水果箱和篮子上，仿佛准备长久等候。

===Original===
The din that greeted the *Memphis* off Alexandria, suburb of Washington, began the noisy welcome that lasted for several hours. Every roof top, window, old ship, wharf and factory floor was filled with those who simply had to see Lindbergh come home. Factory whistles, automobiles, church bells and fire sirens all joined in the pandemonium.

===Chinese===
"孟菲斯"号驶经华盛顿近郊亚历山德里亚（Alexandria）时迎来的喧腾，拉开了持续数小时的喧闹欢迎的序幕。每一处屋顶、窗口、旧船、码头和厂房屋顶，都挤满了非要看林德伯格归家不可的人。工厂汽笛、汽车喇叭、教堂钟声和消防警笛，一齐汇入这片鼎沸。

===Original===
In the air were scores of aircraft. One large squadron of nearly fifty pursuit planes maneuvered in and out of the heavy vaporous clouds that hung over the river. Beneath them moved several flights of slower bombers. The giant dirigible airship, the U.S.S. *Los Angeles*, wound back and forth above the course of the oncoming *Memphis*.

===Chinese===
空中有几十架飞机。一个由近五十架驱逐机组成的大型中队，在笼罩河面的浓密云霭间穿梭机动。它们下方，几队速度较慢的轰炸机列队而行。巨型硬式飞艇"洛杉矶"号（U.S.S. Los Angeles），在驶来的"孟菲斯"号航线上空往返巡曳。

===Original===
By eleven o'clock the saluting began. Vice Admiral Burrage, also returning on the *Memphis*, received his customary fifteen guns from the Navy yard. The President's salute of 21 guns was exchanged. Firing from the cruisers' battery and from the shore stations lent a fine rhythmic punctuation to the constantly increasing noise from other quarters.

===Chinese===
到十一点，礼炮开始了。同乘"孟菲斯"号归来的伯拉奇海军中将（Vice Admiral Burrage）照例接受了海军造船厂的 15 响礼炮，总统的 21 响礼炮也互相应答。巡洋舰舰炮和岸上炮台的轰鸣，为四面八方不断增强的喧声添上了铿锵的节拍。

===Original===
Just before noon the *Memphis* came alongside the Navy Yard dock and a gangplank was hoisted to her rail. On the shore were collected a notable group of cabinet officers and high officials. There were the Secretary of the Navy, Curtis D. Wilbur; the Secretary of War, Dwight F. Davis; Postmaster General Harry S. New; and former Secretary of State, Charles Evans Hughes. There were Admiral Edward W. Eberle, Chief of Naval Operations; Major General Mason W. Patrick and Rear Admiral William A. Moffett, heads of the Army and Navy air forces. There was Commander Richard E. Byrd who flew to the North Pole, and who later followed Lindbergh's trail to France.

===Chinese===
临近正午，"孟菲斯"号靠上海军造船厂码头，一块跳板被吊上舷侧。岸上聚集着一群显赫的内阁成员和高级官员：海军部长柯蒂斯·D·威尔伯（Curtis D. Wilbur）、陆军部长德怀特·F·戴维斯（Dwight F. Davis）、邮政总长哈里·S·纽（Harry S. New），以及前国务卿查尔斯·埃文斯·休斯（Charles Evans Hughes）；有海军作战部长爱德华·W·埃伯利上将（Admiral Edward W. Eberle）、陆军与海军航空部队的首脑梅森·W·帕特里克少将（Major General Mason W. Patrick）和威廉·A·莫菲特海军少将（Rear Admiral William A. Moffett）；还有飞越北极、随后又循林德伯格的航迹飞往法国的理查德·E·伯德中校（Commander Richard E. Byrd）。

===Original===
When the gangplank was in place Admiral Burrage came down it and a moment later returned with a lady on his arm. This lady was Mrs. Evangeline Lindbergh, the young pilot's mother.

===Chinese===
跳板放稳后，伯拉奇上将走下船去，片刻之后挽着一位夫人回来了。这位夫人便是伊万杰琳·林德伯格夫人（Mrs. Evangeline Lindbergh），年轻飞行员的母亲。

===Original===
Instantly a new burst of cheering went up; but many wept—they knew not just why.

===Chinese===
一阵新的欢呼骤然响起；但许多人流下泪来——他们自己也说不清为什么。

===Original===
For a few minutes mother and son disappeared into a cabin aboard the *Memphis*. It was a nice touch; something more than the brass bands and cheering. And it somehow symbolized a great deal of what was being felt and said that hot morning in our country's great capital.

===Chinese===
有那么几分钟，母子俩消失在"孟菲斯"号的一间舱房里。这是动人的一幕，有着铜管乐与欢呼之外更深的东西。它似乎象征着那个炎热的上午，在我们伟大首都里人们心中所感、口中所说的许多许多。

===Original===
Next came brief and a somewhat informal greeting by the dignitaries. In their glistening high silk hats they surrounded Lindbergh and for a bit shut him off from the pushing perspiring crowd still held at bay ashore by the bayonets of the marines.

===Chinese===
接下来是达官显贵们简短而略带随意的一番问候。他们戴着亮闪闪的大礼帽，把林德伯格围在中间，一时将他同岸上仍在推挤、汗流浃背的人群隔开——那人群还靠海军陆战队的刺刀拦在线外。

===Original===
Suddenly the crowd could hold its patience no longer. With one frantic push it broke through the ranks of "Devil Dogs" and swarmed down upon the moored vessel. Trouble was averted by the simple expedient of getting Lindbergh quickly into one of the waiting cars and starting for the Navy Yard gate.

===Chinese===
人群突然再也无法按捺。一阵狂热的推挤，他们冲破了"魔鬼狗"们（Devil Dogs）的队列，涌向系泊的军舰。好在办法简单：人们迅速把林德伯格塞进一辆等候的汽车，朝海军造船厂大门驶去，这才避免了乱子。

===Original===
The parade escort had been lined up some hours ahead of time. Now it got under way toward the center of the city, leading the automobiles that carried the official party. Clattering hoofs of cavalrymen, blare of bands and a rolling cheer along the ranks of waiting thousands marked the progress of the young American flier who had so gloriously come home.

===Chinese===
仪仗队几个小时前就已列队完毕。此刻队伍向市中心进发，走在载着官方一行人的汽车前头。骑兵的马蹄嗒嗒作响，乐队鼓号齐鸣，等候的万千人列中欢声滚过——这一切标记着这位如此荣光归来的美国青年飞行家的行进。

===Original===
Here for the first time Lindbergh saw the spirit in which his people were to greet him. They were curious, yes; crowds always are on such occasions. And they were gay with their handclapping and flag-waving, shouting and confetti throwing. But there was a note of enthusiasm everywhere that transcended just a chorus of holiday seekers witnessing a new form of circus. There was something deeper and finer in the way people voiced their acclaim. Many of them wiped their eyes while they laughed; many stood with expressionless faces, their looks glued upon the face of the lad who had achieved so great a thing and yet seemed to take it all so calmly.

===Chinese===
在这里，林德伯格第一次看到了他的人民迎接他的精神。他们好奇，是的；这种场合下人群总是好奇的。他们也欢腾，鼓掌挥旗，欢呼着抛洒彩带。但处处都有一股热情，超出了凑热闹的人群围观一种新型马戏的合唱。人们表达赞颂的方式里，有某种更深沉、更美好的东西。许多人一边笑一边拭泪；许多人面无表情地伫立着，目光紧紧盯着这个年轻人的脸——他成就了如此伟大的事业，看上去却如此平静地对待这一切。

===Original===
When the parade reached the natural amphitheatre of the Washington Monument the hillsides were jammed with a great gathering of men, women and children. On the high stand that had been erected, the President of the United States and Mrs. Coolidge waited to receive the man who but three weeks and a day before had been a comparatively unknown adventurer hopping off for Paris by air.

===Chinese===
游行队伍抵达华盛顿纪念碑（Washington Monument）天然圆形剧场般的场地时，山坡上挤满了聚会的男女老幼。在高搭的主席台上，合众国总统与柯立芝夫人正等候迎接这个人——而不过三个星期零一天之前，他还只是个驾机飞往巴黎的、默默无闻的冒险者。

===Original===
Ranged about the President were the ambassadors of many foreign countries, members of the diplomatic corps with their wives and daughters, and nearly all the high officials of the government.

===Chinese===
总统四周，环立着许多国家的大使、携妻带女的外交使团成员，以及政府的几乎全体高级官员。

===Original===
When Lindbergh mounted the stand the President came forward and grasped his hand. Those closest to Mr. Coolidge say that rarely has he shown the unrestrained cordiality he put into that simple greeting.

===Chinese===
林德伯格登上主席台时，总统迎上前来，握住了他的手。与柯立芝先生最接近的人说，这样不设防的亲切，他极少展露过。

===Original===
The President now moved to the front of the stand and waited for the applause to be stilled. Presently, when the multitude again was quiet, he began slowly to speak:

===Chinese===
总统随即走到主席台前，等待掌声平息。片刻之后，人群重归安静，他缓缓开口：

===Original===
"My Fellow-Countrymen:

===Chinese===
"我的同胞们：

===Original===
"It was in America that the modern art of flying of heavier-than-air machines was first developed. As the experiments became successful, the airplane was devoted to practical purposes. It has been adapted to commerce in the transportation of passengers and mail and used for national defense by our land and sea forces.

===Chinese===
"正是在美国，重于空气的飞行器的现代飞行技术最先发展起来。随着试验取得成功，飞机被用于实际用途：它在商业上承担乘客与邮件的运输，也被我们的陆海军用作国防力量。

===Original===
"Beginning with a limited flying radius, its length has been gradually extended. We have made many flying records. Our Army fliers have circumnavigated the globe. One of our Navy men started from California and flew far enough to have reached Hawaii, but being off his course, landed in the water. Another officer of the Navy has flown to the North Pole. Our own country has been traversed from shore to shore in a single flight.

===Chinese===
"从有限的飞行半径起步，航程已逐渐延长。我们创造了许多飞行纪录。我们的陆军飞行员完成了环球飞行。我们的一位海军飞行员从加利福尼亚起飞，飞出的距离本已足够到达夏威夷，却因偏离航向落入了水中。另一位海军军官飞到了北极。我们自己的国家，已被一次飞行从海岸到海岸横贯。

===Original===
"It had been apparent for some time that the next great feat in the air would be a continuous flight from the mainland of America to the mainland of Europe. Two courageous Frenchmen made the reverse attempt and passed to a fate that is as yet unknown.

===Chinese===
"一段时间以来人们已经明白，空中下一桩伟业，将是从美洲大陆到欧洲大陆的不间断飞行。两位勇敢的法国人作了相反方向的尝试，随后遭际如何，至今无人知晓。

===Original===
"Others were speeding their preparations to make the trial, but it remained for an unknown youth to attempt the elements and win. It is the same story of valor and victory by a son of the people that shines through every page of American history.

===Chinese===
"别的人也在加紧准备一试身手，但最终直面风浪并赢得胜利的，却是一位无名的青年。这正是人民的儿子以勇毅取胜的故事，它辉映着美国历史的每一页。

===Original===
"Twenty-five years ago there was born in Detroit, Michigan, a boy representing the best traditions of this country, of a stock known for its deeds of adventure and exploration.

===Chinese===
"二十五年前，密歇根州底特律诞生了一个男孩，他体现了这个国家最优秀的传统，出身于一个以冒险与开拓著称的族系。

===Original===
"His father, moved with a desire for public service, was a member of Congress for several years. His mother, who dowered her son with her own modesty and charm, is with us today. Engaged in the vital profession of school-teaching, she has permitted neither money nor fame to interfere with her fidelity to her duties.

===Chinese===
"他的父亲怀着公益之志，连任数年国会议员。他的母亲，把自己谦逊温婉的天性传给了儿子，今天也在我们中间。她从事着教书育人这一重要职业，从不容许金钱或名誉干扰她对职责的恪守。

===Original===
"Too young to have enlisted in the World War, her son became a student at one of the big State universities. His interest in aviation led him to an Army aviation school, and in 1925 he was graduated as an airplane pilot. In November, 1926, he had reached the rank of Captain in the Officers' Reserve Corps.

===Chinese===
"因年纪太轻未能参加世界大战，她的儿子进入了一所大型州立大学就读。对航空的兴趣把他引向一所陆军航空学校，1925 年他毕业成为飞机驾驶员。1926 年 11 月，他已获得军官预备队（Officers' Reserve Corps）的上尉军衔。

===Original===
"Making his home in St. Louis, he had joined the 110th Observation Squadron of the Missouri National Guard. Some of his qualities noted by the Army officers who examined him for promotion, as shown by reports in the files of the Militia Bureau of the War Department, are as follows:

===Chinese===
"他定居圣路易斯，加入了密苏里国民警卫队第 110 侦察中队。陆军军官在考核他晋升时记下的他的一些品质，据陆军部民兵局档案中的报告所载，如下：

===Original===
" 'Intelligent,' 'industrious,' 'energetic,' 'dependable,' 'purposeful,' 'alert,' 'quick of reaction,' 'serious,' 'deliberate,' 'stable,' 'efficient,' 'frank,' 'modest,' 'congenial' 'a man of good moral habits and regular in all his business transactions.'

===Chinese===
"'聪慧'、'勤勉'、'干练'、'可靠'、'有决断'、'机敏'、'反应迅捷'、'认真'、'沉稳'、'坚定'、'高效'、'坦率'、'谦逊'、'随和'，'品行端正，商事往来恪守规矩'。

===Original===
"One of the officers expressed his belief that the young man 'would successfully complete everything he undertakes.' This reads like a prophecy.

===Chinese===
"其中一位军官表示相信，这个年轻人'担当之事必能善成'。这话读来竟像预言。

===Original===
"Later he became connected with the United States Mail Service, where he exhibited marked ability, and from which he is now on leave of absence.

===Chinese===
"后来他任职于美国航空邮政，表现出众，目前正告假在外。

===Original===
"On a morning just three weeks ago yesterday this wholesome, earnest, fearless, courageous product of America rose into the air from Long Island in a monoplane christened the *Spirit of St. Louis* in honor of his home and that of his supporters.

===Chinese===
"就在三周前的昨天早晨，这个健朗、诚挚、无畏、勇敢的美国之子，驾驶一架以他和他支持者的家园命名的单翼机——"圣路易斯精神号"——从长岛腾空而起。

===Original===
"It was no haphazard adventure. After months of most careful preparation, supported by a valiant character, driven by an unconquerable will and inspired by the imagination and the spirit of his Viking ancestors, this reserve officer set wing across the dangerous stretches of the North Atlantic.

===Chinese===
"这不是一次贸然的冒险。经过数月精心的准备，以英勇的品格为支撑，以不可征服的意志为动力，以他北欧先祖的想象力和精神为激励，这位预备役军官展翅飞越了北大西洋的险恶水域。

===Original===
"He was alone. His destination was Paris.

===Chinese===
"他孑然一身。目的地是巴黎。

===Original===
"Thirty-three hours and thirty minutes later, in the evening of the second day, he landed at his destination on the French flying field at Le Bourget. He had traveled over 3,600 miles, and established a new and remarkable record. The execution of his project was a perfect exhibition of art.

===Chinese===
"三十三小时三十分钟之后，第二天入夜时分，他在目的地法国勒布尔热机场降落。他飞了 3,600 多英里，创造了一项崭新而出色的纪录。整个计划的执行，是一场完美技艺的展示。

===Original===
"This country will always remember the way in which he was received by the people of France, by their President and by their Government. It was the more remarkable because they were mourning the disappearance of their intrepid countrymen, who had tried to span the Atlantic on a western flight.

===Chinese===
"这个国家将永远铭记法兰西人民、他们的总统和政府迎接他的方式。尤其难能可贵的是，当时他们正怀着悲痛，悼念那些尝试向西飞越大西洋而无故失踪的英勇同胞。

===Original===
"Our messenger of peace and goodwill had broken down another barrier of time and space and brought two great peoples into closer communion. In less than a day and a half he had crossed the ocean over which Columbus had traveled for sixty-nine days and the Pilgrim Fathers for sixty-six days on their way to the New World.

===Chinese===
"我们的和平与亲善使者，又打破了一道时间与空间的屏障，使两个伟大的民族更加心意相通。不到一天半，他便渡过了那片大洋——当年哥伦布在上面航行了 69 天，清教徒移民先驱前往新大陆时走了 66 天。

===Original===
"But, above all, in showering applause and honors upon this genial, modest American youth, with the naturalness, the simplicity and the poise of true greatness, France had the opportunity to show clearly her goodwill for America and our people.

===Chinese===
"而最重要的是，法兰西把掌声与荣耀洒向这位开朗谦逊的美国青年——他有着真正伟人的自然、质朴与沉静——法兰西也由此得以清楚地表明它对美国和美国人民的善意。

===Original===
"With like acclaim and evidences of cordial friendship our Ambassador without portfolio was received by the rulers, the Governments and the peoples of England and Belgium. From other nations came hearty messages of admiration for him and for his country. For these manifold evidences of friendship we are profoundly grateful.

===Chinese===
"我们的"无任所大使"在英格兰和比利时，同样受到君主、政府和人民的欢迎，收获了同样热忱的友谊。其他国家也纷纷发来诚挚的贺辞，赞美他、赞美他的国家。对这些情谊的种种明证，我们深怀感激。

===Original===
"The absence of self-acclaim, the refusal to become commercialized, which has marked the conduct of this sincere and genuine exemplar of fine and noble virtues, has endeared him to everyone. He has returned unspoiled.

===Chinese===
"不自我夸耀，拒绝商业化——这位诚挚而纯良的美德典范一贯如此，也因此受到了所有人的爱戴。他归来时，初心未染。

===Original===
"Particularly has it been delightful to have him refer to his airplane as somehow possessing a personality and being equally entitled to credit with himself, for we are proud that in every particular this silent partner represented American genius and industry. I am told that more than 100 separate companies furnished materials, parts or service in its construction.

===Chinese===
"尤其令人欣慰的是，他谈起自己的飞机时，仿佛它也有性情，与他自己同样功不可没。我们深感自豪的是，这位无言的搭档在每一处细节上都代表着美国的天才与工业。我得知，参与其制造的独立厂商超过 100 家，提供了材料、部件或服务。

===Original===
"And now, my fellow-citizens, this young man has returned. He is here. He has brought his unsullied fame home. It is our great privilege to welcome back to his native land, on behalf of his own people, who have a deep affection for him and have been thrilled by his splendid achievement, a Colonel of the United States Officers' Reserve Corps, an illustrious citizen of our Republic, a conqueror of the air and strength for the ties which bind us to our sister nations across the sea.

===Chinese===
"而此刻，我的同胞们，这个年轻人回来了。他就在这里。他把无损的荣光带回了家。他的同胞对他怀着深厚的感情，为他的辉煌成就而心潮激荡；我们荣幸地代表他们，欢迎这位美国军官预备队上校、我们共和国的杰出公民、天空的征服者、联结我们与海外姊妹民族情谊的加固者，回到祖国的土地。

===Original===
"And, as President of the United States, I bestow the Distinguished Flying Cross, as a symbol of appreciation for what he is and what he has done, upon Colonel Charles A. Lindbergh."

===Chinese===
"并且，以合众国总统的身份，我将杰出飞行十字勋章（Distinguished Flying Cross）授予查尔斯·A·林德伯格上校，以表达对他其人其行的敬意。"

===Original===
Upon completing this address the President then conferred upon Lindbergh the Distinguished Flying Cross.

===Chinese===
演讲完毕，总统随即为林德伯格佩上了杰出飞行十字勋章。

===Original===
A new burst of cheering went up as the medal was being pinned on by the President. It was at this point in the proceedings that the Secretary of the Navy, ordinarily most placid of men, is alleged to have waved his arm in the air like a college cheer leader and hurrahed as loudly as any. When quiet came again Lindbergh rose and replied to the President. What he said was brief. But had he uttered a hundred times as many words, he could scarcely have conveyed a more important message to those about him.

===Chinese===
总统亲手别上勋章时，又一阵欢呼腾起。据说正是在这时，平日最沉静的海军部长，像大学的啦啦队长一样朝空中挥舞手臂，放声欢呼不落人后。待喧声复归平静，林德伯格起身作答。他的话很短。但即便他说上一百倍的话，也未必能传递给周围的人更重要的信息。

===Original===
He said: "On the evening of May 21, I arrived at Le Bourget, France. I was in Paris for one week, in Belgium for a day and was in London and in England for several days. Everywhere I went, at every meeting I attended, I was requested to bring a message home to you. Always the message was the same.

===Chinese===
他说："5 月 21 日晚，我抵达法国勒布尔热。我在巴黎待了一周，在比利时待了一天，又在伦敦和英格兰待了几天。我所到之处、所赴的每一次聚会，人们都托我带一个口信回家给你们。这口信始终是同一句。

===Original===
" 'You have seen,' the message was, 'the affection of the people of France for the people of America demonstrated to you. When you return to America take back that message to the people of the United States from the people of France and of Europe.'

===Chinese===
"'你已亲眼看到，'那口信说，'法兰西人民对美洲人民的感情。当你返回美国时，请把法兰西人民和欧洲人民的这一心声，带给合众国的人民。'

===Original===
"I thank you."

===Chinese===
"谢谢你们。"

===Original===
This is no place to dwell upon the minutiae of that great day. The picture must be sketched in with bold strokes and stippled background. But it is impossible to pass this one short speech of Lindbergh's and not cajole the reader to gather something of its significance. In a sentence it tells the story of the flight; it gives what the speaker considered his immediate and outstanding achievement; and it phrases that achievement in words so touching and so eloquent that France and America, half-estranged through wretched debt, rang with them for days.

===Chinese===
这里不是细述那个伟大日子枝节的场合。这幅画卷只能以粗犷的笔触和点染的背景勾勒。但林德伯格这一篇短短的讲话，实在无法轻轻略过而不劝请读者体会它的分量。它一句话讲完了飞行始末；道出了演讲者心目中最直接、最了不起的成就；而那措辞如此动人、如此雄辩，以致因债务纠葛而半生嫌隙的法兰西与美国，都为这几句话回响了多日。

===Original===
The final touch of the miracle was that this speech was extemporaneous.

===Chinese===
这奇迹的最后一笔是：这篇演讲是即兴的。

===Original===
Just as when Lincoln finished his Gettysburg address his listeners sat stunned at the very brevity of it, so was there a curious silence immediately following Lindbergh's utterance. Then came long applause. Hats were not thrown in the air. But men and women clapped until their palms were numb. Again many wept. A radio announcer whose stock-in-trade was routine emotional appeal, broke down and sobbed.

===Chinese===
正如林肯念完葛底斯堡演说时，听众为其异常简短而愕然默坐一样，林德伯格话音落下，紧接着也是一阵奇异的沉默。然后是经久的掌声。没有人把帽子抛向空中，但男男女女一直鼓掌，掌心都拍麻了。又有许多人落泪。一位以煽情为看家本领的电台播音员，竟失声啜泣起来。

===Original===
More and more people were beginning to realize that something was happening far greater than just the celebration of a mechanical triumph over the ocean separating Europe from America.

===Chinese===
越来越多的人开始意识到：正在发生的事情，远远不止是庆祝一场跨越欧美之间大洋的机械胜利。

===Original===
The ceremony ended as simply and quickly as it had begun. The President's own car whisked Lindbergh away to the temporary White House in Dupont Circle. A curious and eager crowd lingered there behind police lines throughout the afternoon. From time to time their demanding cheers could be silenced only by Lindbergh's smiling presence at the door or balcony.

===Chinese===
典礼像开始时一样简朴而迅速地结束了。总统的专车把林德伯格载往杜邦圆环（Dupont Circle）的临时白宫。整个下午，好奇而热切的人群在警察警戒线后流连不去。他们的欢呼一浪高过一浪，往往只有林德伯格含笑出现在门口或阳台上，才能平息。

===Original===
President and Mrs. Coolidge entertained members of the Cabinet and their wives that night. Lindbergh sat on Mrs. Coolidge's right. He wore conventional evening dress and was distinguished by the ease and simplicity with which he met both sallies and inquiries of the imposing guests.

===Chinese===
当晚，柯立芝总统和夫人设宴款待内阁成员及诸位夫人。林德伯格坐在柯立芝夫人右手边。他穿着常规的晚礼服，应对显赫宾客们的俏皮话与种种提问时那份从容平易，令满座瞩目。

===Original===
It is one of the cruelties of social lionization that we search for the peculiarities of our specimen. In Lindbergh's case his peculiarity lay in the fact that neither by word, nor look, nor deed was he in any way grotesque. His eyes were clear, his smile quick; like a practised diplomat he eluded entangling discussion; and he had a ready reply for every intelligent inquiry put to him within his range of knowledge or experience.

===Chinese===
社交界的追捧有一桩残酷之处：我们总要在自己的偶像身上搜寻怪癖。而林德伯格的"怪癖"恰恰在于：无论言语、神色还是举止，他没有任何怪诞之处。他目光澄澈，笑容轻快；像老练的外交官一样避开纠缠不清的争论；凡在其知识或阅历范围之内的明智提问，他都对答如流。

===Original===
It is at risk of dampening the ardor of our narrative that we repeatedly point to this trait of simplicity that lies in Lindbergh. We do so because it was from close within the nucleus of this trait that there sprung the incredible emotional reaction towards his personality.

===Chinese===
再三指出林德伯格这一质朴的品格，或许会使我们叙事的热度有所减损。我们仍要如此，因为正是从这一品格的核心深处，进发出了世人对他人格的那场难以置信的情感反应。

===Original===
After the President's dinner Lindbergh attended a meeting of the National Press Club in the Washington Auditorium. This was his first public appearance "under roof" in America. Six thousand people risked imminent heat stroke by crowding into every seat and cranny of the building.

===Chinese===
总统晚宴之后，林德伯格出席了国家新闻俱乐部（National Press Club）在华盛顿礼堂举行的集会。这是他在美国的第一次"室内"公开露面。六千人挤满了大厦的每个座位和角落，中暑晕倒的风险全然不顾。

===Original===
The program opened with an address on behalf of the Press Club by Richard V. Oulahan. Because this address illuminated the feelings of the "Fourth Estate," proverbially cynical toward notoriety, we give it here in full:

===Chinese===
活动以理查德·V·奥拉汉（Richard V. Oulahan）代表新闻俱乐部的致词开场。这篇致词道破了向来以冷眼看浮名的"第四等级"（Fourth Estate）的心声，故全文照录于此：

===Original===
"In your journalistic flight of the past three weeks," said Mr. Oulahan, "you must have learned that much may be read between the lines of what is printed in newspapers. So even a novice in newspaperdom like yourself would have no trouble in reading between the lines of this journalistic expression an intimate note of sincere affection.

===Chinese===
"在过去三个星期的新闻之旅中，"奥拉汉先生说，"您必定已经领教：报纸字里行间大有可读之物。所以，即便是您这样初入新闻界的新手，也不难从我这篇新闻界言辞的字里行间，读出一缕亲切而真挚的情谊。

===Original===
"We of the press rub elbows with all manner of mankind. We see much of good but we see much of self-seeking, of sordid motive, as we sit in the wings watching the world's procession pass across the stage. If it is true that through our contacts we are sprinkled with a coating of the dry dust of cynicism, that dust was blown away in a breath, as it were, when our professional brethren who greeted you overseas broadcast the news of your peerless exploit. To Americans it brought a spontaneous feeling of pride that you were of their nationality.

===Chinese===
"我们新闻界与三教九流摩肩接踵。我们坐在侧幕，看人间的队列从舞台上次第走过，见过许多善，也见过许多自私自利与卑污的动机。如果说经年累月的接触真给我们撒了一身愤世的干尘，那么，当我们的同行在海外迎接您、拍发您那无与伦比的壮举的讯息时，那身尘土可谓一口气就被吹散了。它给美国人带来一种油然而生的自豪——您是他们中的一员。

===Original===
"The whole world was carried off its feet by an accomplishment so daring, so masterful in execution, so superb in achievement, by the picture presented of that onrushing chariot of dauntless youth, flashing across uncharted heavens straight through the storm's barrage.

===Chinese===
"如此大胆、执行得如此娴熟、成就得如此圆满的功业，加上那幅图景——无畏青春的战车隆隆向前，掠过未经测绘的天穹，径直穿透风暴的弹幕——整个世界都为之倾倒。

===Original===
"But if the press, with such an inspiration, performed its mission well, it found equal inspiration. It performed as fine a mission in chronicling the subsequent conduct of our young Ambassador of Good Will. His words and bearing dissipated vapors of misunderstanding. He personified, to a Europe amazed at the revelation, the real spirit of America.

===Chinese===
"不过，新闻界纵然受此感召而克尽厥职，它同样获得了灵感。在记录我们这位年轻亲善大使随后言行的过程中，它完成了一样出色的使命。他的言谈与举止驱散了误会的阴霾。他向一个因这一启示而惊叹的欧洲，具象地展现了美国的真正精神。

===Original===
"The press should be proud then, if in telling the story of this later phase in the career of the American boy, it brought to the peoples of the world a new realization that clean living, clean thinking, fair play and sportsmanship, modesty of speech and manner, faith in a mother's prayers, have a front page news value intriguing imagination and inviting emulation, and are still potent as fundamentals of success."

===Chinese===
"因此新闻界理应自豪：在讲述这位美国青年这段后续经历时，它让世界各国人民重新认识到——清白地生活、清朗地思想、公平竞争与运动家风度、言谈举止的谦逊、对一位母亲祈祷的信心——这些品质具有头版新闻的价值，足以激发想象、引人效法，并且依然是成功的根本要素。"

===Original===
Postmaster General New then stepped forward and gave Lindbergh the first special air mail stamp. As he handed it to the flier he said:

===Chinese===
接着，邮政总长纽走上前来，把第一枚特种航邮邮票赠给林德伯格。他把邮票递到这位飞行家手中时说道：

===Original===
"It is as a pilot in the service of the Air Mail that I greet you. There is no public service devoted to the peace time of the public whose past and present are attended by the romance that are attached to the history of the Post Office Department of the United States.

===Chinese===
"我谨以航邮服务一名驾驶员的身份向您致意。凡属服务公众太平年月的公共事业，没有哪一家的往昔与今朝，比得上美国邮政部的历史那样富于传奇色彩。

===Original===
"From the single couriers of the early days, who followed the uncertain trails through wood and fen on horseback and on foot, the picturesque riders of the pony express of a later day, who risked their lives at the hands of savage foes in the wilderness, the drivers who serve amid the rigors of the frozen North with dog teams and sleds, to those intrepid pilots who pierce the night with the air mail and of whom you are a worthy representative, the whole story is set in an atmosphere of most engaging romance.

===Chinese===
"从早年单人信使跋涉于林莽沼泽间的迢迢驿路，到后来驿马快信（pony express）那些风采独具的骑手在荒野中把性命交到凶悍敌人手里，再到严寒北国驾着狗队与雪橇服役的车夫，直至如今以航邮穿破夜幕的那些无畏飞行员——而您正是他们当之无愧的代表——整个故事都浸润在最动人的传奇气氛之中。

===Original===
"It has no titles to bestow—no medal it can add to those that have been given in recognition of your splendid achievement. There is one thing, however, it can do that will everywhere be regarded as most appropriate. It has issued a stamp designed for special use with the air mail which bears your name and a representation of the other member of that very limited partnership in which you made your now famous journey across seas. It is the first time a stamp has been issued in honor of a man still living—a distinction which you have worthily won.

===Chinese===
"它没有爵位可授——也拿不出一枚新的勋章，加于已为您的辉煌成就而颁授的诸枚之上。不过，有一件事它办得到，而且处处都会认为再合适不过：它发行了一枚航邮专用的特种邮票，票面印着您的大名，以及您那段名扬四海的旅程中那个成员极少的合伙事业里另一位合伙人的画像。为尚在人间的人发行纪念邮票，这是有史以来第一次——这一殊荣，您受之无愧。

===Original===
"It is my great pleasure to be privileged to present to you, and to the mother who gave you to this service, the first two copies of this issue as the best evidence of the enduring regard of the Post Office Department of the United States."

===Chinese===
"我深感荣幸，能将这一邮票的头两枚，敬赠给您，并赠予把您献给这项事业的母亲——这是美国邮政部持久敬意的最好证明。"

===Original===
These speeches are quoted because better than almost any other capturable entity of those days they reflect the wide scope of the effect Lindbergh's success had on both governmental and business routine. Surely it is difficult to conceive of a military victor shaking so many foundations, no matter what the might of his mailed fist.

===Chinese===
我们引录这些演讲，是因为比起那个时代几乎任何可以留存的实物，它们更能反映林德伯格的成功对政府公务与商业常规的影响之广。一位军事上的胜利者，纵有铁腕之力，恐怕也难以撼动如此多的根基。

===Original===
Secretary of State Kellogg next presented Lindbergh with a memorial volume consisting of a compilation of diplomatic exchanges between the State Department and the Foreign Offices of the world in connection with the flight. His words lined in a little more of the bewildering picture of the world's admiration enfolding before Lindbergh's frankly astonished gaze.

===Chinese===
随后，国务卿凯洛格（Kellogg）向林德伯格赠送了一部纪念册，辑录了国务院与世界各国外交部之间关于这次飞行的往来文电。他的话，又为那幅令林德伯格坦然露出惊异的、世人倾慕的画卷添上了一笔。

===Original===
"Colonel Charles A. Lindbergh," he slowly and ponderously began, staring hard at the object of his eulogy. "On May 20th and 21st, 1927, the world was electrified by the news of your nonstop flight from New York to Paris. It was a marvelous accomplishment requiring the highest courage, skill and self-reliance. Probably no act of a single individual in our day has ever aroused such universal enthusiasm and admiration. Your great deed is a milestone marking scientific advancement.

===Chinese===
"查尔斯·A·林德伯格上校，"他缓缓地、字斟句酌地开了口，目光紧盯着他颂扬的对象，"1927 年 5 月 20 日与 21 日，全世界为您从纽约到巴黎的不着陆飞行的消息而震动。这是一项了不起的成就，需要极高的勇气、技艺与自信。我们这个时代，恐怕还没有哪个个人的行为，激起过如此普遍的热情与赞美。您的伟大功绩，是标示科学进步的一座里程碑。

===Original===
"You have been congratulated by Kings and Presidents. You have listened to the plaudits of thousands and thousands in Europe and you know the tributes which have been justly paid to you by millions more. You do not now realize the thousands who have expressed their congratulations in letters and telegrams. I have had printed in this little volume only the official telegrams which passed through the Department of State and I take pleasure in presenting to you this volume in commemoration of your epochal achievement.

===Chinese===
"您已接受过国王们与总统们的道贺。您聆听过欧洲成千上万人的喝彩，也知道还有数百万人向您致以应得的敬意。您现在还体会不到，有多少人已用书信和电报表达了祝贺。这部小册子里，我只印下了经由国务院收发的官方电报。谨以此册奉赠，纪念您划时代的成就，深感荣幸。

===Original===
"Along the highway of human progress, as we look back over the last half century we marvel at the progress in science, the arts and invention. Truly this is a marvelous age and your daring feat will pass into the pages of history."

===Chinese===
"沿着人类进步的大道回望过去半个世纪，我们惊叹于科学、艺术与发明的进步。这确实是一个奇迹般的时代，而您的壮举将载入史册。"

===Original===
Then came Dr. Charles G. Abbott, Acting Secretary of the Smithsonian Institute who informed Lindbergh that the Institute had decided to award him the Langley "Medal of Pioneers." This honor has in the past been bestowed upon a small but distinguished group such as Orville Wright, Glenn H. Curtiss and Gustave Eiffel. Thus was added to the tribute of press and state the commendation of one of the oldest and finest scientific bodies in the world.

===Chinese===
接着是史密森学会（Smithsonian Institute）代理秘书长查尔斯·G·阿博特博士（Dr. Charles G. Abbott），他告知林德伯格，学会已决定授予他兰利"先驱者奖章"（Langley Medal of Pioneers）。这一荣誉过去只授予过一个人数不多却极显赫的群体，如奥维尔·莱特（Orville Wright）、格伦·H·柯蒂斯（Glenn H. Curtiss）和古斯塔夫·埃菲尔（Gustave Eiffel）。于是，在报界与国家的礼赞之上，又加上了全世界最古老、最优秀的科学机构之一的褒奖。

===Original===
Followed next a medley of messages from special organizations. Greetings from cities touched by Lindbergh in his historic flight from San Diego to Paris were read. St. Louis sent a moving reminder that her people were "waiting for you now impatiently … waiting since that gray morning when you launched out over the clouds and the sea for Paris."

===Chinese===
随后是各专门团体来函的大杂烩。林德伯格自圣迭戈至巴黎的历史性飞行所经各城，贺辞被一一宣读。圣路易斯发来一段动人的话，提醒他，她的人民"此刻正焦灼地等着您……自那个阴沉的早晨您穿云破海飞向巴黎时起，就一直在等着"。

===Original===
There was one from the British Government, something almost without precedent when it is considered that its recipient was a private citizen on a private enterprise. The official bearer read:

===Chinese===
还有一封来自英国政府——考虑到收信人不过是一位从事私人事业的平民，这几乎是史无前例的事。官方信使宣读道：

===Original===
"I have been desired by the British Government to express to Colonel Lindbergh on this occasion in behalf of all the people of Great Britain their warm congratulations on the safe return home after his historic flight across the Atlantic. The British people regard Colonel Lindbergh with special admiration and affection not only for his great courage and resource, but also for his equally great modesty in success and generosity in giving their due to other aviators who have gone before."

===Chinese===
"我受英国政府之托，谨在此代表全体大不列颠人民，向林德伯格上校致以热烈的祝贺，祝贺他在历史性的跨越大西洋飞行之后平安归来。英国人民对林德伯格上校怀有特别的钦佩与爱戴，不仅因为他非凡的勇气与才智，也因为他成功之后同样非凡的谦逊，以及他对先行者们的慷慨——他总是把他们应得的荣誉归于他们。"

===Original===
At the end of this bewildering array of orations and gifts the speaker of the evening was announced. One has only to put oneself in Lindbergh's place after reading some of the eloquence listed above to admire the moral courage it took to face that huge audience and once more speak with directness and precision of the things nearest his heart—things often furthest from the burden of the discourse:

===Chinese===
在这一连串令人目眩的演讲与赠礼的最后，当晚的主宾演讲人宣布登台。读过上面这些雄辩辞章的人，只要设身处地替林德伯格想一想，便会敬佩他直面那庞大听众、再次直截了当、条理分明地谈论他最关切之事所需的道义勇气——而那些事，往往离前面诸篇演说的宏旨最远：

===Original===
"I want to express my appreciation of the reception I've met in America and the welcome I have received here tonight." It was plain the flier was going to cover another field than the infinitely delicate one he had touched earlier in the day. "When I landed at Le Bourget a few weeks ago, I landed with the expectancy and hope of being able to see Europe. It was the first time I had ever been abroad. I had seen a number of interesting things when I flew over Ireland and Southern England and France. I had only been gone from America two days or a little less, and I wasn't in any particular hurry to get back.

===Chinese===
"我想表达我对在美国所受接待、以及今晚在此所受欢迎的谢意。"显然，这位飞行家要谈的是另一片天地，而非他白天触及的那片极为微妙的外交领域。"几个星期前我在勒布尔热降落时，心里怀着的是游历欧洲的期待与希望。那是我生平第一次出国。飞越爱尔兰、英格兰南部和法国时，我看到了许多有趣的东西。我离开美国不过两天或不到两天，并不急于回去。

===Original===
"But by the time I had been in France a week, Belgium a day and England two or three days—by that time I had opened several cables from America and talked with three Ambassadors and their attachés and found that it didn't make much difference whether I wanted to stay or not: and while I was informed that it was not necessarily an order to come back home, there was a battleship waiting for me.

===Chinese===
"可等我在法国待了一周、比利时一天、英格兰两三天之后——那时我已经拆阅了美国发来的好几封电报，同三位大使及其随员谈过话，于是明白我想不想留下已无关紧要：虽然有人告诉我，那不一定是一道回国的命令，可有一艘战列舰正等着我。

===Original===
"The Ambassador said this wasn't an order, but advice," the aviator added.

===Chinese===
"大使说这不是命令，是建议，"这位飞行家补充道。

===Original===
"So on June 4 I sailed on the *Memphis* from Cherbourg and this morning as I came up the Potomac I wasn't very sorry that I had listened to it.

===Chinese===
"于是 6 月 4 日，我乘'孟菲斯'号从瑟堡启航。今天早晨溯波托马克河而上时，我不后悔听了那建议。

===Original===
"There were several things I saw in Europe that are of interest to American aviation. All Europe looks on our air mail service with reverence. There is nothing like it anywhere abroad.

===Chinese===
"我在欧洲看到几件与美国航空息息相关的事。整个欧洲都敬重我们的航邮事业。国外任何地方都没有可与比肩者。

===Original===
"But, whereas we have airlines, they have passenger lines. All Europe is covered with a network of lines carrying passengers between all the big cities. Now it is up to us to create and develop passenger lines that compare with our mail routes. For this we have natural advantages in the great distances here that lend themselves to rapid transportation by air. Moreover, we can make these long trips without the inconvenience of passing over international boundaries.

===Chinese===
"不过，我们有航邮线，他们却有客运线。整个欧洲布满了在各大城市之间载客飞行的航线网。现在轮到我们创建并发展能与邮路媲美的客运航线了。在这方面我们有着天然优势：这里幅员辽阔，正适合航空快速运输。而且，我们作这些长途飞行，无须为跨越国界所扰。

===Original===
"The question comes up, 'Why has Europe got ahead of us in commercial airlines?' The reason is, of course, that the Governments over there give subsidies. I don't think we want any subsidies over here. Of course, if we had them they would create passenger lines overnight, so to speak, but in the long run the airlines, the distance they covered and the routes would be controlled entirely by the subsidies.

===Chinese===
"有人会问：'欧洲的商业航空为什么走在了我们前面？'原因当然在于那里的政府给补贴。我认为我们在这里不需要补贴。诚然，有了补贴，客运航线几乎一夜之间就能建立起来，但从长远看，航线、航程和路向，将完全受制于补贴。

===Original===
"What we need now more than any other one thing is a series of airports in every city and town throughout the United States. Given these airports, in a very few years the nations of Europe would be looking toward our passenger lines as they now look at our mail routes."

===Chinese===
"当前我们最需要的，莫过于在全美每个市镇建立起一系列机场。有了这些机场，不出几年，欧洲各国就会像如今仰望我们的邮路那样，仰望我们的客运航线。"

===Original===
Sunday was another full day. Under able guidance of the Chief Executive, Lindbergh did the things every good American would expect him to do. And, as one who has seen the lad at close range, we can say that he did them gladly and with profound appreciation for the privilege of doing them. After you come to know him you find out that's the kind he is.

===Chinese===
星期日又是排得满满的一天。在总统的妥善安排下，林德伯格做了每个善良的美国人都会期望他做的事。而作为近距离见过这个小伙子的人，我们可以说，他做这些事心甘情愿，并深深珍视做这些事的荣幸。认识他之后你就会明白，他就是这样的人。

===Original===
He went to church with President and Mrs. Coolidge. Accompanied by his mother he laid a wreath upon the tomb of the Unknown Soldier in the great memorial amphitheatre in Arlington Cemetery. He drove to Georgetown and visited the wounded soldiers at Walter Reed Hospital. He attended a celebration in honor of the 150th anniversary of the American flag, for which services were held on the steps of the Capitol and presided over by Charles Evans Hughes.

===Chinese===
他与柯立芝总统夫妇一同去了教堂。在母亲陪同下，他在阿灵顿公墓（Arlington Cemetery）宏伟的纪念圆形剧场里，向无名烈士墓敬献了花圈。他驱车前往乔治敦（Georgetown），看望了沃尔特·里德医院（Walter Reed Hospital）的伤兵。他还出席了美国国旗 150 周年庆典——仪式在国会大厦（Capitol）台阶上举行，由查尔斯·埃文斯·休斯主持。

===Original===
It was at this last ceremony that Lindbergh received the Cross of Honor. His response to the honor was brief and typically to the point. He declared that credit for his flight should "not go to the pilot alone but to American science and genius which had given years of study to the advancement of aeronautics."

===Chinese===
正是在最后这个仪式上，林德伯格接受了荣誉十字勋章（Cross of Honor）。他对这一荣誉的答谢简短扼要，一如其人。他宣称，此番飞行的功劳"不应归飞行员一人，而应归于为航空进步付出多年研究的美国科学与天才"。

===Original===
"Some things should be taken into consideration in connection with our flight that have not heretofore been given due weight. That is just what made this flight possible. It was not the act of a single pilot. It was the culmination of twenty years of aeronautical research and the assembling together of all that was practicable and best in American aviation. It represented American industry.

===Chinese===
"关于我们这次飞行，有些事情应当考虑进去，它们此前并未得到应有的重视。恰恰是这些，才使这次飞行成为可能。这不是一个飞行员的单独作为。它是二十年航空研究的结晶，是美国航空中一切切实可用、出类拔萃之物的汇聚。它代表着美国的工业。

===Original===
"In addition to this consideration should be given the scientific researches that have been in progress for countless centuries. All of this should have consideration in apportioning credit for the flight. Credit should go not alone to the pilot, but to the other factors that I have briefly enumerated. I thank you."

===Chinese===
"除此之外，还应当考虑那些已经进行了无数个世纪的科学研究。在分配这次飞行的功劳时，这一切都应计算在内。功劳不应只属于飞行员，也应属于我方才简要列举的其他各项因素。谢谢诸位。"

===Original===
This was the day well worthy of what Lindbergh had done and what he stood for. And again, by the spiritual values it comprised, it struck the inspirational note which had dominated almost everything the lad has done or said from the moment of his landing at Le Bourget to the moment of this writing.

===Chinese===
这一天，配得上林德伯格的作为，也配得上他所代表的一切。而且，凭它所蕴含的精神价值，这一天再次奏响了那激励人心的音符——从他在勒布尔热降落的时刻，直到本文落笔的时刻，这音符几乎主宰着这个小伙子的一言一行。

===Original===
Is it any wonder that the populace responded as it did?

===Chinese===
民众如此反响，还有什么可奇怪的吗？
